📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期登革病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 70% 的儿童入院时出现 重症登革热 休克,58.8% 的儿童出现多器官功能障碍。82.4% 的病例中发现了可改变的因素,包括延迟识别代偿性休克和液体管理问题。 [10.1007/s12098-026-06434-z]。
  • 患者登革热IgM检测呈阳性,伴有血小板减少(104,000/mm3)和肝酶升高。影像学检查确认了肝静脉血栓形成、下腔静脉狭窄和尾状叶增大,确立了诊断与布-奇亚里综合征的关联。血栓形成倾向筛查显示蛋白C、蛋白S和抗凝血酶III缺乏。患者还出现了严重的双下肢疼痛,神经传导研究证实了双下肢的感觉性多发性神经病。
  • ZIKV 感染产生持续的 NS1 反应,而原发性 DENV 感染表现出最小的 ZIKV 交叉反应性,继发性 DENV 感染仅表现出短暂的、低水平的增加。 [10.64898/2026.09.04.26362297]。
  • 在260名参与者中,78名(30.0%)检测出登革热血清学阳性,182名(70.0%)检测为阴性。发热是最常见的症状,有157名(60.38%)参与者报告了发热。大多数参与者未住院,229名(88.08%),248名(95.38%)存活,记录到12名(4.62%)死亡。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Seventy percent of children had severe dengue shock at admission, and 58.8% had multiorgan dysfunction.
  • The patient tested positive for dengue IgM with thrombocytopenia (104,000/mm3) and elevated liver enzymes.
  • ZIKV infections produced sustained NS1 responses, whereas primary DENV infections showed minimal ZIKV cross-reactivity and secondary DENV infections exhibited only transient, low-level increases.
  • Of the 260 participants, 78 (30.0%) tested positive for dengue serology and 182 (70.0%) tested negative. Fever was the most common symptom, reported by 157 (60.38%) participants.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: medRxiv  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-08  |  DOI: 10.64898/2026.09.04.26362297  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026

应用多重电化学发光 NS1 测定法检测和区分巴西和泰国 队列研究 长期社区中的寨卡病毒和 登革病毒 暴露情况

Applying a multiplex electrochemiluminescence NS1 assay to detect and differentiate Zika and dengue virus exposures in long-term community cohorts in Brazil and Thailand
作者： Nivison Nery, Marco Hamins-Puertolas, Lauren Bahr, Jaqueline S. Cruz, Joseph Lu, M. Catherine Muenker, Juan P. Aguilar Ticona, Ruchira Khosavanna, Mariam O. Fofana, Oliveira Dd
摘要中文翻译背景:由于抗原交叉反应,区分寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 和 登革病毒 (DENV) 感染仍然是共流行地区的主要诊断挑战。我们评估了多重电化学发光 Meso Scale Discovery System (MSD) NS1 检测,以在不同免疫背景下高度特异性地定义 ZIKV 感染。方法:我们分析了多个 队列研究 中经 PCR 确认的 ZIKV 和 DENV 感染的纵向样本,评估了 MSD NS1 信号强度和 PRNT50 滴度之间的相关性,并进行了 ROC 分析以得出血清转化阈值。然后,我们应用这些标准来确定巴西(2015-2024 年)和泰国(2015-2025 年)的 ZIKV 血清转化情况。研究结果:ZIKV 感染产生持续的 NS1 反应,而原发性 DENV 感染表现出最小的 ZIKV 交叉反应性,继发性 DENV 感染仅表现出短暂的、低水平的增加。MSD NS1 信号强度与 ZIKV 和所有四种 DENV 血清型的 PRNT50 滴度相关 (rho = 0.54-0.84)。ROC 分析确定,最佳 ZIKV 血清转换截止值至少为 10.96 倍数变化,未接触过 DENV 的参与者的 AUC 为 100%,暴露于 DENV 的参与者的 AUC 为 90.7%。添加第二个标准 - ZIKV/DENV 倍数变化比 > 1 - 进一步提高特异性。应用这种双阈值框架显示,2015-2016 年巴西疫情期间 ZIKV 发病率 较高,而疫情后时期 发病率 非常低。在泰国 队列研究 中,家庭成员之间的同步血清转化表明潜在的家庭内部 队列研究。解释:MSD NS1 多重检测提供了一种可扩展、定量和高度特异性的方法,用于识别 ZIKV 感染并估计共流行环境中的血清发生率。双阈值框架(倍数变化至少 10.96 且 ZIKV/DENV 比率 > 1)提高了诊断精度,为黄病毒流行地区的 监测 和 传播 研究提供了强大的工具。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于抗原交叉反应,区分寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 和 登革病毒 (DENV) 感染仍然是共流行地区的主要诊断挑战。
设计与对象
我们分析了多个 队列研究 中经 PCR 确认的 ZIKV 和 DENV 感染的纵向样本,评估了 MSD NS1 信号强度和 PRNT50 滴度之间的相关性,并进行了 ROC 分析以得出血清转化阈值。
核心方法
然后,我们应用这些标准来确定巴西(2015-2024 年)和泰国(2015-2025 年)的 ZIKV 血清转化情况。
主要结果
ZIKV 感染产生持续的 NS1 反应,而原发性 DENV 感染表现出最小的 ZIKV 交叉反应性,继发性 DENV 感染仅表现出短暂的、低水平的增加。
解释与创新
MSD NS1 多重检测提供了一种可扩展、定量和高度特异性的方法,用于识别 ZIKV 感染并估计共流行环境中的血清发生率。
科研与公卫意义
双阈值框架(倍数变化至少 10.96 且 ZIKV/DENV 比率 > 1)提高了诊断精度,为黄病毒流行地区的 监测 和 传播 研究提供了强大的工具。
局限与证据强度
ROC 分析确定,最佳 ZIKV 血清转换截止值至少为 10.96 倍数变化,未接触过 DENV 的参与者的 AUC 为 100%,暴露于 DENV 的参与者的 AUC 为 90.7%。

Applying a multiplex electrochemiluminescence NS1 assay to detect and differentiate Zika and dengue virus exposures in long-term community cohorts in Brazil and Thailand

Authors: Nivison Nery, Marco Hamins-Puertolas, Lauren Bahr, Jaqueline S. Cruz, Joseph Lu, M. Catherine Muenker, Juan P. Aguilar Ticona, Ruchira Khosavanna, Mariam O. Fofana, Oliveira Dd
Original abstract (English)
Background: Distinguishing Zika virus (ZIKV) from dengue virus (DENV) infection remains a major diagnostic challenge in co-endemic regions due to antigenic cross-reactivity. We evaluated a multiplex electrochemiluminescence Meso Scale Discovery System (MSD) NS1 assay to define ZIKV infection with high specificity across diverse immune backgrounds. Methods: We analyzed longitudinal samples from PCR-confirmed ZIKV and DENV infections across multiple cohorts, assessed correlations between MSD NS1 signal intensities and PRNT50 titres, and performed ROC analyses to derive seroconversion thresholds. We then applied these criteria to identify ZIKV seroconversions in Brazil (2015-2024) and Thailand (2015-2025). Findings: ZIKV infections produced sustained NS1 responses, whereas primary DENV infections showed minimal ZIKV cross-reactivity and secondary DENV infections exhibited only transient, low-level increases. MSD NS1 signal intensities correlated with PRNT50 titres for ZIKV and all four DENV serotypes (rho = 0.54-0.84). ROC analyses identified a fold-change of at least 10.96 as the optimal ZIKV seroconversion cutoff, with an AUC of 100% among DENV-naive and 90.7% among DENV-exposed participants. Adding a second criterion - ZIKV/DENV fold-change ratio > 1 - further increased specificity. Application of this dual-threshold framework revealed high ZIKV incidence during the 2015-2016 epidemic in Brazil and very low incidence in the post-epidemic period. In the Thailand cohort, synchronous seroconversion among household members suggested potential intra-household transmission. Interpretation: The MSD NS1 multiplex assay provides a scalable, quantitative, and highly specific approach for identifying ZIKV infection and estimating seroincidence in co-endemic settings. The dual-threshold framework (fold-change of at least 10.96 and ZIKV/DENV ratio > 1) enhances diagnostic precision, offering a robust tool for surveillance and transmission studies in flavivirus-endemic regions.

Distinguishing Zika virus (ZIKV) from dengue virus (DENV) infection remains a major diagnostic challenge in co-endemic regions due to antigenic cross-reactivity.

超越出血:一例急性登革病毒感染伴发布-奇亚里综合征和双侧感觉性多发性神经病的病例

Beyond Hemorrhage: A Case of Acute Dengue Infection Presenting with Budd-Chiari Syndrome and Bilateral Sensory Polyneuropathy.
作者： Krushna Keshav Dalvi, Priya Khaire, Mohammed Hassan Shaik
摘要中文翻译摘要 布加综合征 (BCS) 是一种罕见的肝血管疾病,其特征是肝静脉流出道阻塞,每年每百万人口中有 1-2 例出现 发病率。虽然 登革热 主要与出血并发症相关,但血栓事件却很少报道。据我们所知,这是第一个有记录的与 登革热 相关的 BCS 病例。一名22岁农村男性因腹痛、发热、呕吐、稀便病史4天就诊。体检发现肝肿大、腹水和轻度脱水。实验室检查证实登革热 IgM 阳性,伴有血小板减少 (104,000/mm 3 ) 和肝酶升高。血栓形成倾向筛查显示蛋白 C、蛋白 S 和抗凝血酶 III 缺乏。影像学研究,包括超声检查、对比计算机断层扫描和彩色多普勒,显示肝静脉血栓形成、下腔静脉狭窄和尾状叶肥大,建立了 BCS 的 诊断。上消化道内镜检查显示II级食管静脉曲张,符合门静脉高压症。入院期间,患者还出现双下肢严重疼痛,强效镇痛药无效,神经传导检查证实双下肢存在感觉性多发性神经病。该患者接受了支持性护理和使用低分子肝素 (LMWH) 的仔细抗凝治疗,随后每天服用 4 mg 华法林。尽管最初出现血小板减少,但通过密切监测出血并发症,成功开始抗凝治疗。该患者表现出良好的临床反应,随访时肝静脉血栓完全消退,肝功能正常化,腹水消退。在 6 个月的随访中,停止华法林并桥接 LMWH 后进行的重复血栓形成倾向检测显示蛋白 C、蛋白 S 和抗凝血酶 III 水平正常,表明不存在真正的遗传缺陷。该病例强调了 登革热、BCS 与患有明显但短暂的血栓形成倾向的患者的感觉多发性神经病之间的新关联。尽管同时存在血小板减少症,但早期识别、适当的抗凝和仔细的神经学评估仍可带来良好的结果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在记录一种罕见且新颖的关联,即登革热与布-奇亚里综合征(BCS)及双侧感觉性多发性神经病之间的联系,强调识别登革病毒感染非典型表现的重要性。
设计与对象
对一名22岁男性患者进行单病例研究,该患者来自农村地区,表现为急性登革病毒感染,伴有腹痛、发热、呕吐和腹泻,同时出现布-奇亚里综合征和感觉性多发性神经病的体征。
核心方法
通过实验室检查,包括登革热IgM检测、血小板减少评估和肝酶升高,确认了患者的诊断。利用超声检查、增强型计算机断层扫描和彩色多普勒成像评估肝静脉流出道阻塞。进行了血栓形成倾向筛查以评估凝血因子。
主要结果
患者登革热IgM检测呈阳性,伴有血小板减少(104,000/mm3)和肝酶升高。影像学检查确认了肝静脉血栓形成、下腔静脉狭窄和尾状叶增大,确立了诊断与布-奇亚里综合征的关联。血栓形成倾向筛查显示蛋白C、蛋白S和抗凝血酶III缺乏。患者还出现了严重的双下肢疼痛,神经传导研究证实了双下肢的感觉性多发性神经病。
解释与创新
作者将此病例解释为首次记录的布-奇亚里综合征与登革热相关联的病例,强调了短暂性血栓形成倾向以及布-奇亚里综合征与双侧感觉性多发性神经病的不寻常组合。
科研与公卫意义
该病例为登革病毒感染的非典型临床表现提供了重要见解,特别是与布-奇亚里综合征和感觉性多发性神经病的关联,这可能有助于更广泛的监测和登革热相关并发症的诊断方法。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单个 病例报告,可能无法推广。然而,证据是强有力的,因为它包括详细的临床、实验室和影像学结果,支持 BCS 的 诊断 和 登革热,并具有明确的时间关系。

Beyond Hemorrhage: A Case of Acute Dengue Infection Presenting with Budd-Chiari Syndrome and Bilateral Sensory Polyneuropathy.

Authors: Krushna Keshav Dalvi, Priya Khaire, Mohammed Hassan Shaik
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Budd–Chiari syndrome (BCS) is a rare hepatic vascular disorder characterized by hepatic venous outflow obstruction with an incidence of 1–2 per million population annually. While dengue fever is primarily associated with hemorrhagic complications, thrombotic events are rarely reported. To our knowledge, this represents the first documented case of BCS occurring in association with dengue fever. A 22-year-old male from a rural area presented with a 4-day history of abdominal pain, fever, vomiting, and loose stools. Physical examination revealed hepatomegaly, ascites, and mild dehydration. Laboratory investigations confirmed dengue IgM positivity with thrombocytopenia (104,000/mm 3 ) and elevated liver enzymes. Thrombophilia screening revealed deficiencies in protein C, protein S, and antithrombin III. Imaging studies, including ultrasonography, computed tomography with contrast, and color Doppler, demonstrated hepatic vein thrombosis, inferior vena cava narrowing, and caudate lobe hypertrophy, establishing the diagnosis of BCS. Upper gastrointestinal endoscopy revealed grade II esophageal varices consistent with portal hypertension. During admission, the patient also developed severe bilateral lower limb pain refractory to strong analgesics, and nerve conduction studies confirmed sensory polyneuropathy in both lower limbs. The patient was managed with supportive care and careful anticoagulation therapy using low-molecular-weight heparin (LMWH), followed by warfarin 4 mg daily. Despite initial thrombocytopenia, anticoagulation was successfully initiated with close monitoring for bleeding complications. The patient showed an excellent clinical response, with complete resolution of hepatic vein thrombosis, normalization of liver function, and resolution of ascites at follow-up. At 6-month follow-up, repeat thrombophilia assays performed after stopping warfarin and bridging with LMWH revealed normal levels of protein C, protein S, and antithrombin III, indicating no true inherited deficiency. This case highlights a novel association between dengue fever, BCS, and sensory polyneuropathy in a patient with apparent but transient thrombophilia. Early recognition, appropriate anticoagulation, and careful neurological evaluation can lead to favorable outcomes despite concurrent thrombocytopenia.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to document a rare and novel association between dengue fever and Budd-Chiari syndrome (BCS) with bilateral sensory polyneuropathy, highlighting the need to recognize atypical presentations of dengue infection.
Design and population
A single-case study was conducted on a 22-year-old male from a rural area who presented with acute dengue infection, characterized by abdominal pain, fever, vomiting, and loose stools, along with signs of BCS and sensory polyneuropathy.
Core methods
The patient's diagnosis was confirmed through laboratory investigations, including dengue IgM testing, thrombocytopenia assessment, and elevated liver enzymes. Imaging studies such as ultrasonography, computed tomography with contrast, and color Doppler were used to evaluate hepatic venous outflow obstruction. Thrombophilia screening was performed to assess coagulation factors.
Main results
The patient tested positive for dengue IgM with thrombocytopenia (104,000/mm3) and elevated liver enzymes. Imaging studies confirmed hepatic vein thrombosis, inferior vena cava narrowing, and caudate lobe hypertrophy, establishing the diagnosis of BCS. Thrombophilia screening revealed deficiencies in protein C, protein S, and antithrombin III. The patient also developed severe bilateral lower limb pain and nerve conduction studies confirmed sensory polyneuropathy in both lower limbs.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret this case as the first documented instance of BCS occurring in association with dengue fever, emphasizing the transient thrombophilia and the unusual combination of BCS and bilateral sensory polyneuropathy.
Scientific and public-health significance
This case provides important insights into the atypical clinical manifestations of dengue infection, particularly the association with BCS and sensory polyneuropathy, which may inform broader surveillance and diagnostic approaches for dengue-related complications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single case report, which may not be generalizable. However, the evidence is strong as it includes detailed clinical, laboratory, and imaging findings that support the diagnosis of BCS and dengue fever, with a clear temporal relationship.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

临床特征、相关因素、治疗行为及Las’anod,Sool地区索马里成年急性发热病例中登革热的结局:一项基于医院的横断面研究

Clinical Characteristics, Associated Factors, Treatment Practices, and Outcomes of Dengue Fever among Adult Acute Febrile Illness Cases in Las’anod, Sool Region, Somalia: A Hospital-Based Cross-Sectional Study
作者： Mohamed ja, Ahmed Farah Darod2*, Fufa Miresa Benti, Jama Abdiaziz Mohamed
摘要中文翻译背景:登革热是索马里新兴的公共卫生问题,但关于其在成年急性发热病例中的临床表现、血清学检测模式、相关因素、治疗行为及结局的证据仍有限。本研究评估了Las’anod,Sool地区索马里成年急性发热病例中的登革热情况。方法:在Las’anod综合医院、Manhal医院和索马里高级医院中,对260名被评估为登革热的成年急性发热患者进行了基于医院的横断面研究。数据通过标准化的数据提取清单从患者记录、临床评估表和实验室记录中收集。通过血清学检测结果确定登革热阳性。分析使用了描述性统计、卡方检验、二元逻辑回归和多变量逻辑回归。报告了调整后的比值比及其95%置信区间,统计学显著性设定为p < 0.05。结果:在260名参与者中,78名(30.0%)检测出登革热血清学阳性,182名(70.0%)检测为阴性。阳性病例中,61名(23.5%)仅IgM阳性,17名(6.5%)同时IgM和NS1阳性。发热是最常见的症状,有157名(60.38%)参与者报告了发热。大多数参与者未住院,229名(88.08%),248名(95.38%)存活,记录到12名(4.62%)死亡。在多变量分析中,年龄25–35岁、家庭月收入、既往登革热诊断、知识水平和室内储水显著与登革热阳性相关。结论:登革热是Las’anod地区成年急性发热病例的重要病因。加强登革热诊断、社区意识、安全储水行为、媒介控制及标准化临床管理可能有助于提高在资源有限环境中的检测、预防和治疗效果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热是索马里新兴的公共卫生问题,但关于其在成年急性发热病例中的临床表现、血清学检测模式、相关因素、治疗行为及结局的证据仍有限。本研究评估了Las’anod,Sool地区索马里成年急性发热病例中的登革热情况。
设计与对象
在Las’anod综合医院、Manhal医院和索马里高级医院中,对260名被评估为登革热的成年急性发热患者进行了基于医院的横断面研究。
核心方法
数据通过标准化的数据提取清单从患者记录、临床评估表和实验室记录中收集。分析使用了描述性统计、卡方检验、二元逻辑回归和多变量逻辑回归。通过血清学检测结果确定登革热阳性。
主要结果
在260名参与者中,78名(30.0%)检测出登革热血清学阳性,182名(70.0%)检测为阴性。发热是最常见的症状,有157名(60.38%)参与者报告了发热。大多数参与者未住院,229名(88.08%),248名(95.38%)存活,记录到12名(4.62%)死亡。
解释与创新
在多变量分析中,年龄25–35岁、家庭月收入、既往登革热诊断、知识水平和室内储水显著与登革热阳性相关。
科研与公卫意义
加强登革热诊断、社区意识、安全储水行为、媒介控制及标准化临床管理可能有助于提高在资源有限环境中的检测、预防和治疗效果。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

Clinical Characteristics, Associated Factors, Treatment Practices, and Outcomes of Dengue Fever among Adult Acute Febrile Illness Cases in Las’anod, Sool Region, Somalia: A Hospital-Based Cross-Sectional Study

Authors: Mohamed ja, Ahmed Farah Darod2*, Fufa Miresa Benti, Jama Abdiaziz Mohamed
Original abstract (English)
Background: Dengue fever is an emerging public health concern in Somalia, but evidence on its clinical presentation, serological test patterns, associated factors, treatment practices, and outcomes among adult acute febrile illness cases remains limited. This study assessed dengue fever among adult acute febrile illness cases in Las’anod, Sool Region, Somalia. Methods A hospital-based cross-sectional study was conducted among 260 adult patients with acute febrile illness assessed for dengue fever at Las’anod General Hospital, Manhal Hospital, and Somali Higher Hospital. Data were collected using a standardized data extraction checklist from patient records, clinical evaluation forms, and laboratory records. Dengue positivity was determined using serology test results. Descriptive statistics, Chi-square tests, binary logistic regression, and multivariable logistic regression were used for analysis. Adjusted odds ratios with 95% confidence intervals were reported, and statistical significance was set at p < 0.05. Results Of the 260 participants, 78 (30.0%) tested positive for dengue serology and 182 (70.0%) tested negative. Among positive cases, 61 (23.5%) were IgM positive only, and 17 (6.5%) were positive for both IgM and NS1. Fever was the most common symptom, reported by 157 (60.38%) participants. Most participants were not admitted to hospital, 229 (88.08%), and 248 (95.38%) survived, while 12 (4.62%) deaths were recorded. In multivariable analysis, age 25–35 years, monthly household income, previous dengue diagnosis, knowledge level, and indoor water storage were significantly associated with dengue positivity. Conclusions Dengue fever was a substantial contributor to acute febrile illness among adults in Las’anod. Strengthening dengue diagnosis, community awareness, safe water storage practices, vector control, and standardized clinical management may improve detection, prevention, and outcomes in resource-limited settings.

Descriptive statistics, Chi-square tests, binary logistic regression, and multivariable logistic regression were used for analysis. Dengue positivity was determined using serology test results.
Main results
Of the 260 participants, 78 (30.0%) tested positive for dengue serology and 182 (70.0%) tested negative. Fever was the most common symptom, reported by 157 (60.38%) participants. Most participants were not admitted to hospital, 229 (88.08%), and 248 (95.38%) survived, while 12 (4.62%) deaths were recorded.

一种肠道共生菌施氏假单胞菌通过产色菌素诱导的自噬抑制登革病毒感染

A gut commensal Serratia marcescens inhibits dengue virus infection via prodigiosin-induced autophagy in Aedes albopictus .
作者： Yijia Huang, Hongbo Li, Jinying Ding, Yihan Zhu, Nan Wang, Jiawei Zhang, Haojie Wang, Xiao Feng, Haodong Xu, Wenquan Liu
摘要中文翻译蚊虫肠道微生物群在调节虫媒病毒传播中起着关键作用,但其原生共生菌产生的特定抗病毒药物代谢产物及其潜在机制仍不清楚。本研究从中国温州地区捕获的Aedes albopictus中分离出一种天然肠道共生菌Serratia marcescens WZ1菌株,并证明其对登革病毒(登革病毒DENV)感染具有显著的抑制作用。通过整合代谢组学分析,我们鉴定了该菌株分泌的一种红色色素产色菌素(PG)作为功能性抗病毒药物代谢产物。PG在剂量和时间依赖性实验中显著抑制了蚊虫细胞中的DENV感染,并显著降低了成年Ae. albopictus中的DENV2 RNA水平。机制研究显示,PG优先定位于内质网(ER),诱导ER应激并上调伴侣蛋白GRP78的表达。这一过程随后激活了完整的自噬通量,表现为Atg8-I向Atg8-II的转化增强、自噬体形成增加以及溶酶体活性升高。重要的是,我们进一步证明PG促进了自噬体与溶酶体的融合,导致登革病毒与溶酶体区室共定位并最终被清除。通过基因敲低自噬基因Atg8和药理学抑制ER应激,显著减弱了PG介导的病毒抑制作用,证实了PG诱导的ER应激、自噬激活与病毒清除之间的功能联系。我们的研究阐明了一种原生蚊虫肠道共生菌代谢产物通过激活宿主 ER应激-自噬通路抑制虫媒病毒感染的新机制,为未来传播阻断策略提供了机制基础和候选靶点。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨蚊虫肠道微生物群在调节虫媒病毒传播中的作用,重点在于鉴定特定的抗病毒药物代谢产物及其对抗登革病毒的机制。
设计与对象
本研究从中国温州地区捕获的Aedes albopictus中分离出Serratia marcescens WZ1菌株,并展示了其抑制登革病毒感染的能力。研究使用了蚊虫细胞和成年Ae. albopictus进行。
核心方法
研究人员通过整合代谢组学分析鉴定了Serratia marcescens WZ1菌株分泌的抗病毒药物代谢产物产色菌素(PG)。他们通过剂量和时间依赖性实验评估了PG对登革病毒感染的影响,并进行了机制研究以确定其对ER应激、自噬激活和病毒清除的作用。
主要结果
PG在剂量和时间依赖性实验中显著抑制了蚊虫细胞中的登革病毒感染,并显著降低了成年Ae. albopictus中的DENV2 RNA水平。机制研究显示,PG诱导ER应激并上调GRP78,从而引发自噬通量。PG还促进了自噬体与溶酶体的融合,导致病毒被清除。
解释与创新
这些发现揭示了一种原生蚊虫肠道共生菌代谢产物通过激活宿主 ER应激-自噬通路抑制虫媒病毒感染的新机制,为未来传播阻断策略提供了机制基础。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为利用肠道微生物群衍生代谢产物开发新的阻断虫媒病毒登革病毒和传播的策略提供了见解,可能有助于未来关于监测、诊断以及干预措施的研究。
局限与证据强度
本研究的证据较强,因为它包括机制研究和剂量依赖性结果,但其研究局限于蚊虫细胞和成年Ae. albopictus的体外和体内实验,可能无法完全反映自然传播动态。

A gut commensal Serratia marcescens inhibits dengue virus infection via prodigiosin-induced autophagy in Aedes albopictus .

Authors: Yijia Huang, Hongbo Li, Jinying Ding, Yihan Zhu, Nan Wang, Jiawei Zhang, Haojie Wang, Xiao Feng, Haodong Xu, Wenquan Liu
Original abstract (English)
The mosquito gut microbiota plays a pivotal role in regulating arbovirus transmission, yet the specific antiviral metabolites produced by native symbiotic bacteria and their underlying mechanisms remain poorly understood. In this study, we isolated a natural gut symbiotic bacterium, Serratia marcescens strain WZ1, from field-caught Aedes albopictus in Wenzhou, China, and demonstrated its potent ability to inhibit dengue virus (DENV) infection. Through integrated metabolomic analysis, we identified the red pigment prodigiosin (PG) as a functional antiviral metabolite secreted by this strain. PG treatment suppressed DENV infection in mosquito cells in a dose- and time-dependent manner and significantly reduced DENV2 RNA levels in adult Ae. Mechanistic investigations revealed that PG preferentially localizes to the endoplasmic reticulum (ER), where it induces ER stress and upregulates the chaperone protein GRP78. This process subsequently activates a complete autophagic flux, as evidenced by enhanced conversion of Atg8-I to Atg8-II, increased autophagosome formation, and elevated lysosomal activity. Crucially, we further demonstrated that PG facilitates the convergence of autophagosomes and lysosomes, culminating in the colocalization of DENV with lysosomal compartments and subsequent viral clearance. Both genetic knockdown of the autophagy gene Atg8 and pharmacological inhibition of ER stress substantially attenuated PG-mediated viral suppression, confirming the functional link between PG-induced ER stress, autophagy activation, and viral clearance. Our findings elucidate a novel mechanism by which a native mosquito gut symbiont metabolite restricts arboviral infection through activation of the host ER stress-autophagy pathway, providing a mechanistic basis and candidate leads for future transmission-blocking studies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the role of mosquito gut microbiota in regulating arbovirus transmission, with a focus on identifying specific antiviral metabolites and their mechanisms of action against dengue virus.
Design and population
The study involved isolating Serratia marcescens strain WZ1 from field-caught Aedes albopictus in Wenzhou, China, and demonstrating its ability to inhibit dengue virus infection. The research was conducted using mosquito cells and adult Ae.
Core methods
The researchers performed integrated metabolomic analysis to identify the antiviral metabolite prodigiosin (PG) secreted by Serratia marcescens strain WZ1. They evaluated PG's effects on dengue virus infection through dose- and time-dependent assays, and mechanistic investigations were conducted to determine its impact on ER stress, autophagy activation, and viral clearance.
Main results
PG treatment suppressed dengue virus infection in mosquito cells in a dose- and time-dependent manner and significantly reduced DENV2 RNA levels in adult Ae. Mechanistic investigations revealed that PG induces ER stress and upregulates GRP78, leading to autophagic flux. PG also facilitates autophagosome-lysosome convergence, resulting in viral clearance.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal a novel mechanism by which a native mosquito gut symbiont metabolite restricts arboviral infection through activation of the host ER stress-autophagy pathway, offering a mechanistic basis for future transmission-blocking strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the potential of gut microbiota-derived metabolites for developing new strategies to block dengue virus transmission, which could inform future research on surveillance, diagnosis, and intervention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is strong, as it includes mechanistic investigations and dose-dependent results, but it is limited to in vitro and in vivo experiments with mosquito cells and adult Ae. albopictus, which may not fully represent natural transmission dynamics.

DrugBank小分子药物针对登革病毒衣壳蛋白的再利用:一项计算机模拟研究。

Repurposing of DrugBank small molecules against Dengue virus capsid protein: an in silico study.
作者： Dwaipayan Chaudhuri, Kalyan Giri
摘要中文翻译未标注:登革病毒(登革病毒)仍然是一个紧迫的全球健康威胁,目前尚无批准的抗病毒药物治疗方法。本研究探讨了登革病毒衣壳蛋白二聚体界面处的保守疏水口袋作为新型抗病毒药物干预靶点。我们使用高分辨率衣壳结构(包含建模的N端无序区域)对DrugBank数据库进行了虚拟筛选,以识别潜在的可再利用抑制剂。根据对接评分和结合位点特异性,筛选出六个最佳结合候选分子。这些分子随后接受了经典分子动力学(MD)模拟以评估复合物的稳定性,并通过热滴定分子动力学(TTMD)评估其在热应激下的相互作用稳定性。三种分子(DB02152、DB08683和DB13014)在MD和TTMD分析中表现出高度稳定的结合,其中DB13014(Hypericin)显示出最高的热稳定性和有利的相互作用能量。计算机模拟的ADMET分析进一步验证了这些化合物的药物特性,表明其口服生物利用度可接受、毒性风险低且易于合成。这些发现突显了登革病毒衣壳蛋白疏水口袋作为可行的药物靶点,并提出了小分子药物再利用作为抗登革热治疗的可能性。补充信息:在线版本包含可在https://doi.org/10.1007/s40203-026-00738-w获取的补充材料。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒(登革病毒)仍然是一个紧迫的全球健康威胁,目前尚无批准的抗病毒药物治疗方法。本研究旨在识别针对登革病毒衣壳蛋白的潜在可再利用抑制剂。
设计与对象
本研究是一项计算机模拟调查,探讨登革病毒衣壳蛋白二聚体界面处的保守疏水口袋。该研究使用包含建模的N端无序区域的高分辨率衣壳结构对DrugBank数据库进行虚拟筛选。
核心方法
使用包含建模的N端无序区域的高分辨率衣壳结构,我们通过AutoDock Vina对DrugBank数据库进行了虚拟筛选,以识别潜在的可再利用抑制剂。根据对接评分和结合位点特异性,筛选出六个最佳结合候选分子。这些分子随后接受了经典分子动力学(MD)模拟以评估复合物的稳定性,并通过热滴定分子动力学(TTMD)评估其在热应激下的相互作用稳定性。
主要结果
三种分子(DB02152、DB08683和DB13014)在MD和TTMD分析中表现出高度稳定的结合,其中DB13014(Hypericin)显示出最高的热稳定性和有利的相互作用能量。计算机模拟的ADMET分析进一步验证了这些化合物的药物特性,表明其口服生物利用度可接受、毒性风险低且易于合成。
解释与创新
这些发现突显了登革病毒衣壳蛋白疏水口袋作为可行的药物靶点,并提出了小分子药物再利用作为抗登革热治疗的可能性。本研究引入了一种新颖的方法,通过关注衣壳蛋白的结构特征来识别潜在的抑制剂。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为这些化合物作为抗登革热治疗的进一步实验验证提供了基础,为药物开发提供了潜在的先导化合物。这可能为未来关于现有药物再利用用于登革热治疗的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于计算机模拟方法,可能无法完全反映体内条件。由于研究结果基于计算模型,证据强度中等,需要实验验证。

Repurposing of DrugBank small molecules against Dengue virus capsid protein: an in silico study.

Authors: Dwaipayan Chaudhuri, Kalyan Giri
Original abstract (English)
UNLABELLED: Dengue virus (DENV) remains a pressing global health threat with no approved antiviral therapy to date. In this study, we explore the conserved hydrophobic pocket at the dimer interface of the dengue capsid protein as a novel target for antiviral intervention. Using a high-resolution capsid structure with modeled N-terminal disordered regions, we performed virtual screening of the entire DrugBank database employing AutoDock Vina to identify potential repurposable inhibitors. Six top-binding candidates were shortlisted based on docking scores and binding site specificity. These molecules were subjected to classical molecular dynamics (MD) simulations to evaluate complex stability, followed by thermal titration MD (TTMD) to assess interaction robustness under thermal stress. Three molecules-DB02152, DB08683, and DB13014-exhibited highly stable binding throughout both MD and TTMD analyses, with DB13014 (Hypericin) showing the highest thermal stability and favorable interaction energy. In silico ADMET profiling further validated the drug-like properties of these compounds, indicating acceptable oral bioavailability, low toxicity risk, and synthetic tractability. These findings highlight the dengue capsid hydrophobic pocket as a viable drug target and propose repurposing of small molecules as anti-dengue therapeutics. SUPPLEMENTARY INFORMATION: The online version contains supplementary material available at https://doi.org/10.1007/s40203-026-00738-w.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus (DENV) remains a pressing global health threat with no approved antiviral therapy to date. This study aims to identify potential repurposable inhibitors targeting the dengue capsid protein.
Design and population
The study is an in silico investigation exploring the conserved hydrophobic pocket at the dimer interface of the dengue capsid protein. It involves virtual screening of the DrugBank database using a high-resolution capsid structure with modeled N-terminal disordered regions.
Core methods
Using a high-resolution capsid structure with modeled N-terminal disordered regions, we performed virtual screening of the entire DrugBank database employing AutoDock Vina to identify potential repurposable inhibitors. Six top-binding candidates were shortlisted based on docking scores and binding site specificity. These molecules were subjected to classical molecular dynamics (MD) simulations to evaluate complex stability, followed by thermal titration MD (TTMD) to assess interaction robustness under thermal stress.
Main results
Three molecules-DB02152, DB08683, and DB13014-exhibited highly stable binding throughout both MD and TTMD analyses, with DB13014 (Hypericin) showing the highest thermal stability and favorable interaction energy. In silico ADMET profiling further validated the drug-like properties of these compounds, indicating acceptable oral bioavailability, low toxicity risk, and synthetic tractability.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings highlight the dengue capsid hydrophobic pocket as a viable drug target and propose repurposing of small molecules as anti-dengue therapeutics. The study introduces a novel approach to identify potential inhibitors by focusing on the capsid protein's structural features.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide a foundation for further experimental validation of these compounds as anti-dengue therapeutics, offering potential leads for drug development. This could inform future studies on repurposing existing drugs for dengue treatment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in silico methods, which may not fully reflect in vivo conditions. The evidence strength is moderate, as the findings are based on computational models and require experimental validation.

资源匮乏地区急性登革热相关心肌炎病例系列:来自科特迪瓦的案例

Acute Dengue-associated Myocarditis in a Resource-limited Setting: A Case Series From Côte D'ivoire
作者： Ines Angoran Regnier, Adama Doumbia, Corinne Akpovo, Roland N’guetta, Christophe Konin, Serge Paul Eholie, Serge Eholié
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革热相关综合征可进展为严重的器官功能障碍。在其中不典型的并发症中,急性心肌炎罕见、诊断不足且可能致命。我们报告了四例年轻男性患者在阿比让的特里奇维尔大学医院(科特迪瓦)入院,展示了与登革热相关的心肌炎多样临床表现。临床表现从急性心力衰竭到需要临时经静脉起搏的高危房室传导阻滞不等,这些情况发生在登革热病程的不同阶段。尽管高敏肌钙蛋白升高是一致的预警信号,但3特斯拉心脏磁共振成像(CMR)通过局部水肿和心外膜下晚期钆增强明确确认了心肌炎症。该病例系列强调了在登革热 暴发 中对心肌炎保持高度临床怀疑的重要性,因为其管理需要在补液和心肌负担之间进行谨慎平衡。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决与登革热相关且可能致命的急性心肌炎这一未被充分诊断的并发症。研究强调了在登革热 暴发 期间识别这一罕见状况的重要性。
设计与对象
本研究是一项病例系列研究,报告了四例年轻男性患者在阿比让的特里奇维尔大学医院入院,他们在登革热病程的不同阶段表现出急性心肌炎。该人群包括因心肌炎作为并发症而患病的登革热感染者。
核心方法
本研究包括对患者的临床评估,使用高敏肌钙蛋白检测和3特斯拉心脏磁共振成像(CMR)来通过局部水肿和心外膜下晚期钆增强确认心肌炎症。这些方法用于评估临床表现并确认登革热相关心肌炎的 诊断。
主要结果
患者表现出一系列症状,从急性心力衰竭到需要临时经静脉起搏的高危房室传导阻滞。高敏肌钙蛋白水平在所有病例中均一致升高,3特斯拉CMR成像在所有病例中均明确确认了心肌炎症。
解释与创新
作者认为,这些病例强调了在登革热 暴发 期间对心肌炎保持高度临床怀疑的必要性,因为其管理需要在补液和心肌负担之间进行谨慎平衡。该病例系列提供了关于登革热相关心肌炎多样化临床表现的新见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为登革热相关心肌炎的诊断和治疗实践提供改进的依据,特别是在资源匮乏的环境中,并强调及时和适当的管理策略对于预防严重后果的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受限于样本量小和缺乏对照组,这可能影响研究结果的普遍适用性。然而,该病例系列提供了关于登革热相关心肌炎临床表现范围的重要见解,支持中等程度的置信度评估。

Acute Dengue-associated Myocarditis in a Resource-limited Setting: A Case Series From Côte D'ivoire

Authors: Ines Angoran Regnier, Adama Doumbia, Corinne Akpovo, Roland N’guetta, Christophe Konin, Serge Paul Eholie, Serge Eholié
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Dengue fever can progress to severe organ dysfunction, known as the expanded dengue syndrome. Among its atypical complications, acute myocarditis is rare, underdiagnosed, and potentially fatal. We report a series of four young male patients admitted to Treichville University Hospital (Abidjan, Côte d’Ivoire) illustrating the diverse spectrum of dengue-related myocarditis. Presentations ranged from acute heart failure to high-grade atrioventricular block requiring temporary transvenous pacing, occurring during different phases of dengue illness While elevated high-sensitivity troponin was a consistent warning sign, 3-Tesla cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) imaging definitively confirmed myocardial inflammation via localized edema and subepicardial late gadolinium enhancement. This series highlights the need for a high index of suspicion during dengue outbreaks, where management demands a careful balance between fluid resuscitation and myocardial strain.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to address the underdiagnosed and potentially fatal complication of acute myocarditis associated with dengue fever, which can progress to severe organ dysfunction known as the expanded dengue syndrome. The research highlights the importance of recognizing this rare condition during dengue outbreaks.
Design and population
The study is a case series reporting on four young male patients admitted to Treichville University Hospital in Abidjan, Côte d’Ivoire, who presented with acute myocarditis during different phases of dengue illness. The population consists of individuals with dengue infection who developed myocarditis as a complication.
Core methods
The study involved clinical assessments of patients, including the use of high-sensitivity troponin testing and 3-Tesla cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) imaging to confirm myocardial inflammation through localized edema and subepicardial late gadolinium enhancement. These methods were employed to evaluate the presentations and confirm the diagnosis of dengue-related myocarditis.
Main results
The patients presented with a range of symptoms, from acute heart failure to high-grade atrioventricular block requiring temporary transvenous pacing. Elevated high-sensitivity troponin was consistently observed, and 3-Tesla CMR imaging definitively confirmed myocardial inflammation in all cases.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that these cases emphasize the need for heightened clinical suspicion of myocarditis during dengue outbreaks, as it requires careful management between fluid resuscitation and myocardial strain. This case series provides novel insights into the diverse clinical manifestations of dengue-associated myocarditis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform improved surveillance and diagnostic practices for dengue-associated myocarditis, particularly in resource-limited settings, and highlight the importance of timely and appropriate management strategies to prevent severe outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size and lack of control groups, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. However, the case series provides valuable insights into the clinical spectrum of dengue-related myocarditis, supporting a moderate confidence assessment.

基于生物发光的直接作用 登革病毒 NS5 甲基转移酶抑制剂的高通量筛选和鉴定。

Bioluminescence-based high-throughput screening and identification of direct-acting dengue virus NS5 methyltransferase inhibitors.
作者： Chenchen Li, Jianfang Zhang, Yuegao Hu, Yanyang Su, Ni Liu, Zhang Zhang, Qinhai Ma, Zifeng Yang, Yingjun Li
摘要中文翻译登革病毒 (DENV) 是一种蚊媒正黄病毒,每年感染热带和亚热带地区数百万人,对全球公共卫生构成严重且日益严重的威胁。目前尚无可用于治疗 DENV 感染的 抗病毒药物 药物。非结构蛋白 5 (NS5 MTase) 的甲基转移酶结构域在病毒 mRNA 5' cap-1 结构的合成过程中催化两个必需的甲基化反应,使其成为抗 DENV 药物开发的一个有前景但尚未得到充分验证的靶标。在此,我们使用纯化的重组 DENV NS5 MTase 优化了基于生物发光的酶促 MTase-Glo 测定,并对聚焦的甲基化化合物库进行了高通量筛选。四种化合物——SGC0946、AMI-1、salirasib 和 gambogenic Acid——被确定为有效对抗所有四种血清型(DENV-1 至 -4)的有效 DENV 甲基化抑制剂。表面等离子共振 (SPR) 分析证实了抑制剂与 NS5 MTase 之间的直接结合。其中,SGC0946对DENV-1至-4表现出最强的抑制活性,半数抑制浓度(IC 50 )为0.05-0.11μM。分子对接预测 SGC0946 与 NS5 MTase 的 S-腺苷甲硫氨酸 (SAM) 结合袋结合。此外,SGC0946 抑制 C6/36 和 BHK-21 细胞中的 DENV 复制,EC50 值分别为 6.67 和 0.60 μM。总的来说,这些发现确定了具有不同支架的新型 DENV MTase 抑制剂,并为未来合理设计更有效的 NS5 MTase 靶向抑制剂提供了有价值的分子见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒(DENV)是一种通过蚊子传播的正黄病毒科病毒,每年在热带和亚热带地区感染数百万人,对全球公共卫生构成严重且日益增长的威胁。本研究旨在鉴定DENV NS5甲基转移酶的直接作用抑制剂,作为抗病毒药物药物开发的潜在靶点。
设计与对象
该研究采用基于生物发光的酶促 MTase-Glo 测定法和纯化的重组 DENV NS5 MTase 以及聚焦甲基化化合物库的高通量筛选方法进行。该研究重点关注 DENV-1 至 DENV-4,没有提及其他黄病毒或 宿主 群体。
核心方法
非结构蛋白 5 (NS5 MTase) 的甲基转移酶结构域用于催化病毒 mRNA 5' cap-1 结构合成中的甲基化反应。该研究使用纯化的重组 DENV NS5 MTase 优化了基于生物发光的酶促 MTase-Glo 测定,并对重点甲基化化合物库进行了高通量筛选。
主要结果
四种化合物——SGC0946、AMI-1、salirasib 和 gambogenic Acid——被确定为有效对抗所有四种血清型(DENV-1 至 -4)的有效 DENV 甲基化抑制剂。其中,SGC0946对DENV-1至-4表现出最强的抑制活性,半数抑制浓度(IC 50 )为0.05-0.11μM。
解释与创新
该研究确定了具有不同支架的新型 DENV MTase 抑制剂,并为未来合理设计更有效的 NS5 MTase 靶向抑制剂提供了宝贵的分子见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为开发抗登革热病毒感染的 抗病毒药物 药物提供了潜在的分子靶标,这可能通过为登革热疾病提供新的治疗选择来显着影响公共健康。
局限与证据强度
该研究没有报告局限性,但证据是基于体外测定和分子对接预测,这可能无法完全反映体内疗效或临床相关性。这些发现的可信度是中等的。

Bioluminescence-based high-throughput screening and identification of direct-acting dengue virus NS5 methyltransferase inhibitors.

Authors: Chenchen Li, Jianfang Zhang, Yuegao Hu, Yanyang Su, Ni Liu, Zhang Zhang, Qinhai Ma, Zifeng Yang, Yingjun Li
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus (DENV) is a mosquito-borne orthoflavivirus that infects millions of people annually across tropical and subtropical regions, posing a severe and growing threat to global public health. Currently there are no antiviral drugs available for DENV infection. The methyltransferase domain of non-structural protein 5 (NS5 MTase) catalyzes two essential methylation reaction in synthesis of the 5' cap-1 structure of viral mRNA, making it a promising yet under-validated target for anti-DENV drug development. Herein, we optimized a bioluminescence-based enzymatic MTase-Glo assay using purified recombinant DENV NS5 MTase and performed high-throughput screening of the focused Methylation Compound Library. Four compounds-SGC0946, AMI-1, salirasib, and gambogenic acid-were identified as potent DENV methylation inhibitors effective against all four serotypes (DENV-1 to -4). Surface plasmon resonance (SPR) analysis confirmed the direct binding between inhibitors and NS5 MTase. Among them, SGC0946 exhibited the strongest inhibitory activity with a half-maximal inhibitory concentration (IC 50 ) of 0.05-0.11 μM against DENV-1 to -4. Molecular docking predicted that SGC0946 binds to the S-adenosylmethionine (SAM)-binding pocket of NS5 MTase. In addition, SGC0946 suppressed DENV replication in C6/36 and BHK-21 cells with EC50 values of 6.67 and 0.60 μM, respectively. Collectively, these findings identify novel DENV MTase inhibitors with diverse scaffolds and provide valuable molecular insights for the rational design of more potent NS5 MTase-targeting inhibitors in the future.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus (DENV) is a mosquito-borne orthoflavivirus that infects millions of people annually across tropical and subtropical regions, posing a severe and growing threat to global public health. The study aimed to identify direct-acting inhibitors of DENV NS5 methyltransferase as a potential target for antiviral drug development.
Design and population
The study was conducted using a bioluminescence-based enzymatic MTase-Glo assay with purified recombinant DENV NS5 MTase and a high-throughput screening approach of the focused Methylation Compound Library. The research focused on DENV-1 to DENV-4, with no mention of other flaviviruses or host populations.
Core methods
The methyltransferase domain of non-structural protein 5 (NS5 MTase) was used to catalyze methylation reactions in the synthesis of the 5' cap-1 structure of viral mRNA. The study optimized a bioluminescence-based enzymatic MTase-Glo assay using purified recombinant DENV NS5 MTase and performed high-throughput screening of the focused Methylation Compound Library.
Main results
Four compounds-SGC0946, AMI-1, salirasib, and gambogenic acid-were identified as potent DENV methylation inhibitors effective against all four serotypes (DENV-1 to -4). Among them, SGC0946 exhibited the strongest inhibitory activity with a half-maximal inhibitory concentration (IC 50 ) of 0.05-0.11 μM against DENV-1 to -4.
Interpretation and novelty
The study identified novel DENV MTase inhibitors with diverse scaffolds and provided valuable molecular insights for the rational design of more potent NS5 MTase-targeting inhibitors in the future.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings offer potential molecular targets for the development of antiviral drugs against DENV infection, which could significantly impact public health by providing new therapeutic options for dengue disease.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study does not report limitations, but the evidence is based on in vitro assays and molecular docking predictions, which may not fully reflect in vivo efficacy or clinical relevance. The confidence in these findings is moderate.

登革热 NS2B-NS3 蛋白酶结构动力学的实验和计算机分析

Experimental and In Silico Analysis of the Structural Dynamics of Dengue NS2B-NS3 Protease
作者： Susmida Seni Balakrishnan, SIVARAMAN MANJINI, Dhina S, KSHIRABDHI DHAL, Shibangi Das, Suresh Kumar Muthuvel
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革病毒 (DENV) 是全球主要的健康问题,它引起 登革热,主要影响热带和亚热带地区。DENV 的 NS2B-NS3 蛋白酶复合物对于病毒复制至关重要,使其成为 抗病毒药物 药物开发的有希望的靶点。本研究结合实验和计算方法,研究了 NS2B-NS3 蛋白酶在不同 pH 条件下的结构动力学。重组 NS2B-NS3 蛋白酶在大肠杆菌中表达,使用亲和层析纯化,并通过 SDS-PAGE 分析纯度。圆二色性 (CD) 光谱揭示了 pH 依赖性二级结构变化,表明在中性至微碱性 pH 下稳定,在酸性和强碱性条件下不稳定。动态光散射 (DLS) 分析证明了极端 pH 水平下的蛋白质聚集和结构异质性。互补的计算机技术,包括同源建模和分子动力学 (MD) 模拟,提供了对蛋白酶构象变化的详细见解。通过计算工具验证和完善的模型结构揭示了生理 pH 下的结构稳定性,在极端 pH 值下会出现显着的破坏。DSSP(蛋白质二级结构词典)和主成分分析强调了显着的二级结构转变,特别是在酸性 pH 条件下,α-螺旋和 β-折叠转化为无规卷曲。使用小分子进行对接和 MD 模拟,以进行登革热 NS2bNS3 和小分子之间的治疗分析。本研究强调了NS2B-NS3蛋白酶的pH依赖性构象可塑性,有助于理解其功能机制,并为合理设计pH特异性抑制剂提供基础。这些发现强调了结构生物学在推进 登革热 治疗策略中的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解 登革病毒 NS2B-NS3 蛋白酶在不同 pH 条件下的结构动力学,因为该复合物对于病毒复制至关重要,并且是 抗病毒药物 药物开发的一个有希望的靶点。
设计与对象
本研究涉及 登革病毒 的 NS2B-NS3 蛋白酶复合物的实验和计算分析,重点关注其在不同 pH 环境下的结构行为。
核心方法
NS2B-NS3 蛋白酶在大肠杆菌中表达,使用亲和层析纯化,并通过 SDS-PAGE 分析纯度。互补的计算机技术,包括同源建模和分子动力学 (MD) 模拟,用于提供对蛋白酶构象变化的详细了解。
主要结果
圆二色性 (CD) 光谱揭示了 pH 依赖性二级结构变化,表明在中性至微碱性 pH 下稳定,在酸性和强碱性条件下不稳定。动态光散射 (DLS) 分析证明了极端 pH 水平下的蛋白质聚集和结构异质性。通过计算工具验证和完善的模型结构揭示了生理 pH 下的结构稳定性,在极端 pH 值下会出现显着的破坏。
解释与创新
这些发现强调了 NS2B-NS3 蛋白酶的 pH 依赖性构象可塑性,有助于理解其功能机制,并为合理设计 pH 特异性抑制剂提供基础。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了结构生物学在推进 登革热 治疗策略中的重要性,提供了可为靶向 抗病毒药物 疗法的开发提供信息的见解。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外和计算机分析,可能无法完全代表体内条件。证据是温和的,因为它基于未经临床验证的实验和计算方法。

Experimental and In Silico Analysis of the Structural Dynamics of Dengue NS2B-NS3 Protease

Authors: Susmida Seni Balakrishnan, SIVARAMAN MANJINI, Dhina S, KSHIRABDHI DHAL, Shibangi Das, Suresh Kumar Muthuvel
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The Dengue virus (DENV), a major global health concern, causes dengue fever, predominantly affecting tropical and subtropical regions. The NS2B-NS3 protease complex of DENV is critical for viral replication, making it a promising target for antiviral drug development. This study investigates the structural dynamics of the NS2B-NS3 protease under varying pH conditions, integrating experimental and computational approaches. The recombinant NS2B-NS3 protease was expressed in E. coli, purified using affinity chromatography, and analysed for purity via SDS-PAGE. Circular dichroism (CD) spectroscopy revealed pH-dependent secondary structural changes, indicating stability at neutral to slightly basic pH and destabilization under acidic and highly basic conditions. Dynamic light scattering (DLS) analysis demonstrated protein aggregation and structural heterogeneity under extreme pH levels. Complementary in silico techniques, including homology modelling and molecular dynamics (MD) simulations, provided detailed insights into the conformational changes of the protease. The modelled structure, validated and refined through computational tools, revealed structural stability at physiological pH, with notable disruptions at pH extremes. DSSP (Dictionary of Secondary Structure of Proteins) and principal component analyses highlighted significant secondary structural transitions, especially at acidic pH where α-helices and β-sheets transformed into random coils Docking and MD simulation was carried out with the small molecule for therapeutic analysis between Dengue NS2bNS3 and small molecule. This study emphasizes the pH-dependent conformational plasticity of the NS2B-NS3 protease, contributing to the understanding of its functional mechanisms and providing a foundation for the rational design of pH-specific inhibitors. These findings underscore the importance of structural biology in advancing therapeutic strategies against dengue fever.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the structural dynamics of the Dengue virus NS2B-NS3 protease under varying pH conditions, as this complex is critical for viral replication and represents a promising target for antiviral drug development.
Design and population
This study involved experimental and computational analysis of the NS2B-NS3 protease complex of Dengue virus, focusing on its structural behavior under different pH environments.
Core methods
The NS2B-NS3 protease was expressed in E. coli, purified using affinity chromatography, and analyzed for purity via SDS-PAGE. Complementary in silico techniques, including homology modelling and molecular dynamics (MD) simulations, were used to provide detailed insights into the conformational changes of the protease.
Main results
Circular dichroism (CD) spectroscopy revealed pH-dependent secondary structural changes, indicating stability at neutral to slightly basic pH and destabilization under acidic and highly basic conditions. Dynamic light scattering (DLS) analysis demonstrated protein aggregation and structural heterogeneity under extreme pH levels. The modelled structure, validated and refined through computational tools, revealed structural stability at physiological pH, with notable disruptions at pH extremes.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the pH-dependent conformational plasticity of the NS2B-NS3 protease, contributing to the understanding of its functional mechanisms and providing a foundation for the rational design of pH-specific inhibitors.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings underscore the importance of structural biology in advancing therapeutic strategies against dengue fever, offering insights that could inform the development of targeted antiviral therapies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro and in silico analysis, which may not fully represent in vivo conditions. The evidence is moderate as it is based on experimental and computational methods without clinical validation.

儿科病例登革热死亡审计:三级护理中心的临床分析。

Dengue Death Audit in Pediatric Cases: A Clinical Analysis from a Tertiary Care Centre.
作者： Raghavendra G. Namadhari, Vinod H. Ratageri, Madhu P K, Shivanand Illalu, Siddappa F Dandinavar, Harish Sheri, Raghavendra G Namadhari, Vinod H Ratageri, Namadhari RG, Ratageri VH
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定导致可预防的儿科登革热死亡的可改变因素,强调尽管医疗保健取得了进步,但与登革热相关的 死亡率 仍面临着持续的挑战。
设计与对象
对 2024 年 3 月至 12 月期间入住三级护理中心的所有实验室确诊的儿童登革热死亡(1 个月至 15 岁)进行了回顾性审计,以分析 死亡率 的潜在因素。
核心方法
该研究审查了 17 名儿童登革热死亡的病历,重点关注临床表现、管理实践和结果,以确定与 死亡率 相关的可改变因素。
主要结果
70% 的儿童入院时出现 重症登革热 休克,58.8% 的儿童出现多器官功能障碍。82.4% 的病例中发现了可改变的因素,包括延迟识别代偿性休克和液体管理问题。
解释与创新
作者解释说,虽然疾病的内在严重程度很重要,但由于早期识别和管理实践的缺陷,许多儿科登革热死亡是可以预防的。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要改进培训、遵守世卫组织指南以及系统监测,以减少儿童中与登革热相关的 死亡率。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和临床记录中的潜在偏差而受到限制,但证据是强有力的,因为它是基于对 17 例登革热死亡儿童的直接分析。

Dengue Death Audit in Pediatric Cases: A Clinical Analysis from a Tertiary Care Centre.

Authors: Raghavendra G. Namadhari, Vinod H. Ratageri, Madhu P K, Shivanand Illalu, Siddappa F Dandinavar, Harish Sheri, Raghavendra G Namadhari, Vinod H Ratageri, Namadhari RG, Ratageri VH
Original abstract (English)
Risk factors for mortality in patients with dengue: A systematic review and meta-analysis. To the Editor: Preventable pediatric dengue deaths persist despite advances, underscoring the importance of mortality audits to improve care [1, 2]. We retrospectively audited all laboratory-confirmed pediatric dengue deaths (1 mo–15 y) admitted to our tertiary care centre between March and December 2024 to identify potentially modifiable contributors to mortality. Seventeen deaths were analyzed. Most children presented during the critical phase of illness with hypotension (82.4%), plasma leakage (approximately 80%), oliguria (76.5%), and multiorgan dysfunction (64.7%), indicating advanced disease at presentation. Severe dengue shock at admission (70.6%) and multiorgan dysfunction (58.8%) were the predominant non-modifiable factors associated with mortality (Supplementary Table S1). However, modifiable factors were identified in 14 (82.4%) children. Delayed recognition of compensated shock occurred in 12 patients and was predominantly attributable to delayed identification by healthcare providers at referring facilities or during the early hospital course rather than delayed caregiver presentation. Consequently, many children reached our centre with established decompensated shock. Fluid management issues were identified in 10 patients and included delayed initiation of resuscitation, inadequate titration of intravenous fluids during compensated shock, and continuation of aggressive fluid therapy beyond the plasma leakage phase, resulting in fluid overload and respiratory compromise. Delayed recognition of acute kidney injury was documented in nine patients because declining urine output and worsening renal function were not detected early, delaying escalation of supportive manag...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify modifiable factors contributing to preventable pediatric dengue deaths, highlighting the ongoing challenge of dengue-related mortality despite advances in medical care.
Design and population
A retrospective audit of all laboratory-confirmed pediatric dengue deaths (1 mo–15 y) admitted to a tertiary care centre between March and December 2024 was conducted to analyze potential contributors to mortality.
Core methods
The study involved reviewing medical records of 17 pediatric dengue deaths, focusing on clinical presentations, management practices, and outcomes to identify modifiable factors associated with mortality.
Main results
Seventy percent of children had severe dengue shock at admission, and 58.8% had multiorgan dysfunction. Modifiable factors were identified in 82.4% of the cases, including delayed recognition of compensated shock and fluid management issues.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that while intrinsic disease severity is important, many pediatric dengue deaths are preventable due to deficiencies in early recognition and management practices.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the need for improved training, adherence to WHO guidelines, and systematic monitoring to reduce dengue-related mortality in children.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential biases in clinical documentation, but the evidence is strong as it is based on direct analysis of 17 pediatric dengue deaths.

模拟 2018 年至 2024 年间厄尔尼诺南方涛动 (ENSO) 对阿根廷登革热 暴发 动态的影响。

Modelling the impact of the El Nino Southern Oscillation (ENSO) on the dynamics of Dengue outbreaks in Argentina between 2018 and 2024.
作者： Chiara Lodigiani, Alejandro Enet, Maia Arrua, Gabriela Lakkis, Lucas Moreno Lell, Carla Panzarella, Sol Otero, Ornella Bellizzi, Bullo, M., Perez-Lloret, S.
摘要中文翻译背景登革热已成为全球流行病学关注的问题,2023-2024 年季节阿根廷报告了 531,617 例病例。厄尔尼诺南方涛动 (ENSO) 现象与登革热动态变化有关。本研究评估了2018年至2024年阿根廷ENSO、当地气候变量和登革热暴发之间的关系。方法利用登革热病例和月平均气温、相对湿度和降雨量进行全国时空分析。南方涛动指数(SOI)被用作ENSO指标(厄尔尼诺相位= 0.5)。我们使用集成嵌套拉普拉斯逼近(INLA)来拟合分层贝叶斯模型。使用登革热病例的负二项分布来解释过度分散的情况。使用 Besag-York-Mollie 2 (BYM2) 结构对空间依赖性进行建模,并根据人口进行调整。结果最终模型解释了研究期间阿根廷部门每月登革热病例的 53.7% 的变异。后验概率和 95% 可信区间的暴露-反应曲线表明,在厄尔尼诺或中性阶段,当月平均气温在 20°C 至 30°C 之间时,登革热风险增加,而在拉尼娜阶段,影响不太一致。湿度 >50% 与拉尼娜阶段的登革热风险增加相关,而在厄尔尼诺阶段或中性阶段,相关性不太陡峭或不存在。控制协变量后,负 SOI 评分与登革热风险增加相关。结论 阿根廷登革热 暴发 与厄尔尼诺和中性阶段温度在 20 至 30°C 之间、拉尼娜阶段湿度 > 50% 以及 ENSO 厄尔尼诺阶段湿度独立相关。将 ENSO 指数纳入预测模型可以增强阿根廷的早期预警系统和及时的公共卫生干预措施。亮点SO_LI这项研究将厄尔尼诺现象与阿根廷的 登革热 暴发 联系起来。C_LIO_LIO风险增加的其他预测因素是厄尔尼诺和中性阶段的月平均气温在 20 摄氏度到 30 摄氏度之间,拉尼娜阶段的湿度> 50%。C_LIO_LI 登革热风险存在高度的空间残差方差,但模型中考虑的因素并未对此进行解释,而登革热风险在阿根廷北部和南部的一些省份有所增加。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
后验概率和 95% 可信区间的暴露-反应曲线表明,在厄尔尼诺或中性阶段,当月平均气温在 20°C 至 30°C 之间时,登革热风险增加,而在拉尼娜阶段,影响不太一致。
设计与对象
本研究评估了 2018 年至 2024 年阿根廷 ENSO、当地气候变量和登革热 暴发 之间的关系。
核心方法
结果最终模型解释了研究期间阿根廷部门每月登革热病例的 53.7% 的变异。
主要结果
湿度 >50% 与拉尼娜阶段的登革热风险增加相关,而在厄尔尼诺阶段或中性阶段,相关性不太陡峭或不存在。
解释与创新
C_LIO_LIO风险增加的其他预测因素是厄尔尼诺和中性阶段的月平均气温在 20 摄氏度到 30 摄氏度之间,拉尼娜阶段的湿度> 50%。
科研与公卫意义
将 ENSO 指数纳入预测模型可以增强阿根廷的早期预警系统和及时的公共卫生干预措施。
局限与证据强度
亮点SO_LI这项研究将厄尔尼诺现象与阿根廷的 登革热 暴发 联系起来。

Modelling the impact of the El Nino Southern Oscillation (ENSO) on the dynamics of Dengue outbreaks in Argentina between 2018 and 2024.

Authors: Chiara Lodigiani, Alejandro Enet, Maia Arrua, Gabriela Lakkis, Lucas Moreno Lell, Carla Panzarella, Sol Otero, Ornella Bellizzi, Bullo, M., Perez-Lloret, S.
Original abstract (English)
BackgroundDengue fever has become an epidemiological concern worldwide, with 531,617 cases reported in Argentina during the 2023-2024 season. The El Nino- Southern Oscillation (ENSO) phenomenon has been linked to changes in dengue dynamics. This study assessed the relationships among ENSO, local climatic variables, and dengue outbreaks in Argentina from 2018 to 2024. MethodsA nationwide spatiotemporal analysis was conducted using dengue cases and monthly mean temperature, relative humidity, and rainfall. The Southern Oscillation Index (SOI) was used as the ENSO indicator (El Nino phase= 0.5). We fitted Hierarchical Bayesian models with Integrated Nested Laplace Approximation (INLA). A Negative Binomial distribution of dengue cases was used to account for overdispersion. Spatial dependence was modeled by using the Besag-York-Mollie 2 (BYM2) structure, adjusted for population. ResultsThe final model explained 53.7% of the variation in Argentinas departmental monthly dengue cases during the study period. Exposure-response curves of posterior probability and 95% credible intervals suggested that dengue risk increased when monthly mean temperatures were between 20{degrees} and 30{degrees} Celsius during the El Nino or neutral phase, with less consistent effects during the La Nina phase. Humidity >50% was associated with increased dengue risk during the La Nina phase, with less steep or absent associations during El Nino or neutral phases. Negative SOI scores were associated with an increased risk of dengue after controlling for the covariates. ConclusionDengue outbreaks in Argentina were independently associated with temperatures between 20 and 30 {degrees}C during the El Nino and neutral phases, humidity > 50% during the La Nina phase, and the El Nino phase of the ENSO. Incorporating ENSO indices into predictive models could enhance early warning systems and timely public health interventions in Argentina. HIGHLIGHTSO_LIThis study linked the El Nino phenomenon to Dengue fever outbreaks in Argentina. C_LIO_LIOther predictors of increased risk were monthly mean temperatures between 20{degrees} and 30{degrees} Celsius during the El Nino and neutral phases, and humidity > 50% during the La Nina phase. C_LIO_LIThere was a high degree of spatial residual variance, not accounted for by the factors considered in the model, in dengue risk, which was increased in some northern and southern provinces of Argentina.

Exposure-response curves of posterior probability and 95% credible intervals suggested that dengue risk increased when monthly mean temperatures were between 20{degrees} and 30{degrees} Celsius during the El Nino or neutral phase, with less consistent effects during the La Nina phase.

印度尼西亚马塔兰城市高 发病率 地区循环 登革病毒 血清型的分子检测和空间分布(2024-2025 年)

Molecular detection and spatial distribution of circulating dengue virus serotypes in an urban high-incidence area of Mataram, Indonesia (2024–2025)
作者： Raodatul Jannah, Sitti Rahmah Umniyati, Tri Baskoro Tunggul Satoto, Nur Alvira Pascawati, Muhammad Sopian Sauri, Imran Sadewo, Hilmi Hidayati, Rizal M. Suhardi, Sais Sularsah
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革热 (DF) 是一种由四种 登革病毒 血清型(DENV-1 至 DENV-4)引起的蚊媒病毒性疾病,仍然是包括印度尼西亚在内的热带地区的主要公共卫生问题。2024年,马塔兰市报告了364例确诊的登革热病例,表明当地负担沉重。这项观察性横断面研究于 2024 年 11 月至 2025 年 1 月在马塔兰市 Karang Pule 健康中心工作区内的 8 个高 发病率 邻里协会进行。根据世界卫生组织昆虫学 监测 指南,从基于病例的家庭收集蚊子幼虫和产卵器样本,将其饲养为 F1 成年雌性,并汇集(每个地点 20 只蚊子)进行病毒 RNA 提取。登革病毒 检测和血清分型是使用一步 RT-PCR 测定和血清型特异性引物进行的,包括适当的阳性和阴性对照,以确保测定的有效性。八个采样地点中有五个呈 DENV 阳性。DENV-3 是主要血清型,在三个地点检测到,而 DENV-2 和 DENV-3 在两个地点共循环。未检测到 DENV-1 或 DENV-4。西帕古坦等村庄的报告病例比例和 发病率 率最高。记录每个地点的环境参数(温度、湿度、水温和光照强度)并进行描述性分析。在 F1 代埃及伊蚊中检测到 DENV,支持了局部病毒持续循环的证据,并表明垂直(经卵巢)传播 在维持病毒 储存宿主 方面的潜在贡献。这项研究提供了印度尼西亚城市环境中登革热血清型分布的精细尺度、分区级地图,提供了本地化证据来支持有针对性的 媒介 控制策略,同时承认与样本量和地理覆盖范围相关的局限性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 仍然是包括印度尼西亚在内的热带地区的主要公共卫生问题,因为它由蚊子传播,并且存在四种 登革病毒 血清型(DENV-1 至 DENV-4)。
设计与对象
这项观察性横断面研究于 2024 年 11 月至 2025 年 1 月在马塔兰市 Karang Pule 卫生中心工作区内的 8 个高 发病率 邻里协会进行,涉及 364 例确诊的登革热病例。
核心方法
根据世界卫生组织昆虫学 监测 指南,从基于病例的家庭收集蚊子幼虫和产卵器样本,将其饲养为 F1 成年雌性,并汇集(每个地点 20 只蚊子)进行病毒 RNA 提取。登革病毒 检测和血清分型是使用一步 RT-PCR 测定和血清型特异性引物进行的,包括适当的阳性和阴性对照,以确保测定的有效性。
主要结果
八个采样地点中有五个呈 DENV 阳性。DENV-3 是主要血清型,在三个地点检测到,而 DENV-2 和 DENV-3 在两个地点共循环。未检测到 DENV-1 或 DENV-4。
解释与创新
在 F1 代埃及伊蚊中检测到 DENV,支持了局部病毒持续循环的证据,并表明垂直(经卵巢)传播 在维持病毒 储存宿主 方面的潜在贡献。
科研与公卫意义
这项研究提供了印度尼西亚城市环境中登革热血清型分布的精细尺度、分区级地图,为支持有针对性的 媒介 控制策略提供了本地化证据。
局限与证据强度
该研究承认与样本量和地理覆盖范围相关的局限性,并且由于这些限制,证据强度适中。

Molecular detection and spatial distribution of circulating dengue virus serotypes in an urban high-incidence area of Mataram, Indonesia (2024–2025)

Authors: Raodatul Jannah, Sitti Rahmah Umniyati, Tri Baskoro Tunggul Satoto, Nur Alvira Pascawati, Muhammad Sopian Sauri, Imran Sadewo, Hilmi Hidayati, Rizal M. Suhardi, Sais Sularsah
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Dengue fever (DF) is a mosquito-borne viral disease caused by four dengue virus serotypes (DENV-1 to DENV-4) and remains a major public health concern in tropical regions, including Indonesia. In 2024, Mataram City reported 364 confirmed dengue cases, indicating a substantial local burden. This observational cross-sectional study was conducted from November 2024 to January 2025 in eight high-incidence neighborhood associations within the Karang Pule Health Center working area, Mataram City. Mosquito larvae and ovitrap samples were collected from case-based households following WHO entomological surveillance guidelines, reared to F1 adult females, and pooled (20 mosquitoes per site) for viral RNA extraction. Dengue virus detection and serotyping were performed using a one-step RT-PCR assay with serotype-specific primers, including appropriate positive and negative controls to ensure assay validity. Five of eight sampling locations were positive for DENV. DENV-3 was the predominant serotype, detected in three locations, while co-circulation of DENV-2 and DENV-3 was identified in two locations. No DENV-1 or DENV-4 was detected. Villages such as West Pagutan exhibited the highest proportion of reported cases and incidence rates. Environmental parameters (temperature, humidity, water temperature, and light intensity) were recorded at each site and analyzed descriptively. The detection of DENV in F1-generation Aedes aegypti supports evidence of ongoing local viral circulation and suggests the potential contribution of vertical (transovarial) transmission in maintaining the virus reservoir. This study provides fine-scale, sub-district-level mapping of dengue serotype distribution in an urban Indonesian setting, offering localized evidence to support targeted vector control strategies while acknowledging limitations related to sample size and geographic coverage.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever remains a major public health concern in tropical regions, including Indonesia, due to its mosquito-borne transmission and the presence of four dengue virus serotypes (DENV-1 to DENV-4).
Design and population
This observational cross-sectional study was conducted from November 2024 to January 2025 in eight high-incidence neighborhood associations within the Karang Pule Health Center working area, Mataram City, involving 364 confirmed dengue cases.
Core methods
Mosquito larvae and ovitrap samples were collected from case-based households following WHO entomological surveillance guidelines, reared to F1 adult females, and pooled (20 mosquitoes per site) for viral RNA extraction. Dengue virus detection and serotyping were performed using a one-step RT-PCR assay with serotype-specific primers, including appropriate positive and negative controls to ensure assay validity.
Main results
Five of eight sampling locations were positive for DENV. DENV-3 was the predominant serotype, detected in three locations, while co-circulation of DENV-2 and DENV-3 was identified in two locations. No DENV-1 or DENV-4 was detected.
Interpretation and novelty
The detection of DENV in F1-generation Aedes aegypti supports evidence of ongoing local viral circulation and suggests the potential contribution of vertical (transovarial) transmission in maintaining the virus reservoir.
Scientific and public-health significance
This study provides fine-scale, sub-district-level mapping of dengue serotype distribution in an urban Indonesian setting, offering localized evidence to support targeted vector control strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges limitations related to sample size and geographic coverage, and the evidence strength is moderate due to these constraints.

使用具有基于块的序列建模和可解释性分析的 Transformer 从完整的 基因组 中进行 登革病毒 血清型分类

Dengue virus serotype classification from complete genomes using a Transformer with chunk-based sequence modeling and explainability analysis
作者： Francesca Galeone, Nicola Altini, Mariapia Toscanelli, Davide Cacchiarelli, Anna De Filippis, Massimiliano Galdiero, Antonio Brunetti, Gian Maria Zaccaria, Vitoantonio Bevilacqua
摘要中文翻译人工智能越来越多地用于传染病的 基因组监测,但预测模型必须在现实的泛化设置下进行评估并谨慎解释。我们提出了 XAI-DENFormer,这是一个面向可解释性的框架,用于根据完整病毒 基因组 进行 登革病毒 (DENV) 血清型分类。由 29,091 个公开可用的 DENV 基因组 组成的精选数据集被编码为核苷酸级 one-hot 序列,并使用基于块的 Transformer 编码器进行处理,该编码器专为内存高效的整体 基因组 分析而设计。使用三种互补的验证协议来评估泛化性:CD-HIT 集群感知、地理和时间验证。DENFormer 变体在相同条件下针对逻辑回归、前馈神经网络、Longformer 和 Performer 进行了基准测试。平均池 DENFormer 配置在所有三种验证协议中都保持了高性能,在 CD-HIT 折叠中的准确度为 98.68-99.91%,在地理保留中的准确度为 97.18-100.00%,在时间保留中的准确度为 96.35-99.86%。为了检查模型行为,我们结合了注意力图、梯度×输入归因、嵌入可视化和区域统计分析。所得的概况支持跨结构、非结构和非翻译区域分布的血清型相关信号,而不是依赖于单个基因组位点。XAI-DENFormer 为大规模 DENV 基因组 分类和模型引导的基因组检查提供了强大且可解释的框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在利用基因组数据开发一个可解释的 登革病毒 血清型分类框架,解决 基因组监测 中准确且可解释的预测模型的需求。
设计与对象
该研究涉及由 29,091 个公开可用的 登革病毒 基因组 组成的精选数据集,使用基于块的 Transformer 编码器进行处理,以进行内存高效的整体 基因组 分析。
核心方法
该框架使用三个补充协议进行了验证:CD-HIT 集群感知、地理和时间验证。DENFormer 变体在相同条件下针对逻辑回归、前馈神经网络、Longformer 和 Performer 进行了基准测试。
主要结果
均值池 DENFormer 配置在 CD-HIT 折叠中实现了 98.68-99.91% 的准确度,在地理保留中实现了 97.18-100.00% 的准确度,在时间保留中实现了 96.35-99.86% 的准确度。
解释与创新
该模型在不同验证设置中的表现凸显了其稳健性,而可解释性分析揭示了跨多个基因组区域的分布式血清型相关信号。
科研与公卫意义
XAI-DENFormer 为大规模 登革病毒 基因组 分类提供了可靠且可解释的工具,有助于 基因组监测 和了解血清型分布模式。
局限与证据强度
该研究的仅抽象证据限制了对模型行为的详细分析,但多个验证协议的高精度支持了对该框架有效性的适度信心。

Dengue virus serotype classification from complete genomes using a Transformer with chunk-based sequence modeling and explainability analysis

Authors: Francesca Galeone, Nicola Altini, Mariapia Toscanelli, Davide Cacchiarelli, Anna De Filippis, Massimiliano Galdiero, Antonio Brunetti, Gian Maria Zaccaria, Vitoantonio Bevilacqua
Original abstract (English)
Artificial intelligence is increasingly used for genomic surveillance of infectious diseases, but predictive models must be evaluated under realistic generalization settings and interpreted cautiously. We present XAI-DENFormer, an explainability-oriented framework for dengue virus (DENV) serotype classification from complete viral genomes. A curated dataset of 29,091 publicly available DENV genomes was encoded as nucleotide-level one-hot sequences and processed with a chunk-based Transformer encoder designed for memory-efficient whole-genome analysis. Generalization was assessed using three complementary validation protocols: CD-HIT cluster-aware, geographical, and temporal validation. DENFormer variants were benchmarked against Logistic Regression, a feed-forward neural network, Longformer, and Performer under identical conditions. The mean-pooling DENFormer configuration maintained high performance across all three validation protocols, with accuracies of 98.68–99.91% across CD-HIT folds, 97.18–100.00% across geographical holdouts, and 96.35–99.86% across temporal holdouts. To inspect model behavior, we combined attention maps, Gradient × Input attributions, embedding visualizations, and regional statistical analyses. The resulting profiles supported distributed serotype-associated signals across structural, non-structural, and untranslated regions rather than reliance on a single genomic locus. XAI-DENFormer provides a robust and interpretable framework for large-scale DENV genome classification and model-guided genomic inspection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop an interpretable framework for dengue virus serotype classification using genomic data, addressing the need for accurate and explainable predictive models in genomic surveillance.
Design and population
The study involved a curated dataset of 29,091 publicly available dengue virus genomes, processed using a chunk-based Transformer encoder for memory-efficient whole-genome analysis.
Core methods
The framework was validated using three complementary protocols: CD-HIT cluster-aware, geographical, and temporal validation. DENFormer variants were benchmarked against Logistic Regression, a feed-forward neural network, Longformer, and Performer under identical conditions.
Main results
The mean-pooling DENFormer configuration achieved accuracies of 98.68–99.91% across CD-HIT folds, 97.18–100.00% across geographical holdouts, and 96.35–99.86% across temporal holdouts.
Interpretation and novelty
The model's performance across diverse validation settings highlights its robustness, while the explainability analysis revealed distributed serotype-associated signals across multiple genomic regions.
Scientific and public-health significance
XAI-DENFormer offers a reliable and interpretable tool for large-scale dengue virus genome classification, aiding in genomic surveillance and understanding serotype distribution patterns.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's abstract-only evidence limits detailed analysis of model behavior, but the high accuracy across multiple validation protocols supports a moderate confidence in the framework's effectiveness.

基因组分析确定了野生埃及伊蚊登革热 媒介 蚊子 宿主 摄食行为的候选超基因架构

Genomic analysis identifies candidate supergene architecture for host-feeding behavior in wild Aedes aegypti dengue vector mosquitoes
作者： Devojit Kumar Sarma, Sudeesh Warkare, Sweta Mishra, Deepanker Das, Lokendra Rathod, Manoj Kumawat, Siddharth Singh Tomar, Samradhi Singh, Manoj Kumar, Rajnarayan R. Tiwari
摘要中文翻译背景 埃及伊蚊是全球主要的虫媒病毒 媒介,其以人类 宿主 为食的亲和力是 传播 强度的关键决定因素。尽管人类性在流行病学上具有重要性,但 宿主 进食行为的分子基础的基因组结构仍未得到充分研究。方法在这里,我们提出了一项综合的 基因组 全关联研究 (GWAS) 和对 21 种现场采集的白蚊 宿主 摄食行为的结构变异分析。埃及伊蚊雌性的表型为人类血液喂养(HF;n = 11)或非人类血液喂养(NHF;n = 10)。全 基因组 测序产生了分布在所有三个染色体上的 661,519 个高质量 SNP。对连锁不平衡修剪数据集的主成分分析揭示了适度的群体结构(PC1 = 11.69%,PC2 = 10.98%)。结果 GWAS 使用具有 5 个 PC 协变量 (λ = 1.05) 的一般线性模型在基因中识别出 11 个暗示性 SNP,包括 2 号染色体上的肌动蛋白结合蛋白和 3 号染色体上的 G 蛋白偶联受体 39。使用 Delly 进行的染色体倒位分析确定了 2 号染色体上的 HF 特异性倒位,其中包含 13 个基因,涵盖三个功能上一致的化学感应类别:感觉发育调节器水龙头、气味受体(Or6、 Or31、Or33)和气味结合蛋白(Gp68)以及八个味觉受体基因(Gr15–Gr19a、Gr35、Gr36、Gr66)。结论 单个非重组染色体单位内共同适应的化学感应基因的浓度与可能的人性化超基因模型一致,与冈比亚按蚊中倒位介导的行为分歧相一致。这些探索性发现为伊蚊 宿主 寻找行为提供了初步的基因组框架。aegypti 并确定可能的候选基因座,在应用于 媒介 监测 之前保证功能验证。

问题与背景
该研究旨在解决对埃及伊蚊 宿主 摄食行为的基因组基础缺乏了解的问题,这对于登革热 传播 至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究涉及 基因组 全关联研究 (GWAS) 和对 21 种现场采集的白蚊 宿主 摄食行为的结构变异分析。埃及伊蚊雌性,表型为人类血液喂养(HF;n = 11)或非人类血液喂养(NHF;n = 10)。
核心方法
进行全 基因组 测序以鉴定 661,519 个高质量 SNP,并使用主成分分析来评估群体结构。GWAS 使用具有五个 PC 协变量的一般线性模型进行。
主要结果
主成分分析显示人口结构适中(PC1 = 11.69%,PC2 = 10.98%)。GWAS 在与化学感应功能相关的基因中发现了 11 个提示性 SNP,包括 2 号染色体上的肌动蛋白结合蛋白和 3 号染色体上的 G 蛋白偶联受体 39。
解释与创新
该研究表明,单个非重组染色体单元内的共同适应化学感应基因的浓度可能支持人类性的超基因模型,类似于在冈比亚按蚊中观察到的倒位介导的行为分歧。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为理解伊蚊的 宿主 寻找行为提供了初步的基因组框架。aegypti,这可以为未来关于 媒介 监测 和控制策略的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是探索性的,基于仅抽象的分析,这限制了研究结果的可信度。证据是中等的,因为它确定了候选基因座,但需要进一步的功能验证。

Genomic analysis identifies candidate supergene architecture for host-feeding behavior in wild Aedes aegypti dengue vector mosquitoes

Authors: Devojit Kumar Sarma, Sudeesh Warkare, Sweta Mishra, Deepanker Das, Lokendra Rathod, Manoj Kumawat, Siddharth Singh Tomar, Samradhi Singh, Manoj Kumar, Rajnarayan R. Tiwari
Original abstract (English)
Background Aedes aegypti is the primary arboviral vector globally, and its affinity to feed on human hosts is a key determinant of transmission intensity. Despite the epidemiological importance of anthropophily, the genomic architecture underlying the molecular basis of host-feeding behavior remains understudied. Methods Here, we present an integrated genome-wide association study (GWAS) and structural variant analysis of host-feeding behavior in 21 field-collected Ae. aegypti females phenotyped as human-blood-fed (HF; n = 11) or non-human-blood-fed (NHF; n = 10). Whole-genome sequencing yielded 661,519 high-quality SNPs distributed across all three chromosomes. Principal component analysis on a linkage disequilibrium-pruned dataset revealed modest population structure (PC1 = 11.69%, PC2 = 10.98%). Results GWAS using a general linear model with five PC covariates (λ = 1.05) identified 11 suggestive SNPs in genes including an actin binding protein on chromosome 2 and G-protein coupled receptor 39 on chromosome 3. Chromosomal inversion analysis using Delly identified an HF-specific inversion on chromosome 2 harboring 13 genes across three functionally coherent chemosensory categories: the sensory developmental regulator tap, odorant receptors (Or6, Or31, Or33) and an odorant-binding protein ( Gp68 ), and eight gustatory receptor genes ( Gr15–Gr19a, Gr35, Gr36, Gr66 ). Conclusion The concentration of co-adapted chemosensory genes within a single non-recombining chromosomal unit is consistent with a possible supergene model of anthropophily, paralleling inversion-mediated behavioral divergence in Anopheles gambiae . These exploratory findings provide a preliminary genomic framework for host-seeking behavior in Ae. aegypti and identify probable candidate loci warranting functional validation before application to vector surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of understanding regarding the genomic basis of host-feeding behavior in Aedes aegypti mosquitoes, which is critical for dengue transmission.
Design and population
The study involved a genome-wide association study (GWAS) and structural variant analysis of host-feeding behavior in 21 field-collected Ae. aegypti females, phenotyped as human-blood-fed (HF; n = 11) or non-human-blood-fed (NHF; n = 10).
Core methods
Whole-genome sequencing was performed to identify 661,519 high-quality SNPs, and principal component analysis was used to assess population structure. GWAS was conducted using a general linear model with five PC covariates.
Main results
Principal component analysis revealed modest population structure (PC1 = 11.69%, PC2 = 10.98%). GWAS identified 11 suggestive SNPs in genes related to chemosensory functions, including an actin binding protein on chromosome 2 and G-protein coupled receptor 39 on chromosome 3.
Interpretation and novelty
The study suggests that a concentration of co-adapted chemosensory genes within a single non-recombining chromosomal unit may support a supergene model of anthropophily, similar to inversion-mediated behavioral divergence observed in Anopheles gambiae.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide a preliminary genomic framework for understanding host-seeking behavior in Ae. aegypti, which could inform future studies on vector surveillance and control strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is exploratory and based on an abstract-only analysis, which limits the confidence in the findings. The evidence is moderate as it identifies candidate loci but requires further functional validation.

通过基于情感的网络分析、空间映射和时间动态检查印度尼西亚 Twitter 上与登革热相关的信息传播

Examining Dengue-related Information Diffusion on Indonesian Twitter Through Sentiment-based Network Analysis, Spatial Mapping, and Temporal Dynamics
作者： Ribka Devina Margaretha, Wiwik Anggraeni
摘要中文翻译登革热 仍然是印度尼西亚的一项重大公共卫生挑战,但整合情感分析、社交网络分析和空间分析来检查社交媒体上登革热相关信息传播的研究仍然有限。这项研究提出了一个综合框架,应用于 2021 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月期间从印度尼西亚 Twitter 收集的 5,715 条登革热相关推文。使用 IndoBERT 进行的情感分类显示,主要是中性话语 (65.23%),有意义的负面言论(20.31%) 和正 (14.45%) 比例。构建和建立了情感加权交互网络,揭示了高度碎片化的结构,模块化得分超过 0.98。医疗保健专业人员通过介数中心性成为最具影响力的参与者,基于 PageRank 的影响力以自循环 治疗 为条件,而卫生部拥有最高的入度值。空间分析,主要基于用户自我报告位置、表明爪哇-巴厘岛走廊沿线的地理集中度以及时间分析显示 1 月和 6 月反复出现的高峰与印度尼西亚的雨季和登革热 暴发 周期一致。这些发现证明了整合情绪、网络和空间维度对于全面公共卫生传播分析的价值。

Examining Dengue-related Information Diffusion on Indonesian Twitter Through Sentiment-based Network Analysis, Spatial Mapping, and Temporal Dynamics

Authors: Ribka Devina Margaretha, Wiwik Anggraeni
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever remains a major public health challenge in Indonesia, yet studies integrating sentiment analysis, Social Network Analysis, and spatial analysis to examine dengue-related information diffusion on social media remain limited.This study proposes an integrated framework applied to 5,715 dengue-related tweets collected from Indonesian Twitter between January 2021 and December 2025.Sentiment classification using IndoBERT revealed a predominantly neutral discourse (65.23%), with meaningful negative (20.31%) and positive (14.45%) proportions.Sentiment-weighted interaction networks were constructed and analysed, revealing highly fragmented structures with modularity scores exceeding 0.98.Healthcare professionals emerged as the most robustly influential actors by betweenness centrality, with PageRank-based influence conditional on self-loop treatment, while the Ministry of Health held the highest in-degree values.Spatial analysis, based primarily on self-reported user locations, indicated geographic concentration along the Java-Bali corridor, and temporal analysis revealed recurring peaks in January and June aligned with Indonesia's wet season and dengue outbreak cycles.These findings demonstrate the value of integrating sentiment, network, and spatial dimensions for comprehensive public health communication analysis.

登革病毒串联xrRNA之间的结构桥促进外切核酸酶抗性的协调

A structural bridge between dengue virus tandem xrRNAs facilitates coordination of exonuclease resistance
作者： Elizabeth Spear, Zoe O'Donoghue, Steve L. Bonilla, Kevin P. Larsen, Madeline E. Sherlock, Jeffrey S. Kieft
摘要中文翻译摘要 正黄病毒 RNA 基因组 可抵抗 宿主 5'–3' 核糖核酸外切酶,产生亚基因组黄病毒 RNA (sfRNA)。这种抵抗力是由病毒 3' 非翻译区内的外核糖核酸酶抗性 RNA (xrRNA) 结构赋予的,这些结构通常串联出现,并且其功能可以耦合。在 登革病毒 血清型 2 (DENV2) 中,这种偶联导致与病毒适应 宿主 的能力相关的 sfRNA 特性和丰度模式发生变化。这种偶联的物理基础尚不清楚。我们结合病毒学、生物化学、生物信息学、结构生物学和生物物理学,探索了 DENV2 中串联 xrRNA 偶联的结构和序列决定因素。我们发现串联 xrRNA 的空间邻近性、顺序和结构完整性对于偶联都很重要。此外,位于两个 xrRNA 之间的未配对的富含 A 的接头对于稳定与偶联相关的特定结构至关重要。这种富含 A 的序列可能与相邻的茎环结构形成三级接触,从而在两个 xrRNA 之间形成物理桥,这一发现得到了 DENV2 串联 xrRNA 的中分辨率冷冻电子显微镜 (cryo-EM) 图的支持。突变 破坏该桥的结构会改变串联 xrRNA 之间的相对方向或间距,这与其功能耦合相关。这些发现有助于为串联 xrRNA 之间的耦合提供解释,提出一种新的机制假设,其中两个串联 xrRNA 可以同时遇到 Xrn1。重要性 登革病毒 (DENV) 产生非编码亚基因组黄病毒 RNA (sfRNA),影响多种细胞途径,对于成功感染非常重要。这些 sfRNA 由病毒 基因组 中称为核糖核酸外切酶抗性 RNA (xrRNA) 的结构化 RNA 元件形成,它们折叠成独特的三维拓扑结构,以阻止 宿主 细胞核糖核酸外切酶的降解,并且通常串联发生。感染期间产生的 sfRNA 的特定模式对于 宿主 与 媒介 的适应性非常重要,并且在 DENV2 中,这种模式取决于串联 xrRNA 之间的功能耦合。然而,这种功能耦合的来源尚不清楚。我们确定串联 xrRNA 之间未配对的富含 A 的接头对于在串联 xrRNA 之间创建结构桥是必要的。这种桥似乎有利于串联 xrRNA 之间的特定方向,该方向与耦合相关,因此与感染期间产生的 sfRNA 的模式和相对丰度相关。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒 产生亚基因组黄病毒 RNA (sfRNA),这对于成功感染很重要,但串联 xrRNA 之间功能耦合的机制尚不清楚。本研究旨在揭示这种耦合的结构基础。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注 登革病毒 血清型 2 (DENV2),并结合病毒学、生物化学、生物信息学、结构生物学和生物物理学探索串联 xrRNA 偶联的结构和序列决定因素。该研究是在实验室环境中进行的。
核心方法
研究人员采用多学科方法,结合病毒学、生物化学、生物信息学、结构生物学和生物物理学来研究 DENV2 中串联 xrRNA 偶联的结构和序列决定因素。他们分析了串联 xrRNA 的空间邻近性、顺序和结构完整性,并检查了未配对的富含 A 的接头的作用。
主要结果
研究发现串联 xrRNA 的空间邻近性、顺序和结构完整性对于它们的耦合非常重要。此外,两个 xrRNA 之间未配对的富含 A 的接头对于稳定与偶联相关的特定结构至关重要。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,富含 A 的接头在串联 xrRNA 之间形成了物理桥梁,有利于与其功能耦合相关的特定方向。这为串联 xrRNA 如何同时遇到 Xrn1 提供了新的机制假设。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果有助于了解 登革病毒 RNA 对 宿主 核糖核酸外切酶的抗性分子机制,这可以为未来的病毒适应研究和针对 RNA 降解的 抗病毒药物 策略的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象证据,这限制了分析的深度。然而,这些发现得到了中分辨率冷冻电镜图和 突变 实验的支持,为所提出的结构模型提供了适度的信心。

A structural bridge between dengue virus tandem xrRNAs facilitates coordination of exonuclease resistance

Authors: Elizabeth Spear, Zoe O'Donoghue, Steve L. Bonilla, Kevin P. Larsen, Madeline E. Sherlock, Jeffrey S. Kieft
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Orthoflavivirus RNA genomes resist host 5′–3′ exoribonucleases to produce subgenomic flaviviral RNAs (sfRNAs). This resistance is conferred by exoribonuclease-resistant RNA (xrRNA) structures within the viral 3′ untranslated region that often occur in tandem, and whose function can be coupled. In dengue virus serotype 2 (DENV2), this coupling results in changing patterns of sfRNA identity and abundance associated with the ability of the virus to adapt to host vs. The physical basis of this coupling was unknown. Using a combination of virology, biochemistry, bioinformatics, structural biology, and biophysics, we explored the structural and sequence determinants of tandem xrRNA coupling in DENV2. We discovered that the spatial proximity, order, and structural integrity of the tandem xrRNAs are all important for coupling. Furthermore, an unpaired A-rich linker that lies between the two xrRNAs is essential in stabilizing a specific structure that correlates to coupling. This A-rich sequence likely forms tertiary contacts with an adjacent stem-loop structure to form a physical bridge between the two xrRNAs, a finding that is supported by a mid-resolution cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM) map of the DENV2 tandem xrRNAs. Disruption of the structure of this bridge by mutation changes the relative orientation or spacing between the tandem xrRNAs, which is correlated to their functional coupling. These findings help provide an explanation for the coupling between tandem xrRNAs, suggesting a new mechanistic hypothesis in which the two tandem xrRNAs can simultaneously encounter Xrn1. IMPORTANCE Dengue virus (DENV) generates non-coding subgenomic flaviviral RNAs (sfRNAs) that affect several cellular pathways and are important for successful infection. These sfRNAs are formed by structured RNA elements in the viral genome called exoribonuclease-resistant RNAs (xrRNAs), which fold into a distinct three-dimensional topology to block degradation by host cell exoribonucleases and often occur in tandem. Specific patterns of sfRNAs made during infection are important for host vs. vector fitness, and in DENV2, this pattern depends on functional coupling between tandem xrRNAs. However, the source of this functional coupling was unknown. We determined that an unpaired A-rich linker between the tandem xrRNAs is necessary for creating a structural bridge between the tandem xrRNAs. This bridge appears to favor a specific orientation between the tandem xrRNAs that is correlated to coupling and therefore to the patterns and relative abundance of sfRNAs produced during infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus generates subgenomic flaviviral RNAs (sfRNAs) that are important for successful infection, but the mechanism underlying the functional coupling between tandem xrRNAs was unknown. This study aimed to uncover the structural basis of this coupling.
Design and population
The study focused on dengue virus serotype 2 (DENV2) and explored the structural and sequence determinants of tandem xrRNA coupling using a combination of virology, biochemistry, bioinformatics, structural biology, and biophysics. The research was conducted in a laboratory setting.
Core methods
The researchers used a multidisciplinary approach, combining virology, biochemistry, bioinformatics, structural biology, and biophysics to investigate the structural and sequence determinants of tandem xrRNA coupling in DENV2. They analyzed the spatial proximity, order, and structural integrity of the tandem xrRNAs and examined the role of an unpaired A-rich linker.
Main results
The study found that the spatial proximity, order, and structural integrity of the tandem xrRNAs are important for their coupling. Additionally, an unpaired A-rich linker between the two xrRNAs is essential for stabilizing a specific structure that correlates with coupling.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the A-rich linker forms a physical bridge between the tandem xrRNAs, favoring a specific orientation that is correlated with their functional coupling. This provides a new mechanistic hypothesis for how tandem xrRNAs can simultaneously encounter Xrn1.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results contribute to understanding the molecular mechanisms of dengue virus RNA resistance to host exoribonucleases, which could inform future studies on viral adaptation and the development of antiviral strategies targeting RNA degradation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence, which limits the depth of analysis. However, the findings are supported by a mid-resolution cryo-EM map and mutation experiments, providing moderate confidence in the structural model proposed.
