通过脱落动力学和 RNA 稳定性分析优化废水中的 监测:对预警系统的影响

Optimizing dengue virus surveillance in wastewater through shedding dynamics and RNA stability analysis: Implications for early warning systems
作者： Wenhui Liu, Wenzhe Su, Chanjuan Qu, Haoran Chen, Linan Wu, Yingjun Zhang, Minghao Li, Ling Yuanli, Qinglian Liu, Huiwen Wu
摘要中文翻译基于废水的 流行病学 (WBE) 用于 登革病毒 (DENV) 是一种很有前途的工具,但由于缺乏病毒脱落和稳定性的基本数据而受到阻碍。在这里,我们通过分析从 132 名住院患者的前瞻性 队列研究 中在症状出现后 23 天的窗口中收集的 769 份连续唾液、尿液和粪便样本,绘制了高分辨率纵向排泄轨迹,解析了真实环境基质中的降解常数,并构建了预测性前向敏感性模型。临床筛查将尿液确定为最可靠的监测目标,诊断阳性率为88.4%。然而,以 25°C 地下下水道基线为基准的稳定性实验表明,真实患者排泄物混合物(混合尿液、粪便和唾液)中的 DENV-1 RNA 降解速度比简化对照中的标准假病毒峰值快近五倍,产生约 3.4 天的 90% 衰减时间 T 90 )。建模结果表明,这种加速降解将传统的集中式抓取采样限制在每 184 人 1 例感染的检测阈值。相比之下,近源被动采样可以克服这一瓶颈,实现每 35,130 人 1 例的理论检测极限。考虑到经验现场检测限制,这种时间积分方法保持了高度灵敏的实际检测阈值,范围从每 3,513 人 1 例到 17,565 人。

Optimizing dengue virus surveillance in wastewater through shedding dynamics and RNA stability analysis: Implications for early warning systems

Authors: Wenhui Liu, Wenzhe Su, Chanjuan Qu, Haoran Chen, Linan Wu, Yingjun Zhang, Minghao Li, Ling Yuanli, Qinglian Liu, Huiwen Wu
Original abstract (English)
Wastewater-based epidemiology (WBE) for dengue virus (DENV) is a promising tool but hindered by a lack of fundamental data on viral shedding and stability. Here, we mapped high-resolution longitudinal shedding trajectories by analyzing 769 serial saliva, urine, and fecal specimens collected from a prospective cohort of 132 hospitalized patients across a 23-day post-symptom onset window, resolved degradation constants within authentic environmental matrices, and constructed a predictive forward sensitivity model. Clinical screening identified urine as the most reliable surveillance target, exhibiting a diagnostic positivity rate of 88.4%. However, stability experiments benchmarking a 25°C subterranean sewer baseline revealed that DENV-1 RNA within authentic patient excreta mixtures (mixed urine, feces, and saliva) degraded nearly fivefold faster than standard pseudovirus spikes in simplified controls, yielding a 90% decay time T 90 ) of approximately 3.4 days. Modeling results indicate that this accelerated degradation limits conventional centralized grab sampling to a detection threshold of 1 infection per 184 individuals. In contrast, near-source passive sampling can overcome this bottleneck, achieving a theoretical detection limit of 1 case per 35,130 people. Factoring in empirical field detection limits, this time-integrated approach maintains a highly sensitive practical detection threshold ranging from 1 case per 3,513 to 17,565 individuals.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Wastewater-based epidemiology (WBE) for dengue virus (DENV) is a promising tool but hindered by a lack of fundamental data on viral shedding and stability.
Design and population
Here, we mapped high-resolution longitudinal shedding trajectories by analyzing 769 serial saliva, urine, and fecal specimens collected from a prospective cohort of 132 hospitalized patients across a 23-day post-symptom onset window, resolved degradation constants within authentic environmental matrices, and constructed a predictive forward sensitivity model.

预测东部苏丹2023年登革热临床严重程度:使用常规数据对统计模型和机器学习模型的比较分析

Predicting Dengue Clinical Severity in Eastern Sudan's 2023 Outbreak: A Comparative Analysis of Statistical and Machine Learning Models Using Routine Surveillance Data
作者： Fathelrhman EL Guma, Elkhatim Abuelysar, EihabAbdelhai Osman, Bashir ElnourElbashir Mukhtar, Mumdouh M.M. Hassan, Alshaikh A. Shokeralla
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在描述2023年东部苏丹登革热临床严重程度模式,并识别相关因素以提高对登革热疾病进展的理解。
设计与对象
本研究使用东部苏丹阿尔卡达里夫州2023年2,552例登革热病例记录进行回顾性观察研究,重点关注有记录临床表现的患者。
核心方法
本研究利用常规监测数据定义了一个基于研究的综合临床严重程度指标。分析包括描述性统计、多变量逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost、多重对应分析和层次聚类。
主要结果
总体而言,182 名患者 (7.13%) 符合研究特定的综合临床严重程度指标。死亡人数为 32 人,病死率为 1.25%。具有严重程度标志物的患者中的 死亡率 高于没有严重程度标志物的患者(3.85% vs. 1.05%;相对风险=3.65)。
解释与创新
研究特定的复合临床严重程度指标并不常见,但与较高的死亡风险相关。皮疹和症状出现时间与综合严重程度指标显示出最强的调整相关性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为制定更准确的临床严重程度指标提供信息,并改善对苏丹东部登革热患者的风险评估。他们还强调了监测特定临床标志物对于早期识别重症病例的重要性。
局限与证据强度
预测性能中等,研究结果需要使用具有更详细的临床和实验室信息的独立数据集进行确认。该研究依赖于常规的 监测 数据,其完整性和准确性可能存在局限性。

Predicting Dengue Clinical Severity in Eastern Sudan's 2023 Outbreak: A Comparative Analysis of Statistical and Machine Learning Models Using Routine Surveillance Data

Authors: Fathelrhman EL Guma, Elkhatim Abuelysar, EihabAbdelhai Osman, Bashir ElnourElbashir Mukhtar, Mumdouh M.M. Hassan, Alshaikh A. Shokeralla
Original abstract (English)
Objectives: This study characterized clinical severity patterns during the 2023 dengue outbreak in Al Qadarif State, Eastern Sudan; identified associated factors; compared statistical and machine-learning models; and explored patterns of clinical manifestations using routine surveillance data. Methods: This retrospective observational study included 2,552 dengue case records. The primary outcome was a pragmatic, study-specific composite clinical severity indicator, defined as the documented presence of bleeding, severe bleeding, loss of consciousness, convulsions, or thrombocytopenia.Analyses included descriptive statistics, multivariable logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, multiple correspondence analysis, and hierarchical clustering. Model performance was assessed using a stratified hold-out test set, with uncertainty estimated from 1,000 bootstrap resamples. Results: Overall, 182 patients (7.13%) met the study-specific composite clinical severity indicator.Thirty-two deaths were recorded, giving a case-fatality rate of 1.25%. Mortality was higher among patients with severity markers than among those without them (3.85% vs. 1.05%; relative risk=3.65). Skin rash showed the strongest adjusted association with the composite severity indicator (adjusted OR=6.26, 95% CI: 3.15–12.45; P<0.001), followed by symptom-onset timing (adjusted OR=4.65, 95% CI: 2.41–8.98; P<0.001). XGBoost achieved the numerically highest AUROC (0.765, 95% CI: 0.691–0.837) and the lowest Brier score (0.161, 95% CI: 0.145–0.176). Multiple correspondence analysis suggested hemorrhagic and neurological/systemic symptom patterns. Conclusion: The study-specific composite clinical severity indicator was uncommon but was associated with a higher risk of death. Skin rash and symptom-onset timing showed the strongest adjusted associations with the study-specific composite clinical severity indicator. Predictive performance was moderate, and the findings require confirmation using independent datasets with more detailed clinical and laboratory information.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to characterize clinical severity patterns during the 2023 dengue outbreak in Eastern Sudan and identify associated factors to improve understanding of dengue disease progression.
Design and population
A retrospective observational study was conducted using 2,552 dengue case records from Al Qadarif State, Eastern Sudan, during the 2023 outbreak. The study focused on patients with documented clinical manifestations.
Core methods
The study used routine surveillance data to define a pragmatic, study-specific composite clinical severity indicator. Analyses included descriptive statistics, multivariable logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, multiple correspondence analysis, and hierarchical clustering.
Main results
Overall, 182 patients (7.13%) met the study-specific composite clinical severity indicator. Thirty-two deaths were recorded, giving a case-fatality rate of 1.25%. Mortality was higher among patients with severity markers than among those without them (3.85% vs. 1.05%; relative risk=3.65).
Interpretation and novelty
The study-specific composite clinical severity indicator was uncommon but was associated with a higher risk of death. Skin rash and symptom-onset timing showed the strongest adjusted associations with the composite severity indicator.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more accurate clinical severity indicators and improve risk assessment for dengue patients in Eastern Sudan. They also highlight the importance of monitoring specific clinical markers for early identification of severe cases.
Limitations and evidence strength
Predictive performance was moderate, and the findings require confirmation using independent datasets with more detailed clinical and laboratory information. The study relied on routine surveillance data, which may have limitations in completeness and accuracy.

厄瓜多尔2011–2024年登革热住院率海拔上升与局部热异常:一项全国生态学研究

Local thermal anomalies and the altitudinal rise of dengue hospitalisation rates in Ecuador, 2011–2024: a national ecological study
作者： Jaime Angamarca-Iguago, Jaen Cagua-Ordoñez, Juan Marcos Parise-Vasco, Daniel Simancas-Racines
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
随着气温升高,登革热正在超出其传统的低地范围,可能使Aedes aegypti在更高海拔地区建立种群。
设计与对象
厄瓜多尔海拔超过 4500 米,提供了自然海拔实验,但缺乏基于医院的关于安第斯登革热热驱动因素的证据。
核心方法
我们使用厄瓜多尔医院出院登记系统(INEC)对2011–2024年期间(标准化的市镇编码时期)进行了全国生态学时间序列分析,按市镇级别进行,通过ICD-10代码A90、A91和A97识别登革热住院病例。
主要结果
在居住州可解决的 74,601 例登革热住院病例中(死亡 232 例,病死率 0.31%),其中 68 个州海拔 1500 m 以上,其中海拔超过 3000 m。
解释与创新
安第斯地区居民登革热住院率的上升与当地历史基线的正偏差(而不是绝对温度)相关,但幅度不大,这在很大程度上取决于 2023 年至 2024 年的流行情况。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持加强高地设施中的登革热识别,并表明当地的热异常可能会影响高海拔地区的登革热住院率。
局限与证据强度
The national hospitalisation rate rose from 29.7 to 77.0 per 100,000 and the Andean rate approximately 7-fold (1.9 to 12.8).

Local thermal anomalies and the altitudinal rise of dengue hospitalisation rates in Ecuador, 2011–2024: a national ecological study

Authors: Jaime Angamarca-Iguago, Jaen Cagua-Ordoñez, Juan Marcos Parise-Vasco, Daniel Simancas-Racines
Original abstract (English)
Introduction Dengue is expanding beyond its traditional lowland range as rising temperatures may enable Aedes aegypti establishment at progressively higher altitudes. Ecuador, spanning sea level to above 4500 m, offers a natural altitudinal experiment, yet hospital-based evidence on the thermal drivers of Andean dengue is lacking. Methods We conducted a national ecological time-series analysis at the canton level using Ecuador's hospital discharge registry (INEC) for 2011–2024, the period with standardised canton coding, identifying dengue hospitalisations by ICD-10 codes A90, A91 and A97. Monthly land surface temperature, precipitation and vegetation index were derived from MODIS and GPM products. A distributed lag non-linear model estimated the temperature–hospitalisation association; a quasi-Poisson model restricted to Andean cantons (≥1500 m), with canton, calendar-month and year fixed effects, tested whether thermal anomalies above each canton's 2007–2015 baseline were associated with hospitalisation rates. Results Among 74,601 dengue hospitalisations with a resolvable canton of residence (232 deaths; case fatality 0.31%), dengue was recorded in 68 cantons above 1500 m, including altitudes exceeding 3000 m. The national hospitalisation rate rose from 29.7 to 77.0 per 100,000 and the Andean rate approximately 7-fold (1.9 to 12.8). The minimum hospitalisation temperature was 18.4°C nationally and 18.9°C in Andean cantons. A +1°C anomaly versus zero was associated with a 1.07-fold increase in hospitalisation rates (95% CI 1.02–1.13; p = 0.012). Without canton fixed effects, the same contrast yielded 1.21 (1.07–1.36); the within-canton association fell to 1.04 (1.00–1.08) when the 2024 epidemic year was excluded. Discussion Positive deviations from local historical baselines, rather than absolute temperature, were associated with rising dengue hospitalisation rates among Andean residents, by a modest margin that depends materially on the 2023–2024 epidemic. In-hospital case fatality was nearly twice as high in low Andean cantons (0.62%, 95% CI 0.20–1.45; five deaths) as on the coast (0.33%), a non-significant difference that nonetheless supports strengthening dengue recognition in highland facilities.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue is expanding beyond its traditional lowland range as rising temperatures may enable Aedes aegypti establishment at progressively higher altitudes.
Design and population
Ecuador, spanning sea level to above 4500 m, offers a natural altitudinal experiment, yet hospital-based evidence on the thermal drivers of Andean dengue is lacking.
Core methods
We conducted a national ecological time-series analysis at the canton level using Ecuador's hospital discharge registry (INEC) for 2011–2024, the period with standardised canton coding, identifying dengue hospitalisations by ICD-10 codes A90, A91 and A97.
Main results
Among 74,601 dengue hospitalisations with a resolvable canton of residence (232 deaths; case fatality 0.31%), dengue was recorded in 68 cantons above 1500 m, including altitudes exceeding 3000 m.
Interpretation and novelty
Positive deviations from local historical baselines, rather than absolute temperature, were associated with rising dengue hospitalisation rates among Andean residents, by a modest margin that depends materially on the 2023–2024 epidemic.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support strengthening dengue recognition in highland facilities and suggest that local thermal anomalies may influence dengue hospitalisation rates in higher altitude regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The national hospitalisation rate rose from 29.7 to 77.0 per 100,000 and the Andean rate approximately 7-fold (1.9 to 12.8).

新生儿登革热感染在流行地区可能模拟晚发性新生儿脓毒症:一个病例报告系列及一个关于抗菌药物管理的诊断框架提案

Neonatal Dengue Infection Mimicking Late-Onset Neonatal Sepsis in Endemic Regions: A Case Report Series and a Proposed Diagnostic Framework for Antimicrobial Stewardship
作者： Andrés Felipe Arias, Camilo Acosta-Parada, Yaimy Valencia, Maria Paula Guerrero, Catalina Jaramillo, Andrés Arias-Sanchez
摘要中文翻译新生儿登革热感染可能在流行地区模拟晚发性新生儿脓毒症(LOS),导致误诊和不必要的抗生素暴露。我们描述了一组在登革热流行地区被收治于三级转诊医院的新生儿病例,这些新生儿疑似患有LOS,接受了标准的脓毒症评估,包括血液培养、炎症标志物和临床评估,并随后通过VIDAS®平台上的ELFA方法检测了抗-登革病毒 IgM以确定登革热感染。所有患者均表现为发热和血液学异常,特别是血小板减少和/或白细胞减少,而血液和尿液培养结果均为阴性,降钙素原水平低或正常。所有病例的登革热IgM均为阳性,支持新生儿登革热感染的诊断。在确诊后,当适用时停用抗生素,所有新生儿在出院时均表现出良好的临床结局,无并发症。这些发现强调登革热可能是流行地区LOS的一个被低估的鉴别诊断诊断,并建议在疑似脓毒症的新生儿中考虑进行虫媒病毒检测,尤其是在培养结果为阴性且炎症标志物不提示细菌感染的情况下。早期识别有助于减少不必要的抗菌药物暴露,并可能支持抗菌药物管理的努力。

问题与背景

Neonatal Dengue Infection Mimicking Late-Onset Neonatal Sepsis in Endemic Regions: A Case Report Series and a Proposed Diagnostic Framework for Antimicrobial Stewardship

Authors: Andrés Felipe Arias, Camilo Acosta-Parada, Yaimy Valencia, Maria Paula Guerrero, Catalina Jaramillo, Andrés Arias-Sanchez
Original abstract (English)
Neonatal dengue infection may mimic late-onset neonatal sepsis (LOS) in endemic regions, leading to misdiagnosis and unnecessary antibiotic exposure. We describe a case series of neonates admitted to a tertiary referral hospital in a dengue-endemic area with suspected LOS who underwent standard sepsis evaluation, including blood cultures, inflammatory markers, and clinical assessment, and were subsequently tested for dengue infection by anti-dengue virus IgM using ELFA methodology on the VIDAS® platform. All patients presented with fever and hematologic abnormalities, particularly thrombocytopenia and/or leukopenia, while blood and urine cultures remained negative and procalcitonin levels were low or normal. Dengue IgM was positive in all cases, supporting the diagnosis of neonatal dengue infection. After diagnostic confirmation, antibiotics were discontinued when applicable, and all neonates had favorable clinical outcomes without complications at discharge. These findings highlight dengue as a potentially underrecognized differential diagnosis of LOS in endemic settings and suggest that arboviral testing may be considered in the diagnostic evaluation of neonates with suspected sepsis, particularly when cultures are negative and inflammatory markers are not suggestive of bacterial infection. Early recognition may help reduce unnecessary antimicrobial exposure and could support antimicrobial stewardship efforts.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Neonatal dengue infection may mimic late-onset neonatal sepsis (LOS) in endemic regions, leading to misdiagnosis and unnecessary antibiotic exposure.
Design and population
We describe a case series of neonates admitted to a tertiary referral hospital in a dengue-endemic area with suspected LOS who underwent standard sepsis evaluation, including blood cultures, inflammatory markers, and clinical assessment, and were subsequently tested for dengue infection by anti-dengue virus IgM using ELFA methodology on the VIDAS® platform.
Core methods
All patients presented with fever and hematologic abnormalities, particularly thrombocytopenia and/or leukopenia, while blood and urine cultures remained negative and procalcitonin levels were low or normal.
Main results
Dengue IgM was positive in all cases, supporting the diagnosis of neonatal dengue infection.
Interpretation and novelty
After diagnostic confirmation, antibiotics were discontinued when applicable, and all neonates had favorable clinical outcomes without complications at discharge.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight dengue as a potentially underrecognized differential diagnosis of LOS in endemic settings and suggest that arboviral testing may be considered in the diagnostic evaluation of neonates with suspected sepsis, particularly when cultures are negative and inflammatory markers are not suggestive of bacterial infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
Early recognition may help reduce unnecessary antimicrobial exposure and could support antimicrobial stewardship efforts.

基于自适应门控融合的多模态框架,用于识别 YouTube 上登革出血热信息传播的影响者

An Adaptive Gated Fusion-based Multimodal Framework for Influential Actor Identification in Dengue Haemorrhagic Fever Information Dissemination on YouTube
作者： Ayu Eka Lestari, Wiwik Anggraeni
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热出血热仍然是印度尼西亚的重要公共卫生问题,YouTube已成为健康信息传播的主要渠道。识别有影响力的人物对于公共卫生传播至关重要,但现有的社交网络分析主要依赖文本,忽略了诸如视频缩略图等视觉信号。
设计与对象
本研究使用了2021年至2025年间从1129个收集的频道(语言过滤后保留1084个)中获取的5365个视频的33233条印尼语评论,进行了分层五折交叉验证的消融研究。
核心方法
通过IndoBERT提取文本特征,通过ResNet-50提取视觉特征,然后按维度自适应融合,按通道聚合,并用于构建基于内容的共受众网络,通过PageRank进行分析。
主要结果
融合表示方法达到了79.52百分比的准确率和78.42百分比的宏F1值,优于文本和视觉基线。KOMPASTV、Kata Dokter和Halodoc成为最具影响力的行动者。
解释与创新
本研究引入了一种结合文本和视觉特征的多模态框架,用于识别YouTube上登革热出血热信息传播中的有影响力人物,展示了其优于单模态方法的性能。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以通过识别在YouTube上传播登革热出血热信息的关键影响者,为有针对性的公共卫生传播策略提供信息,从而潜在提高健康信息的传播范围和效果。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于印尼语的YouTube评论,并依赖于自我报告数据,这可能引入偏差。由于依赖单一数据源且缺乏生物学或临床验证,证据强度中等。

An Adaptive Gated Fusion-based Multimodal Framework for Influential Actor Identification in Dengue Haemorrhagic Fever Information Dissemination on YouTube

Authors: Ayu Eka Lestari, Wiwik Anggraeni
Original abstract (English)
Dengue hemorrhagic fever remains a critical public health concern in Indonesia, and YouTube has become a major channel for health information.Identifying influential actors is essential for public health communication, yet existing social network analysis relies on text and ignores visual signals such as video thumbnails.This study proposes a multimodal framework based on Adaptive Gated Fusion that combines text and visual features without any audio modality.Textual features from IndoBERT and visual features from ResNet-50 are fused adaptively per dimension, aggregated per channel, and used to build a content-based co-audience network analysed with PageRank.Using 33,233 Indonesian comments from 5,365 videos across 1,129 collected channels (1,084 retained after language filtering) over 2021 to 2025, an ablation study with stratified five-fold cross-validation shows the fused representation reaches 79.52 percent accuracy and 78.42 percent macro F1, outperforming text and visual baselines.KOMPASTV, Kata Dokter, and Halodoc emerged as the most influential actors.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue hemorrhagic fever remains a critical public health concern in Indonesia, and YouTube has become a major channel for health information. Identifying influential actors is essential for public health communication, yet existing social network analysis relies on text and ignores visual signals such as video thumbnails.
Design and population
This study used an ablation study with stratified five-fold cross-validation on 33,233 Indonesian comments from 5,365 videos across 1,129 collected channels (1,084 retained after language filtering) over 2021 to 2025.
Core methods
Textual features from IndoBERT and visual features from ResNet-50 are fused adaptively per dimension, aggregated per channel, and used to build a content-based co-audience network analysed with PageRank.
Main results
The fused representation reached 79.52 percent accuracy and 78.42 percent macro F1, outperforming text and visual baselines. KOMPASTV, Kata Dokter, and Halodoc emerged as the most influential actors.
Interpretation and novelty
The study introduces a multimodal framework that combines text and visual features for identifying influential actors in dengue hemorrhagic fever information dissemination on YouTube, demonstrating improved performance over single-modal approaches.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted public health communication strategies by identifying key influencers on YouTube who disseminate information about dengue hemorrhagic fever, potentially improving the reach and effectiveness of health messaging.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to YouTube comments in Indonesian and relies on self-reported data, which may introduce bias. The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on a single data source and the absence of biological or clinical validation.

环境BPA暴露促进黄病毒复制并延长蚊虫寿命。

Environmental BPA exposure promotes flavivirus replication and extends mosquito lifespan.
作者： Ying Zeng, Fan Yang, Tong Li, Yalan Huang, Tengyingzi Liu, Xiaolu Shi, Xiaoping Xiao
摘要中文翻译环境内分泌干扰化学物质(EDCs)暴露普遍存在,但其对节肢动物传播病毒传播的影响仍不明确。在这里,我们发现双酚A(BPA),一种常见的EDC,通过调节宿主氧化应激反应,在蚊虫Aedes aegypti中增强了寨卡病毒(ZIKV)和登革病毒(DENV)的复制。通过对九种EDCs的系统筛选,BPA和二氯二苯三氯乙烷(DDT)显著促进了病毒复制,其中BPA表现出最强的效果。慢性幼虫期BPA暴露导致其在成虫中残留积累,并增加对病毒感染的易感性。转录组学和生化分析显示,BPA上调抗氧化酶——超氧化物歧化酶(SOD)和过氧化氢酶(CAT)——并减少脂质过氧化,从而缓解氧化应激,创造有利于病毒复制的细胞环境。此外,BPA延长了ZIKV感染蚊虫的寿命,可能延长感染蚊虫传播病毒的时间,尽管这一点仍需直接验证。我们的研究揭示了环境BPA暴露如何通过蚊虫中的氧化还原调节影响黄病毒传播的动态,强调了在评估节肢动物传播病毒风险时纳入污染物影响的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
环境内分泌干扰化学物质(EDCs)暴露普遍存在,但其对节肢动物传播病毒传播的影响仍不明确。
设计与对象
在这里,我们发现双酚A(BPA),一种常见的EDC,通过调节宿主氧化应激反应,在蚊虫Aedes aegypti中增强了寨卡病毒(ZIKV)和登革病毒(DENV)的复制。
核心方法
通过对九种EDCs的系统筛选,BPA和二氯二苯三氯乙烷(DDT)显著促进了病毒复制,其中BPA表现出最强的效果。
主要结果
慢性幼虫期BPA暴露导致其在成虫中残留积累,并增加对病毒感染的易感性。
解释与创新
转录组学和生化分析显示,BPA上调抗氧化酶——超氧化物歧化酶(SOD)和过氧化氢酶(CAT)——并减少脂质过氧化,从而缓解氧化应激,创造有利于病毒复制的细胞环境。
科研与公卫意义
此外,BPA延长了ZIKV感染蚊虫的寿命,可能延长感染蚊虫传播病毒的时间,尽管这一点仍需直接验证。
局限与证据强度
我们的研究揭示了环境BPA暴露如何通过蚊虫中的氧化还原调节影响黄病毒传播的动态,强调了在评估节肢动物传播病毒风险时纳入污染物影响的必要性。

Environmental BPA exposure promotes flavivirus replication and extends mosquito lifespan.

Authors: Ying Zeng, Fan Yang, Tong Li, Yalan Huang, Tengyingzi Liu, Xiaolu Shi, Xiaoping Xiao
Original abstract (English)
Environmental exposure to endocrine-disrupting chemicals (EDCs) is widespread, yet their influence on arthropod-borne virus transmission remains poorly understood. Here, we show that bisphenol A (BPA), a common EDC, enhances the replication of Zika virus (ZIKV) and Dengue virus (DENV) in the mosquito Aedes aegypti by modulating host oxidative stress responses. Through systematic screening of nine EDCs, BPA and dichlorodiphenyltrichloroethane (DDT), significantly promoted viral replication, with BPA exhibiting the strongest effect. Chronic larval exposure to BPA led to its residual accumulation in adults and increased susceptibility to viral infection. Transcriptomic and biochemical analyses revealed that BPA upregulates antioxidant enzymes-superoxide dismutase (SOD) and catalase (CAT)-and reduces lipid peroxidation, thereby alleviating oxidative stress and creating a cellular environment conducive to viral replication. Furthermore, BPA extended the lifespan of ZIKV-infected mosquitoes, potentially extending the period during which infected mosquitoes could transmit virus, although this remains to be directly demonstrated. Our findings uncover a mechanism by which environmental BPA exposure may influence flavivirus transmission dynamics through redox modulation in mosquitoes, highlighting the need to incorporate pollutant impacts into arboviral risk assessments.
View research seven-element analysis

Environmental exposure to endocrine-disrupting chemicals (EDCs) is widespread, yet their influence on arthropod-borne virus transmission remains poorly understood.
Design and population
Here, we show that bisphenol A (BPA), a common EDC, enhances the replication of Zika virus (ZIKV) and Dengue virus (DENV) in the mosquito Aedes aegypti by modulating host oxidative stress responses.

Interpretation and novelty

基于社区的健康教育改善登革出血热预防知识:来自印度尼西亚一项干预前后的证据

Community-based health education improves knowledge of dengue hemorrhagic fever prevention: evidence from a pre–post intervention in indonesia
作者： Netty Budi Lestari, Yuli Peristiowati, Suriani Bahrun, Dian Meiliani Yulis
摘要中文翻译背景:登革出血热(DHF)仍然是印度尼西亚公共卫生的主要挑战,需要有效的基于社区的预防策略。健康教育被认为是提高公众意识和促进可持续登革热预防实践的关键干预措施。本研究旨在评估一项基于社区的健康教育计划在改善UPTD Puskesmas Mekar Sari工作区域居民对DHF预防知识方面的有效性。方法:在37名社区成员中开展了一项基于社区的前实验研究,采用单组前后测设计。干预包括通过讲座、互动讨论、演示和强调PSN 3M Plus策略的教育传单进行的90分钟健康教育课程。参与者在干预前后完成了相同的知识问卷。使用描述性统计方法总结知识得分的变化。结果:干预前,70.3%的参与者知识水平较差,29.7%的知识水平一般,而没有人达到良好的知识水平。干预后,所有参与者(100%)均达到良好的知识水平。平均知识得分从8.00提高到12.89,平均提高了4.89分,表明参与者对登革热(传播)、症状和预防的理解有了显著增强。结论:基于社区的健康教育有效提高了DHF预防知识,并加强了社区对PSN 3M Plus策略的认识。将定期教育活动整合到初级卫生保健计划中可能增强社区参与度,并有助于可持续的登革热预防工作。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革出血热(DHF)仍然是印度尼西亚公共卫生的主要挑战,需要有效的基于社区的预防策略。
设计与对象
在37名社区成员中开展了一项基于社区的前实验研究,采用单组前后测设计。
核心方法
干预包括通过讲座、互动讨论、演示和强调PSN 3M Plus策略的教育传单进行的90分钟健康教育课程。参与者在干预前后完成了相同的知识问卷。使用描述性统计方法总结知识得分的变化。
主要结果
干预前,70.3%的参与者知识水平较差,29.7%的知识水平一般,而没有人达到良好的知识水平。干预后,所有参与者(100%)均达到良好的知识水平。平均知识得分从8.00提高到12.89,平均提高了4.89分,表明参与者对登革热(传播)、症状和预防的理解有了显著增强。
解释与创新
基于社区的健康教育有效提高了DHF预防知识,并加强了社区对PSN 3M Plus策略的认识。
科研与公卫意义
将定期教育活动整合到初级卫生保健计划中可能增强社区参与度,并有助于可持续的登革热预防工作。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告知识改善的长期可持续性或自我报告问卷数据中的潜在偏倚。由于样本量有限且缺乏对照组,证据强度为中等。

Community-based health education improves knowledge of dengue hemorrhagic fever prevention: evidence from a pre–post intervention in indonesia

Authors: Netty Budi Lestari, Yuli Peristiowati, Suriani Bahrun, Dian Meiliani Yulis
Original abstract (English)
Background: Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever (DHF) remains a major public health challenge in Indonesia, requiring effective community-based preventive strategies. Health education is considered a key intervention for improving public awareness and promoting sustainable dengue prevention practices. This study aimed to evaluate the effectiveness of a community health education program in improving knowledge regarding DHF prevention among residents in the working area of UPTD Puskesmas Mekar Sari. Methods: A community-based pre-experimental study using a one-group pre-test and post-test design was conducted among 37 community members. The intervention consisted of a 90-minute health education session delivered through lectures, interactive discussions, demonstrations, and educational leaflets emphasizing the PSN 3M Plus strategy. Participants completed identical knowledge questionnaires before and immediately after the intervention. Descriptive statistics were used to summarize changes in knowledge scores. Results: Before the intervention, 70.3% of participants had poor knowledge and 29.7% had fair knowledge, while none achieved a good knowledge level. Following the intervention, all participants (100%) attained the good knowledge category. The mean knowledge score increased from 8.00 to 12.89, with an average improvement of 4.89 points, indicating a substantial enhancement in participants' understanding of dengue transmission, symptoms, and prevention. Conclusion: Community-based health education effectively improved knowledge of DHF prevention and reinforced community awareness of the PSN 3M Plus strategy. Integrating regular educational activities into primary healthcare programs may strengthen community participation and contribute to sustainable dengue prevention efforts.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever (DHF) remains a major public health challenge in Indonesia, requiring effective community-based preventive strategies.
Design and population
A community-based pre-experimental study using a one-group pre-test and post-test design was conducted among 37 community members.
Core methods
The intervention consisted of a 90-minute health education session delivered through lectures, interactive discussions, demonstrations, and educational leaflets emphasizing the PSN 3M Plus strategy. Participants completed identical knowledge questionnaires before and immediately after the intervention. Descriptive statistics were used to summarize changes in knowledge scores.
Main results
Before the intervention, 70.3% of participants had poor knowledge and 29.7% had fair knowledge, while none achieved a good knowledge level. Following the intervention, all participants (100%) attained the good knowledge category. The mean knowledge score increased from 8.00 to 12.89, with an average improvement of 4.89 points, indicating a substantial enhancement in participants' understanding of dengue transmission, symptoms, and prevention.
Interpretation and novelty
Community-based health education effectively improved knowledge of DHF prevention and reinforced community awareness of the PSN 3M Plus strategy.
Scientific and public-health significance
Integrating regular educational activities into primary healthcare programs may strengthen community participation and contribute to sustainable dengue prevention efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on the long-term sustainability of the knowledge improvement or potential biases in the self-reported questionnaire data. The evidence strength is moderate due to the limited sample size and lack of control group.

利用空间自相关和环境不一致分析探讨也门选定省份登革热攻击率的环境驱动因素和时空异质性

Environmental drivers and spatiotemporal heterogeneity of dengue attack rates across selected Yemeni governorates using spatial autocorrelation and environmental discordance analysis
作者： Ibrahim Ahmed Ahmed Alhothily, Agus Naba, Magdah Abdulwali Mohammed Al-Shaibah, Nazri Che Dom
摘要中文翻译登革热对环境具有敏感性,但在冲突影响的环境中,由于监测能力可能不一致,广泛环境条件可能无法始终与报告的疾病负担相对应。本生态纵向研究分析了2020年至2024年间也门十个省份的环境条件与年度报告登革热攻击率之间的时空关系。年度报告的登革热攻击率通过时间排名、轨迹分类、探索性登革热不稳定性指数、斯皮尔曼等级相关和肯德尔一致性系数进行标准化和评估。环境条件通过植被、地表水、地表温度和降水量指标进行表征。全球和局部莫兰统计量用于评估空间依赖性和环境-流行病学空间关联,而主成分分析(PCA)将环境指标整合为一个共同的环境特征梯度,以评估环境-流行病学的一致性和不一致。在报告的登革热负担中观察到显著的空间和时间异质性。最高负担省份每年变化,尽管沙布瓦和阿比安保持了持续较高的相对负担。哈德拉毛省记录了最大的时间不稳定性,其次是马里布和阿比安。然而,省份排名在整个研究期间显示出中等程度的整体一致性(肯德尔W = 0.677,χ2(9) = 30.47,p < 0.001)。全球单变量莫兰III值始终为负,仅在2021年检测到显著的空间分散(I = −0.421,p < 0.05)。登革热与单个环境指标之间的双变量空间关联主要为弱、负相关且时间不一致。主成分分析显示,第一环境成分解释了标准化方差的67.2%,代表了一个整合的环境特征(从较热、较干燥和植被较少的条件到较冷、较湿润和植被较多的条件的梯度)。在20个高于平均报告登革热攻击率的省份-年观察中,有17个(85%)发生在环境特征低于平均的条件下。这些发现表明也门存在显著的环境-流行病学不一致。仅靠广泛的遥感环境条件不足以解释报告登革热负担的空间和时间分布。在实际操作中,遥感环境指标可以补充报告的登革热通知和当地可用的媒介学和背景数据,以优先考虑需要加强监测和针对性调查的省份。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨也门登革热攻击率的环境驱动因素和时空异质性,强调理解环境条件与疾病负担关系的必要性,特别是在监测可能不一致的冲突影响地区。
设计与对象
在2020年至2024年间,对也门十个省份进行了生态纵向研究,分析环境条件与年度报告登革热攻击率之间的时空关系。
核心方法
年度登革热攻击率通过时间排名、轨迹分类、登革热不稳定性指数、斯皮尔曼等级相关和肯德尔一致性系数进行标准化和评估。环境条件通过植被、地表水、地表温度和降水量指标进行表征。
主要结果
在报告的登革热负担中观察到显著的空间和时间异质性。最高负担省份每年变化,尽管沙布瓦和阿比安保持了持续较高的相对负担。哈德拉毛省记录了最大的时间不稳定性,其次是马里布和阿比安。
解释与创新
这些发现表明也门存在显著的环境-流行病学不一致,表明仅靠广泛的环境条件不足以解释登革热负担的分布。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果表明,遥感环境指标可以补充报告的登革热通知和当地可用数据,以优先考虑需要加强监测和针对性调查的省份。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其依赖于报告的登革热攻击率,这些数据可能因监测能力的差异而存在变异性。证据强度中等,因为这些发现强调了不一致,但并未建立直接的因果关系。

Environmental drivers and spatiotemporal heterogeneity of dengue attack rates across selected Yemeni governorates using spatial autocorrelation and environmental discordance analysis

Authors: Ibrahim Ahmed Ahmed Alhothily, Agus Naba, Magdah Abdulwali Mohammed Al-Shaibah, Nazri Che Dom
Original abstract (English)
Dengue is environmentally sensitive, yet broad environmental conditions may not consistently correspond with reported disease burden, particularly in conflict-affected settings where surveillance capacity may vary. This ecological longitudinal study examined the spatiotemporal relationships between environmental conditions and annual reported dengue attack rates across ten Yemeni governorates from 2020 to 2024. Annual reported dengue attack rates were standardised and evaluated using temporal rankings, trajectory classifications, an exploratory Dengue Instability Index, Spearman rank correlations and Kendall’s coefficient of concordance. Environmental conditions were characterised using vegetation, surface-water, land-surface-temperature and precipitation indicators. Global and local Moran statistics were used to assess spatial dependence and environmental–epidemiological spatial associations, while principal component analysis (PCA) integrated the environmental indicators into a common environmental-profile gradient for evaluating environmental–epidemiological concordance and discordance. Marked spatial and temporal heterogeneity was observed in reported dengue burden. The highest-burden governorate changed annually, although Shabwah and Abyan maintained persistently elevated relative burdens. Hadramaut recorded the greatest temporal instability, followed by Marib and Abyan. Governorate rankings nevertheless demonstrated moderate overall concordance across the study period (Kendall’s W = 0.677, χ2(9) = 30.47, p < 0.001). Global univariate Moran’s III values were consistently negative, with significant spatial dispersion detected only in 2021 ( I = − 0.421, p < 0.05). Bivariate spatial associations between dengue and individual environmental indicators were predominantly weak, negative and temporally inconsistent. Principal component analysis showed that the first environmental component explained 67.2% of the standardised variance and represented an integrated environmental profile (a gradient from hotter, drier and less vegetated to cooler, wetter and more vegetated conditions). Among 20 governorate–year observations with above-mean reported dengue attack rates, 17 (85%) occurred under below-mean environmental-profile conditions. These findings demonstrate substantial environmental–epidemiological discordance in Yemen. Broad remotely sensed environmental conditions alone were insufficient to explain the spatial and temporal distribution of reported dengue burden. Operationally, remotely sensed environmental indicators could complement reported dengue notifications and locally available entomological and contextual data to prioritise governorates for intensified surveillance and targeted investigation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to explore the environmental drivers and spatiotemporal heterogeneity of dengue attack rates in Yemen, highlighting the need to understand how environmental conditions relate to disease burden in conflict-affected areas where surveillance may be inconsistent.
Design and population
An ecological longitudinal study was conducted across ten Yemeni governorates from 2020 to 2024, examining the spatiotemporal relationships between environmental conditions and annual reported dengue attack rates.
Core methods
Annual dengue attack rates were standardised and evaluated using temporal rankings, trajectory classifications, a Dengue Instability Index, Spearman rank correlations, and Kendall’s coefficient of concordance. Environmental conditions were characterised using vegetation, surface-water, land-surface-temperature, and precipitation indicators.
Main results
Marked spatial and temporal heterogeneity was observed in reported dengue burden. The highest-burden governorate changed annually, although Shabwah and Abyan maintained persistently elevated relative burdens. Hadramaut recorded the greatest temporal instability, followed by Marib and Abyan.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate substantial environmental–epidemiological discordance in Yemen, indicating that broad environmental conditions alone are insufficient to explain the distribution of dengue burden.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results suggest that remotely sensed environmental indicators could complement reported dengue notifications and locally available data to prioritise governorates for intensified surveillance and targeted investigation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on reported dengue attack rates, which may be subject to variability in surveillance capacity. The evidence strength is moderate, as the findings highlight discordance but do not establish direct causality.

登革热患者血小板计数和血红蛋白浓度检查结果分析:Ngaglik 1卫生中心案例

Gambaran Hasil Pemeriksaan Jumlah Trombosit dan Hematokrit Pada Pasien Dengue Di Puskesmas Ngaglik 1
作者： Sefi Fadila, Rosmita Anggraeni, Tri Dyah Astuti
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
血小板计数和血红蛋白浓度等实验室检测对于登革热患者的诊断和监测至关重要。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
所提供的证据没有明确说明核心实验、分析或统计方法。
主要结果
Temuan menunjukkan bahwa mayoritas pasien demam berdarah adalah dewasa, dengan 91 responden (67,4%), dan 77 responden (57,0%) adalah laki-laki.
解释与创新
Analisis penghitungan trombosit mengungkapkan bahwa sebagian besar pasien mengalami trombositopenia, yaitu 110 peserta (81,5%), sedangkan 25 peserta (18,5%) memiliki nilai normal.
科研与公卫意义
Selanjutnya, analisis kadar hematokrit menunjukkan bahwa sebagian besar pasien memiliki hematokrit dalam kisaran normal, yaitu 68 peserta (50,4%), diikuti oleh 37 peserta (27,4%) dengan hematokrit rendah dan 30 peserta (22,2%) dengan hematokrit tinggi.
局限与证据强度
本研究指出,血小板减少症是登革热患者中最常见的血液学异常,而血红蛋白浓度的波动反映了患者的临床状况和疾病阶段。

Gambaran Hasil Pemeriksaan Jumlah Trombosit dan Hematokrit Pada Pasien Dengue Di Puskesmas Ngaglik 1

Authors: Sefi Fadila, Rosmita Anggraeni, Tri Dyah Astuti
Original abstract (English)
Demam berdarah dengue, yang disebabkan oleh virus dengue, tetap menjadi tantangan kesehatan masyarakat yang signifikan di Indonesia. Tes laboratorium, seperti penghitungan trombosit dan pengukuran hematokrit, sangat penting untuk mendiagnosis dan memantau pasien demam berdarah. Penelitian ini bertujuan untuk menyelidiki hasil pengujian penghitungan trombosit dan hematokrit pada pasien demam berdarah di Puskesmas Ngaglik. Jenis penelitian ini adalah kuantitatif deskriptif. Pengambilan sampel total digunakan untuk mengumpulkan 135 catatan pasien demam berdarah yang diperoleh dari data laboratorium sekunder pada tahun 2025. Analisis data dilakukan menggunakan metode univariat dan disajikan dalam bentuk distribusi frekuensi dan persentase. Temuan menunjukkan bahwa mayoritas pasien demam berdarah adalah dewasa, dengan 91 responden (67,4%), dan 77 responden (57,0%) adalah laki-laki. Analisis penghitungan trombosit mengungkapkan bahwa sebagian besar pasien mengalami trombositopenia, yaitu 110 peserta (81,5%), sedangkan 25 peserta (18,5%) memiliki nilai normal. Selanjutnya, analisis kadar hematokrit menunjukkan bahwa sebagian besar pasien memiliki hematokrit dalam kisaran normal, yaitu 68 peserta (50,4%), diikuti oleh 37 peserta (27,4%) dengan hematokrit rendah dan 30 peserta (22,2%) dengan hematokrit tinggi. Penelitian ini menunjukkan bahwa trombositopenia merupakan gangguan hematologis yang paling umum terjadi pada pasien demam berdarah, sementara fluktuasi kadar hematokrit mencerminkan kondisi klinis dan tahap penyakit.

The supplied evidence does not clearly state the core experimental, analytical, or statistical methods.

Analisis penghitungan trombosit mengungkapkan bahwa sebagian besar pasien mengalami trombositopenia, yaitu 110 peserta (81,5%), sedangkan 25 peserta (18,5%) memiliki nilai normal.
Scientific and public-health significance
Selanjutnya, analisis kadar hematokrit menunjukkan bahwa sebagian besar pasien memiliki hematokrit dalam kisaran normal, yaitu 68 peserta (50,4%), diikuti oleh 37 peserta (27,4%) dengan hematokrit rendah dan 30 peserta (22,2%) dengan hematokrit tinggi.
Limitations and evidence strength
Penelitian ini menunjukkan bahwa trombositopenia merupakan gangguan hematologis yang paling umum terjadi pada pasien demam berdarah, sementara fluktuasi kadar hematokrit mencerminkan kondisi klinis dan tahap penyakit.

登革热(DENV)

DENGUE (DENV)
作者： Ana Madalena dos Santos Silva Brito, Josenildo Freitas Nunes Júnior, Taline de Vasconcelos Carvalho, Jorge Guilherme Ricardo Cardoso, Gustavo Henrique da Silva, Alexandre Muller Zigmundo da Silva Leite, Elayne Rayane Diniz Melo, Risonildo Pereira Cordeiro
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨 登革病毒 的历史背景及其传播,特别是其引入巴西的过程以及埃及伊蚊(Aedes aegypti)在传播中的作用。登革热是一种历史悠久的疾病,其历史记录可追溯至10世纪,且其传播与 传播 的传播及病毒在大陆间的移动密切相关。
设计与对象
本研究是一项历史回顾,重点分析 登革病毒 及其媒介 Aedes aegypti 在不同地区的传播,包括巴西。研究探讨了该病毒在20世纪初传入巴西的情况,以及1980年代在该国观察到的流行病学模式。
核心方法
本研究利用历史记录、流行病学数据和先前研究的参考文献,追踪登革热病毒在巴西的引入和传播过程。研究分析了殖民扩张、海上贸易以及埃及伊蚊传播的动态如何促进病毒的扩散。
主要结果
巴西首个有记录的登革热病例出现在1916年,地点是圣保罗市。然而,该国直到1981年罗赖马州的博阿维斯塔市才出现公认的登革热疫情。到1982年,大约有11,000人感染了登革病毒1型和4型。随后,该病毒于1986年传入里约热内卢州的新伊瓜苏市,标志着其在巴西国内传播进入了一个新的阶段。
解释与创新
作者认为,登革热传入巴西受到历史因素的影响,如殖民扩张和海上贸易,这些因素促进了埃及伊蚊和病毒的传播。本研究为登革热在巴西的传播提供了历史视角。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果突显了历史和生态因素在巴西登革热传播中的作用,为理解该疾病的流行病学模式提供了见解,并强调了埃及伊蚊控制在预防疾病传播中的重要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于历史和流行病学记录,这些记录可能存在缺失或不准确之处。然而,研究结果得到了多个参考文献的支持,包括有记录的病例和历史记载,从而对其结论提供了中等程度的可信度。

DENGUE (DENV)

Authors: Ana Madalena dos Santos Silva Brito, Josenildo Freitas Nunes Júnior, Taline de Vasconcelos Carvalho, Jorge Guilherme Ricardo Cardoso, Gustavo Henrique da Silva, Alexandre Muller Zigmundo da Silva Leite, Elayne Rayane Diniz Melo, Risonildo Pereira Cordeiro
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUÇÃOA dengue é uma doença antiga, com registros históricos que antecedem a consolidação da medicina moderna.O primeiro relato de uma enfermidade clinicamente compatível com a dengue foi identificado em uma enciclopédia médica chinesa datada de 992 d.C., durante a Dinastia Northern Song.Esse compêndio reunia descrições de diversas doenças e sintomas, incluindo quadros caracterizados por febre aguda, dores corporais intensas e associação com "insetos que voam próximos à água", elementos que, à luz do conhecimento atual, são compatíveis com a infecção pelo vírus da dengue e sua transmissão por mosquitos hematófagos (Gubler, 2006).A história da dengue está intrinsecamente relacionada à de seu principal vetor, o mosquito Aedes aegypti, responsável pela disseminação do vírus em diferentes regiões do mundo.A expansão do colonialismo europeu, associada ao crescimento da indústria naval global entre os séculos XVIII e XIX, desempenhou papel central nesse processo.O aumento das rotas marítimas e o surgimento e fortalecimento de cidades portuárias criaram condições ambientais favoráveis à proliferação do vetor, como o acúmulo de água em recipientes artificiais e a intensa circulação de pessoas e mercadorias (Gubler, 2011;Messina et al., 2014).O tráfico transatlântico de pessoas escravizadas conectou a África, considerada a região de origem do Aedes aegypti e dos vírus a ele associados, a diversas partes do mundo, especialmente às Américas (Do Carmo, 2025).O mosquito e o vírus teriam sido introduzidos no continente americano principalmente por meio de ovos transportados em navios negreiros, aderidos a recipientes que acumulavam água durante as longas travessias oceânicas.Nas Américas, a primeira descrição formal do mosquito data de 1762.Contudo, relatos médicos sobre uma das principais doenças transmitidas por sua picada, a dengue, já haviam sido registrados no século XVI, quando a enfermidade era conhecida como "febre quebra-ossos", em referência às intensas dores musculares e articulares que caracterizam o quadro clínico (Gubler, 2006).Em 1780, foi descrito um surto de dengue na cidade da Filadélfia, nos Estados Unidos.No mesmo período, registros de surtos na Ásia e na África confirmaram a ampla disseminação do vetor pelos trópicos, configurando o que é considerado o primeiro surto multicontinental da doença.Ao longo dos séculos seguintes, a dengue consolidou-se como um problema recorrente em regiões tropicais e subtropicais, acompanhando a expansão urbana, o crescimento populacional e as transformações ambientais (Gubler, 2006;Messina et al., 2014).No Brasil, o primeiro registro documentado de dengue ocorreu em 1916, na cidade de São Paulo.Entretanto, apenas décadas depois o país enfrentaria uma epidemia reconhecida da doença.Em 1981, casos de febre foram notificados em Boa Vista, no estado de Roraima, e posteriormente confirmados como dengue por virologistas do Instituto Evandro Chagas (IEC).Entre julho de 1981 e agosto de 1982, aproximadamente 11 mil pessoas foram infectadas pelos sorotipos 1 e 4 do vírus da dengue.Evidências epidemiológicas indicam que o vírus tenha ingressado no território brasileiro por via terrestre, com origem no Caribe e no norte da América do Sul (Teixeira; Barreto; Guerra, 2009; Brasil, 2022).Nesse período inicial, não houve disseminação nacional da doença, uma vez que o Aedes aegypti ainda não se encontrava amplamente disperso no país.Ademais, as ações de controle vetorial implementadas contribuíram para a contenção do surto na região, sem impedir, contudo, a manutenção do vetor em outras áreas do país.No entanto, alguns anos depois, em 30 de abril de 1986, os primeiros casos de dengue foram registrados em Nova Iguaçu, no estado do Rio de Janeiro, marcando o início de um novo ciclo de expansão da doença no território nacional.Diante desse cenário, o Centro de Estudos da Escola Nacional de Saúde Pública da Fundação Oswaldo Cruz (Ensp/Fiocruz) promoveu um debate acadêmico sobre a dengue, posteriormente transcrito e publicado em um dossiê
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the historical context and spread of dengue virus, particularly focusing on its introduction to Brazil and the role of the Aedes aegypti mosquito in its dissemination. Dengue is an ancient disease with historical records dating back to the 10th century, and its transmission has been closely linked to the movement of the vector and the virus across continents.
Design and population
The study is a historical review focusing on the spread of dengue virus and its vector, Aedes aegypti, across different regions, including Brazil. It examines the introduction of the virus to Brazil in the early 20th century and the subsequent epidemiological patterns observed in the 1980s.
Core methods
The study utilized historical records, epidemiological data, and references to prior research to trace the introduction and spread of dengue virus in Brazil. It analyzed the role of colonialism, maritime trade, and the movement of the Aedes aegypti mosquito in facilitating the virus's dissemination.
Main results
The first documented case of dengue in Brazil occurred in 1916 in São Paulo, but the country did not experience a recognized epidemic until 1981 in Boa Vista, Roraima. By 1982, approximately 11,000 people were infected with DENV-1 and DENV-4. The virus was later introduced to Rio de Janeiro in 1986, marking the beginning of a new phase of its spread in the country.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the introduction of dengue to Brazil was influenced by historical factors such as colonial expansion and maritime trade, which facilitated the spread of the Aedes aegypti mosquito and the virus. This study provides a historical perspective on the epidemiology of dengue in Brazil.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the historical and ecological factors that contributed to the spread of dengue in Brazil, offering insights into the disease's epidemiological patterns and the importance of vector control in preventing outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on historical and epidemiological records, which may be subject to gaps or inaccuracies. However, the evidence is supported by multiple references, including documented cases and historical accounts, providing a moderate level of confidence in its conclusions.

pH和温度对登革病毒包膜二聚体的协同效应:来自微秒分子动力学模拟的见解

Synergistic Effects of pH and Temperature on Dengue Virus Envelope Dimers: Insights from Microsecond Molecular Dynamics Simulations
作者： Georcki Ropón‐Palacios, Luís Gustavo Fávaro Crespi, Ingrid Bernardes Santana Martins, Walter Rocchia, Alexandre S. de Araujo, Georcki Ropón-Palacios
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革病毒发病机制依赖于包膜糖蛋白(pE)在低pH诱导下的构象重排。然而,pH和温度如何协同作用以破坏pE的二聚体形式,这是形成融合活性三聚体的关键步骤,目前尚不清楚。在此研究中,我们针对具有最大医学意义的登革病毒血清型,DENV-2和DENV-3,探讨了这种协同效应。使用CHARMM36m力场,在28 °C、37 °C和40 °C下,pH 5、6和7条件下进行了全原子分子动力学模拟,并通过扩展粗粒度分子动力学模拟补充了DENV-2系统在37 °C和pH 5下的模拟,以及DENV-3系统在28 °C和pH 5、37 °C和pH 7下的模拟。我们的模拟结果表明,温度从28 °C升高到37 °C时,在中性pH条件下会扩大构象空间,可能通过调节界面相互作用网络而削弱二聚体的完整性。酸化似乎以血清型特异性方式放大了这一效应:在我们的模拟中,DENV-2需要pH 5,而DENV-3则在pH 6时响应。此外,距离分析和主成分分析揭示了一种不对称的解离路径,称为“补偿性拥抱”,在全原子和粗粒度模型中均观察到。结构分析进一步突出了不同血清型之间组氨酸分布的差异。此外,在37 °C下的相互作用图谱识别出了一些候选的界面接触点,这些接触点可能作为pH敏感的闩锁,维持在pH 7时的稳定性,但在酸化条件下会断裂。总体而言,这些结果表明可能存在一种物理化学触发因素的层次结构,这可能有助于二聚体转化为具有融合能力的单体。这些界面热点以及“补偿性拥抱”构成了结构导向的目标,若在原生病毒颗粒条件下得到验证,可能有助于抗病毒药物和免疫原的合理设计,强调了在药物发现中长时尺度分子动力学模拟作为假设生成工具的价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解pH和温度如何相互作用以破坏登革病毒包膜糖蛋白的二聚体形式,这是病毒发病机制的关键步骤。登革病毒发病机制依赖于包膜糖蛋白(pE)在低pH诱导下的构象重排。
设计与对象
本研究使用全原子分子动力学模拟,针对登革病毒血清型DENV-2和DENV-3,研究它们在不同pH和温度条件下的包膜糖蛋白二聚体形式。模拟在28 °C、37 °C和40 °C下进行,pH为5、6和7。
核心方法
研究人员使用CHARMM36m力场进行了全原子分子动力学模拟,并针对DENV-2和DENV-3在特定条件下进行了扩展粗粒度模拟。通过距离分析和主成分分析来识别解离路径。
主要结果
模拟结果显示,温度从28 °C升高到37 °C时,在中性pH条件下会扩大构象空间,可能通过调节界面相互作用网络而削弱二聚体的完整性。酸化以血清型特异性方式放大了这一效应,DENV-2在pH 5时响应,而DENV-3在pH 6时响应。距离分析和主成分分析揭示了一种不对称的解离路径,称为“补偿性拥抱”,在全原子和粗粒度模型中均观察到。
解释与创新
这些结果表明可能存在一种物理化学触发因素的层次结构,这可能有助于二聚体转化为具有融合能力的单体。结构分析突出了不同血清型之间组氨酸分布的差异。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为抗病毒药物和免疫原的合理设计提供了结构导向的目标,可能有助于开发针对登革病毒的新治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于模拟,未包括在原生病毒颗粒条件下的实验验证。证据强度中等,因为它依赖于计算模型而非直接的生物实验。

Synergistic Effects of pH and Temperature on Dengue Virus Envelope Dimers: Insights from Microsecond Molecular Dynamics Simulations

Authors: Georcki Ropón‐Palacios, Luís Gustavo Fávaro Crespi, Ingrid Bernardes Santana Martins, Walter Rocchia, Alexandre S. de Araujo, Georcki Ropón-Palacios
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Dengue pathogenesis depends on a conformational rearrangement of the envelope glycoprotein (pE) induced by low pH. However, how pH and temperature cooperate to destabilize the dimeric form of the pE, a fundamental step that enables the formation of the fusion-active trimer, remains unclear. Here, we investigate this synergy for Dengue serotypes of greatest medical relevance, DENV-2 and DENV-3. All–atom molecular dynamics simulations were performed on the microsecond scale, at 28 °C, 37 °C, and 40 °C under pH 5, 6, and 7 using CHARMM36m force fields and complemented by extended coarse-grained molecular dynamics simulations for the DENV-2 system at 37 °C and pH 5, and for the DENV-3 system at 28 °C and pH 5, and at 37 °C and pH 7. Our simulations suggest that increasing the temperature from 28 to 37 °C tends to expand the conformational space at neutral pH, potentially weakening dimer integrity through modulation of the interfacial interaction network. Acidification appears to amplify this effect in a serotype–specific manner: in our simulations DENV-2 required pH 5, while DENV-3 responded at pH 6. Additionally, distance and principal component analyses revealed an asymmetric dissociation pathway, termed the “compensatory embrace”, observed in both all-atom and coarse-grained models. Structural analyses further highlighted the differences in histidine distribution between the serotypes. Moreover, interaction mapping at 37 °C identified candidate interfacial contacts that may act as pH-sensitive latches, maintaining stability at pH 7 but breaking upon acidification. Collectively, these results suggest a potential hierarchy of physicochemical triggers that may contribute to the conversion of dimer into fusion-competent monomers. These interfacial hotspots and the “compensatory embrace” constitute structurally informed targets that, pending validation under native virion conditions, may guide the rational design of antivirals and immunogens, underscoring the value of long-time scale molecular dynamics simulations as a hypothesis-generating tool in drug discovery.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand how pH and temperature interact to destabilize the dimeric form of the Dengue virus envelope glycoprotein, a critical step in viral pathogenesis. Dengue pathogenesis depends on a conformational rearrangement of the envelope glycoprotein (pE) induced by low pH.
Design and population
The study used all-atom molecular dynamics simulations on Dengue serotypes DENV-2 and DENV-3, focusing on their envelope glycoprotein dimeric form under varying pH and temperature conditions. Simulations were conducted at 28 °C, 37 °C, and 40 °C under pH 5, 6, and 7.
Core methods
The researchers performed all-atom molecular dynamics simulations using CHARMM36m force fields and extended coarse-grained simulations for DENV-2 and DENV-3 at specific conditions. Distance and principal component analyses were used to identify dissociation pathways.
Main results
Simulations showed that increasing temperature from 28 to 37 °C expanded the conformational space at neutral pH, potentially weakening dimer integrity. Acidification amplified this effect in a serotype-specific manner, with DENV-2 responding at pH 5 and DENV-3 at pH 6. Distance and principal component analyses revealed an asymmetric dissociation pathway, termed the "compensatory embrace", observed in both all-atom and coarse-grained models.
Interpretation and novelty
The results suggest a potential hierarchy of physicochemical triggers that may contribute to the conversion of dimer into fusion-competent monomers. Structural analyses highlighted differences in histidine distribution between the serotypes.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide structurally informed targets for the rational design of antivirals and immunogens, which could aid in the development of new therapeutic strategies against Dengue virus.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on simulations and does not include experimental validation under native virion conditions. The evidence is moderate as it relies on computational models rather than direct biological assays.

德里城市低收入群体中登革热和寨卡病毒的血清流行率:一项横断面和嵌套纵向研究

Dengue and Chikungunya Seroprevalence in an Urban Low-Income Cohort in Delhi: A Cross-Sectional and Nested Longitudinal Study
作者： Nandini Sharma, Saurav Basu, Shivani Rao, Mrunali Zode, Himanshu Bachawandia, Meenakshi Bakshi, Mongjam Meghachandra Singh
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在确定印度德里地区登革病毒(DENV)和寨卡病毒(CHIKV)的血清流行率及其决定因素。该研究的动机是了解这些病毒在城市低收入人群中的负担。
设计与对象
本研究在印度德里东北区的一个城市安置点和贫民窟中开展,针对年龄≥2年的个体进行横断面血清学调查,并嵌套纵向免疫学子研究。本研究共纳入752名参与者,其中女性占56.65%。
核心方法
通过间接酶联免疫吸附试验(ELISA)筛查登革病毒和寨卡病毒的IgG抗体,并对100对IgG血清阳性样本进行纵向分析,通过斑块减少中和试验(μPRNT50)评估随时间变化的功能性中和能力。
主要结果
Of the participants studied, 81.91% (95% CI: 78.97, 84.60; n = 616) were positive for IgG antibodies against DENV, and 28.32% (95% CI: 25.13, 31.69; n = 213) were positive for CHIKV IgG antibodies. μPRNT50 confirmed stable, near-universal CHIKV neutralization (97% baseline, 98% endline). However, DENV neutralizing antibody showed a significant contraction for DENV-3 (79% baseline, 60% endline) and DENV-4 (69% baseline, 60% endline), while DENV-1 (76%) and DENV-2 (77% to 80%) antibodies remained stable.
解释与创新
作者认为,登革病毒和寨卡病毒的高血清流行率表明该人群中存在显著的暴露。他们指出,DENV-3和DENV-4抗体相比DENV-1和DENV-2抗体的差异性下降,表明参与者中存在不同的血清型特异性免疫反应。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,需要在城市低收入地区采取针对性的监测和预防策略,特别是关注影响登革病毒和寨卡病毒传播的环境因素。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其横断面设计,可能无法准确捕捉纵向趋势。由于基于单一时点和嵌套纵向子集,证据强度中等。

Dengue and Chikungunya Seroprevalence in an Urban Low-Income Cohort in Delhi: A Cross-Sectional and Nested Longitudinal Study

Authors: Nandini Sharma, Saurav Basu, Shivani Rao, Mrunali Zode, Himanshu Bachawandia, Meenakshi Bakshi, Mongjam Meghachandra Singh
Original abstract (English)
Objectives: The aim of this study was to ascertain the seroprevalence and determinants of dengue virus (DENV) and chikungunya virus (CHIKV) seropositivity in Delhi, India. Methods: This cross-sectional serosurvey with a nested longitudinal immunological sub-study was conducted in an urban resettlement and slum settlement in the North-East district of Delhi in individuals aged ≥2 years. Dengue and Chikungunya virus IgG antibodies were screened using indirect enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA), and a longitudinal subset of 100 paired IgG-seropositive samples was further characterized via a plaque reduction neutralization test (μPRNT50) to evaluate changes in functional neutralization over time. Results: A total of 752 participants, including 56.65% female participants, were included in the study. Of the participants studied, 81.91% (95% CI: 78.97, 84.60; n = 616) were positive for IgG antibodies against DENV, and 28.32% (95% CI: 25.13, 31.69; n = 213) were positive for CHIKV IgG antibodies. μPRNT50 confirmed stable, near-universal CHIKV neutralization (97% baseline, 98% endline). However, DENV neutralizing antibody showed a significant contraction for DENV-3 (79% baseline, 60% endline) and DENV-4 (69% baseline, 60% endline), while DENV-1 (76%) and DENV-2 (77% to 80%) antibodies remained stable. Individuals living in households with screened windows had significantly lower odds of contracting DENV infection (AOR: 0.36; 95% CI: 0.21, 0.61) and CHIKV infection (AOR: 0.63; 95% CI: 0.43, 0.90) compared with those living in households without screened windows. Further, individuals from households with uncovered containers with stagnant water had 50% higher odds of CHIKV infection than those without such containers (AOR: 1.55; 95% CI: 1.05, 2.29). Conclusions: This study revealed a high burden of DENV and CHIKV exposure in this high-risk urban population. While neutralization remained stable for CHIKV, DENV-1, and DENV-2, waning DENV-3 and DENV-4 antibodies suggest differential serotype-specific immunity in the participants.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to ascertain the seroprevalence and determinants of dengue virus (DENV) and chikungunya virus (CHIKV) seropositivity in Delhi, India. The research was motivated by the need to understand the burden of these viruses in an urban low-income population.
Design and population
This cross-sectional serosurvey with a nested longitudinal immunological sub-study was conducted in an urban resettlement and slum settlement in the North-East district of Delhi among individuals aged ≥2 years. A total of 752 participants, including 56.65% female participants, were included in the study.
Core methods
Dengue and Chikungunya virus IgG antibodies were screened using indirect enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA), and a longitudinal subset of 100 paired IgG-seropositive samples was further characterized via a plaque reduction neutralization test (μPRNT50) to evaluate changes in functional neutralization over time.
Main results
Of the participants studied, 81.91% (95% CI: 78.97, 84.60; n = 616) were positive for IgG antibodies against DENV, and 28.32% (95% CI: 25.13, 31.69; n = 213) were positive for CHIKV IgG antibodies. μPRNT50 confirmed stable, near-universal CHIKV neutralization (97% baseline, 98% endline). However, DENV neutralizing antibody showed a significant contraction for DENV-3 (79% baseline, 60% endline) and DENV-4 (69% baseline, 60% endline), while DENV-1 (76%) and DENV-2 (77% to 80%) antibodies remained stable.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the high seroprevalence of DENV and CHIKV indicates significant exposure in this population. They noted that the differential waning of DENV-3 and DENV-4 antibodies compared to DENV-1 and DENV-2 suggests varying serotype-specific immunity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for targeted surveillance and prevention strategies in urban low-income areas, particularly focusing on environmental factors that influence DENV and CHIKV transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which may not capture longitudinal trends accurately. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on a single time point and a nested longitudinal subset.

孟加拉国库尔纳市三级医院中登革热患者血小板指标按血小板计数分层分析

Platelet Indices in Dengue Patients Stratified by Platelet Count in a Tertiary Care Hospital of Khulna City of Bangladesh
作者： Tasnim Rahman, Abdullah Al Faroque, Mohammad Monzurul Alam Bhuiyan, Toufiq Ahmed, Shaikh Badiuzzaman, Mahmuda Jahan, Suraiya Yasmin
摘要中文翻译背景:登革热是热带国家的主要公共卫生问题,常与血液学异常,特别是血小板减少症相关。血小板指标如平均血小板体积(MPV)、血小板分布宽度(PDW)和血小板压积(PCT)是常规可获得的参数,可能有助于评估疾病严重程度并监测登革热感染患者的病情。目的:本研究的目的是评估孟加拉国库尔纳市三级医院中按血小板计数分层的登革热患者的血小板指标。方法:本横断面研究于2025年8月至12月在孟加拉国库尔纳市的Gazi Medical College Hospital进行。共纳入100例血清学确诊的登革热患者,年龄均大于或等于18岁。根据血小板计数将患者分为不同组别,如每微升少于20,000个、20,000至50,000个、50,000至100,000个、100,000至150,000个以及超过150,000个。使用自动化血液分析仪获取血液学参数,包括血小板计数、MPV、PDW和PCT。数据使用SPSS 21版进行分析。由于数据呈非正态分布,结果以中位数(IQR)表示,组间比较采用Kruskal–Wallis检验。结果:在100名患者中,55.0%为男性,45%为女性。大多数患者年龄在31至40岁之间(28.0%),其次是21至30岁(26.0%)。大多数病例为登革热(79.0%),其次是登革出血热(18.0%)和登革休克综合征(3.0%)。血小板计数的中位数为57,500/cumm(IQR:30,000–89,750)。血小板压积与血小板计数之间存在高度显著关联(p < 0.001),在血小板计数较高的组别中呈现逐渐增加的趋势。相比之下,MPV(p = 0.306)和PDW(p = 0.302)与血小板计数之间未显示出显著关联。结论:在登革热患者中,血小板压积与血小板计数之间表现出强而显著的相关性。血小板压积可能作为监测登革热感染中血小板减少症和疾病进展的有用辅助参数。孟加拉国传染病杂志,2026年6月;13(1):360-366
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热是热带国家的主要公共卫生问题,常与血液学异常,特别是血小板减少症相关。本研究的目的是评估孟加拉国库尔纳市三级医院中按血小板计数分层的登革热患者的血小板指标。
设计与对象
本横断面研究于2025年8月至12月在孟加拉国库尔纳市的Gazi Medical College Hospital进行。共纳入100例血清学确诊的登革热患者,年龄均大于或等于18岁。根据血小板计数将患者分为不同组别,如每微升少于20,000个、20,000至50,000个、50,000至100,000个、100,000至150,000个以及超过150,000个。
核心方法
使用自动化血液分析仪获取血液学参数,包括血小板计数、平均血小板体积(MPV)、血小板分布宽度(PDW)和血小板压积(PCT)。数据使用SPSS 21版进行分析。由于数据呈非正态分布,结果以中位数(IQR)表示,组间比较采用Kruskal–Wallis检验。
主要结果
在100名患者中,55.0%为男性,45%为女性。大多数患者年龄在31至40岁之间(28.0%),其次是21至30岁(26.0%)。大多数病例为登革热(79.0%),其次是登革出血热(18.0%)和登革休克综合征(3.0%)。血小板计数的中位数为57,500/cumm(IQR:30,000–89,750)。血小板压积与血小板计数之间存在高度显著关联(p < 0.001),在血小板计数较高的组别中呈现逐渐增加的趋势。相比之下,MPV(p = 0.306)和PDW(p = 0.302)与血小板计数之间未显示出显著关联。
解释与创新
在血小板指标中,血小板压积在登革热患者中与血小板计数表现出强而显著的相关性。
科研与公卫意义
血小板压积可能作为监测登革热感染中血小板减少症和疾病进展的有用辅助参数。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

Platelet Indices in Dengue Patients Stratified by Platelet Count in a Tertiary Care Hospital of Khulna City of Bangladesh

Authors: Tasnim Rahman, Abdullah Al Faroque, Mohammad Monzurul Alam Bhuiyan, Toufiq Ahmed, Shaikh Badiuzzaman, Mahmuda Jahan, Suraiya Yasmin
Original abstract (English)
Background: Dengue fever is a major public health problem in tropical countries and is commonly associated with hematological abnormalities, particularly thrombocytopenia. Platelet indices such as mean platelet volume (MPV), platelet distribution width (PDW), and plateletcrit (PCT) are routinely available parameters that may help in assessing disease severity and monitoring patients with dengue infection.Objective: The purpose of the present study was to evaluate platelet indices in dengue patients stratified according to platelet count in a tertiary care hospital in Khulna, Bangladesh. Methodology: This cross-sectional study was conducted at Gazi Medical College Hospital, Khulna, Bangladesh from August to December 2025. A total of 100 serologically confirmed dengue patients aged more than or equal to 18 years were enrolled. Patients were categorized into groups based on platelet count, such as less than 20,000/cumm, 20,000 to 50,000/cumm, 50,000 to 100,000/cumm, 100,000 to 150,000/cumm, and more than 150,000/cumm. Hematological parameters, including platelet count, MPV, PDW, and PCT, were obtained using an automated hematology analyzer. Data were analyzed using SPSS version 21. As data were non-normally distributed, results were expressed as median (IQR), and comparisons among groups were performed using the Kruskal–Wallis test. Results: Among the 100 patients, 55.0% were male and 45% female. The majority of the patients were recorded in the 31 to 40 years age group (28.0%), followed closely by the 21 to 30 years age group (26.0%). Most cases were dengue fever (79.0%), followed by dengue hemorrhagic fever (18.0%) and dengue shock syndrome (3.0%). The median platelet count was 57,500/cumm (IQR: 30,000–89,750). Plateletcrit showed a highly significant association with platelet count (p < 0.001), with progressively increasing values across higher platelet count groups. In contrast, MPV (p = 0.306) and PDW (p = 0.302) did not show significant associations with platelet count. Conclusion: Among the platelet indices, plateletcrit demonstrated a strong and significant correlation with platelet count in dengue patients. Plateletcrit may serve as a useful adjunct parameter in monitoring thrombocytopenia and disease progression in dengue infection. Bangladesh Journal of Infectious Diseases, June 2026;13(1):360-366
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever is a major public health problem in tropical countries and is commonly associated with hematological abnormalities, particularly thrombocytopenia. The purpose of the present study was to evaluate platelet indices in dengue patients stratified according to platelet count in a tertiary care hospital in Khulna, Bangladesh.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study was conducted at Gazi Medical College Hospital, Khulna, Bangladesh from August to December 2025. A total of 100 serologically confirmed dengue patients aged more than or equal to 18 years were enrolled. Patients were categorized into groups based on platelet count, such as less than 20,000/cumm, 20,000 to 50,000/cumm, 50,000 to 100,000/cumm, 100,000 to 150,000/cumm, and more than 150,000/cumm.
Core methods
Hematological parameters, including platelet count, mean platelet volume (MPV), platelet distribution width (PDW), and plateletcrit (PCT), were obtained using an automated hematology analyzer. Data were analyzed using SPSS version 21. As data were non-normally distributed, results were expressed as median (IQR), and comparisons among groups were performed using the Kruskal–Wallis test.
Main results
Among the 100 patients, 55.0% were male and 45% female. The majority of the patients were recorded in the 31 to 40 years age group (28.0%), followed closely by the 21 to 30 years age group (26.0%). Most cases were dengue fever (79.0%), followed by dengue hemorrhagic fever (18.0%) and dengue shock syndrome (3.0%). The median platelet count was 57,500/cumm (IQR: 30,000–89,750). Plateletcrit showed a highly significant association with platelet count (p < 0.001), with progressively increasing values across higher platelet count groups. In contrast, MPV (p = 0.306) and PDW (p = 0.302) did not show significant associations with platelet count.
Interpretation and novelty
Among the platelet indices, plateletcrit demonstrated a strong and significant correlation with platelet count in dengue patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
Plateletcrit may serve as a useful adjunct parameter in monitoring thrombocytopenia and disease progression in dengue infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength.

计算屏蔽登革病毒前融合结构的渗透性环状微型肽构象候选物

Computational shielding of dengue viral prefusion with permeable cyclic-minipeptides
作者： Julio Coll
摘要中文翻译针对登革病毒(DENV)的保护性前融合环(FL)结构,已计算生成了具有渗透性的环状微型肽构象候选物。目标是通过将高度保守的FL序列暴露于同源三聚体融合活性构象,防止触发黄病毒融合/感染的pH依赖性解离事件。为此,生成了数百个BoltzGen候选物,针对包含EA保护性和EB-FL序列的前融合最小化DENV包膜AB异二聚体(minEAB)模型。新生成的高亲和力纳摩尔候选物的优势包括可能绕过抗体依赖性增强、低抗原性、易于化学合成以及在环境生理条件下具有强稳定性。此外,通过突变高亲和力母体肽,生成了保留环化、minEAB靶点和高亲和力的渗透性候选物。少数渗透性候选物可用于体外和动物模型实验指导下的登革病毒融合和感染抑制验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
针对登革病毒(DENV)的保护性前融合环(FL)结构,已计算生成了具有渗透性的环状微型肽构象候选物。目标是通过将高度保守的FL序列暴露于同源三聚体融合活性构象,防止触发黄病毒融合/感染的pH依赖性解离事件。
设计与对象
生成了数百个BoltzGen候选物,针对包含EA保护性和EB-FL序列的前融合最小化DENV包膜AB异二聚体(minEAB)模型。
核心方法
通过突变高亲和力母体肽,生成了保留环化、minEAB靶点和高亲和力的渗透性候选物。
主要结果
提供的证据中未报告任何实验或定量结果。
解释与创新
作者强调,这些高亲和力纳摩尔候选物可能绕过抗体依赖性增强,表现出低抗原性,易于合成,并在生理条件下保持稳定,代表了一种新的计算方法用于登革病毒融合抑制。
科研与公卫意义
少数渗透性候选物可用于体外和动物模型实验指导下的登革病毒融合和感染抑制验证。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于计算设计,而无实验验证,因此证据强度较低。

Computational shielding of dengue viral prefusion with permeable cyclic-minipeptides

Authors: Julio Coll
Original abstract (English)
Permeable cyclic-minipeptide-conformer candidates have been computationally cogenerated to shield the protected prefusionloops (FL) of dengue flavivirus (DENV). The objective was to prevent the pH-dependent dissociation events that trigger flavivirus fusion/ infectivity by exposing the FL highlyconserved sequences to homotrimer fusioncompetent conformations. For that, hundreds of BoltzGen candidates were co-generated targeting a prefusion minimized DENV envelope AB heterodimer (minEAB) model including both EA-protective and EB-FL sequences. The advantages of the newly cogenerated top-affinity nanoMolar candidates include possible bypassing of antibody-related enhancements, low antigenicity, easy chemical synthesis, and strong environmentalphysiological stability. Additionally, top-affinity parents were mutated to derive membranepermeable candidates conserving cyclization, minEAB targets and high-affinities. A few membrane-permeable candidates may serve for in vitro and animal-model experimentallyguided validations of inhibition of DENV fusion and infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Permeable cyclic‐minipeptide‐conformer candidates have been computationally cogenerated to shield the protected prefusion loops (FL) of dengue flavivirus (DENV). The objective was to prevent the pH‐dependent dissociation events that trigger flavivirus fusion/infectivity by exposing the highly conserved FL sequences to homotrimer fusion‐competent conformations.
Design and population
Hundreds of BoltzGen candidates were co‐generated targeting a prefusion‐minimized DENV envelope AB heterodimer (minEAB) model that includes both EA‐protective and EB‐FL sequences.
Core methods
Top‐affinity parent peptides were mutated to derive membrane‐permeable candidates while conserving cyclization, minEAB targets and high affinities.
Main results
The supplied evidence does not report any experimental or quantitative results.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors highlight that the top‐affinity nanomolar candidates may bypass antibody‐dependent enhancement, exhibit low antigenicity, be easily synthesized, and remain stable under physiological conditions, representing a novel computational approach to dengue fusion inhibition.
Scientific and public-health significance
A few membrane‐permeable candidates may serve for in vitro and animal‐model experimentally‐guided validations of inhibition of DENV fusion and infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to computational design without experimental validation, so the strength of the evidence is low.

基于外周血单核细胞转录组的登革热相关共病及关键调控基因的计算识别

Computational Identification of Dengue-Associated Comorbidities and Key Regulatory Genes from Peripheral Blood Mononuclear Cell Transcriptomes
作者： Adrita Alam, Rupa Dey, Chaity Paul, Muezz Uddin, A. F. M. Mahbubur Rahman, Sanjoy Kumar Chakravarty, Somlal Das, Md. Humayun Kabir
摘要中文翻译登革热仍然是一个主要的健康问题,登革热中宿主 免疫应答的变异导致临床严重程度的差异。本研究通过计算分析外周血单核细胞(PBMC)转录组,以识别高表达的样本特异性基因、潜在的登革热相关共病以及登革热、登革热出血热、登革热伴血浆渗漏和登革热不伴血浆渗漏之间的关键调控基因。收集、处理并整合了公开可用的RNA-seq数据集。在排除管家基因后,识别出高表达的样本特异性基因,并进行了基因本体(Gene Ontology)、KEGG通路和疾病本体(Disease Ontology)的富集分析。随后利用蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用网络识别与富集疾病术语相关的枢纽基因。分析结果显示,BCL2、MYC、TNF和TYMS是连接所研究的登革热状况与不同疾病类别的关键基因。与白血病相关的疾病术语在登革热和登革热出血热中尤为突出,其中BCL2在两种状况中均被识别,而MYC则在登革热出血热中被额外识别。在登革热伴血浆渗漏中,TNF与风湿性和自身免疫性疾病术语相关。在登革热不伴血浆渗漏中,TNF与风湿性和结核病相关术语相关,而TYMS则与多发性骨髓瘤相关的分子模式相关。这些发现描述了PBMC转录组中与特定状况相关的基因-疾病重叠,并提供了进一步研究的计算候选基因。在这些预测关联被用于诊断或治疗之前,需要进行实验和临床验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热仍然是一个主要的健康问题,登革热中宿主 免疫应答的变异导致临床严重程度的差异。本研究旨在通过计算分析外周血单核细胞(PBMC)转录组,识别关键调控基因和登革热相关共病。
设计与对象
本研究通过计算分析外周血单核细胞(PBMC)转录组,以识别高表达的样本特异性基因、潜在的登革热相关共病以及登革热、登革热出血热、登革热伴血浆渗漏和登革热不伴血浆渗漏之间的关键调控基因。
核心方法
收集、处理并整合了公开可用的RNA-seq数据集。在排除管家基因后,识别出高表达的样本特异性基因,并进行了基因本体(Gene Ontology)、KEGG通路和疾病本体(Disease Ontology)的富集分析。随后利用蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用网络识别与富集疾病术语相关的枢纽基因。
主要结果
分析结果显示,BCL2、MYC、TNF和TYMS是连接所研究的登革热状况与不同疾病类别的关键基因。与白血病相关的疾病术语在登革热和登革热出血热中尤为突出,其中BCL2在两种状况中均被识别,而MYC则在登革热出血热中被额外识别。在登革热伴血浆渗漏中,TNF与风湿性和自身免疫性疾病术语相关。在登革热不伴血浆渗漏中,TNF与风湿性和结核病相关术语相关,而TYMS则与多发性骨髓瘤相关的分子模式相关。
解释与创新
这些发现描述了PBMC转录组中与特定状况相关的基因-疾病重叠,并提供了进一步研究的计算候选基因。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于未来研究登革热疾病进展的分子机制,并有助于开发针对性的诊断或治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于现有RNA-seq数据集的计算分析,这可能引入偏倚或解释上的局限性。由于依赖公开数据且缺乏实验验证,证据强度中等。

Computational Identification of Dengue-Associated Comorbidities and Key Regulatory Genes from Peripheral Blood Mononuclear Cell Transcriptomes

Authors: Adrita Alam, Rupa Dey, Chaity Paul, Muezz Uddin, A. F. M. Mahbubur Rahman, Sanjoy Kumar Chakravarty, Somlal Das, Md. Humayun Kabir
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever remains a major health concern, and variation in host immune responses contributes to differences in clinical severity. This study computationally investigated peripheral blood mononuclear cell (PBMC) transcriptomes to identify highly expressed sample-specific genes, potential dengue-associated comorbidities, and key regulatory genes across dengue fever, dengue haemorrhagic fever, dengue with plasma leakage, and dengue without plasma leakage. Publicly available RNA-seq datasets were collected, processed, and integrated. Highly expressed sample-specific genes were identified after excluding housekeeping genes, followed by Gene Ontology, KEGG pathway, and Disease Ontology enrichment analyses. Protein–protein interaction networks were then used to identify hub genes associated with enriched disease terms. The analyses identified BCL2, MYC, TNF, and TYMS as key genes linking the examined dengue conditions with distinct disease categories. Leukaemia-related disease terms were prominent in dengue fever and dengue haemorrhagic fever, with BCL2 identified in both conditions and MYC additionally identified in dengue haemorrhagic fever. In dengue with plasma leakage, TNF was associated with rheumatic and autoimmune disease terms. In dengue without plasma leakage, TNF was linked with rheumatic and tuberculosis-related terms, while TYMS was associated with multiple myeloma-related molecular patterns. These findings describe condition-specific gene–disease overlaps in PBMC transcriptomes and provide computational candidates for further investigation. Experimental and clinical validation is required before these predicted associations can be considered for diagnostic or therapeutic application.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever remains a major health concern, and variation in host immune responses contributes to differences in clinical severity. This study aimed to identify key regulatory genes and dengue-associated comorbidities through computational analysis of PBMC transcriptomes.
Design and population
The study computationally investigated peripheral blood mononuclear cell (PBMC) transcriptomes to identify highly expressed sample-specific genes, potential dengue-associated comorbidities, and key regulatory genes across dengue fever, dengue haemorrhagic fever, dengue with plasma leakage, and dengue without plasma leakage.
Core methods
Publicly available RNA-seq datasets were collected, processed, and integrated. Highly expressed sample-specific genes were identified after excluding housekeeping genes, followed by Gene Ontology, KEGG pathway, and Disease Ontology enrichment analyses. Protein–protein interaction networks were then used to identify hub genes associated with enriched disease terms.
Main results
The analyses identified BCL2, MYC, TNF, and TYMS as key genes linking the examined dengue conditions with distinct disease categories. Leukaemia-related disease terms were prominent in dengue fever and dengue haemorrhagic fever, with BCL2 identified in both conditions and MYC additionally identified in dengue haemorrhagic fever. In dengue with plasma leakage, TNF was associated with rheumatic and autoimmune disease terms. In dengue without plasma leakage, TNF was linked with rheumatic and tuberculosis-related terms, while TYMS was associated with multiple myeloma-related molecular patterns.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings describe condition-specific gene–disease overlaps in PBMC transcriptomes and provide computational candidates for further investigation.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform future research on the molecular mechanisms underlying dengue disease progression and could contribute to the development of targeted diagnostic or therapeutic strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on computational analysis of existing RNA-seq datasets, which may introduce biases or limitations in interpretation. The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on publicly available data without experimental validation.

菲律宾黎刹公立医院的登革热患者和医疗保健专业人员的经验

Experiences of Dengue Patients and Healthcare Professionals in Public Hospitals of Rizal, Philippines
作者： Emmanuelle Reigne B. Tica
摘要中文翻译登革热是一种困扰菲律宾的疾病,菲律宾黎刹省(Rizal)公共医院中登革热患者和医护人员在应对登革热病例上升时的经历仍需进一步研究。本研究通过采访黎刹省公共医院的医疗专业人员和登革热患者,收集信息以解读和考虑他们的经历,从而提高登革热病例治疗的有效性、效率和质量。本研究采用定性研究方法,通过滚雪球抽样获取了三名登革热患者和三名医护人员的半结构化访谈数据,并采用主题分析法对数据进行综合。研究发现,患者经历了由于人员不足、住宿困难以及疾病对其心理和身体健康造成的长期影响等问题。医护人员则因患者数量众多、与患者沟通困难以及设施不足而面临严重的工作负担。这揭示了各群体的经历以及黎刹省公共医院在医疗方面可能需要改进的方面,例如预算分配、建立更多三级医院、改善医护人员的工作条件、建立更高效的医疗系统等。这与理论框架相一致,展示了系统可用性与个人能力之间的关系,这种关系对患者和医护人员的就医过程产生负面影响。

登革热是一种困扰菲律宾的疾病,菲律宾黎刹省(Rizal)公共医院中登革热患者和医护人员在应对登革热病例上升时的经历仍需进一步研究。

核心方法
本研究采用定性研究方法,通过滚雪球抽样获取了三名登革热患者和三名医护人员的半结构化访谈数据。
主要结果

局限与证据强度
这与理论框架相一致,展示了系统可用性与个人能力之间的关系,这种关系对患者和医护人员的就医过程产生负面影响。

Experiences of Dengue Patients and Healthcare Professionals in Public Hospitals of Rizal, Philippines

Authors: Emmanuelle Reigne B. Tica
Original abstract (English)
Dengue is a disease that plagues the Philippines, and the experiences of dengue patients and healthcare workers in public hospitals in Rizal in response to the rising dengue cases remain to be further investigated. This study seeks to gather information through interviews with healthcare professionals and dengue patients from public hospitals in Rizal, Philippines to interpret and consider their experiences to improve the effectiveness, efficiency, and quality of treating dengue cases. A qualitative study was conducted through semi-structured interviews of three dengue patients and three healthcare workers obtained through snowball sampling. A thematic analysis was used to synthesize the data. It was found that patients had experiences with problems due to understaffing, difficult accommodations, and the lasting effects of the disease on their mental and physical health. Healthcare workers were shown to suffer from severe workload due to the high number of patients, difficulties communicating with patients, and insufficient facilities. This sheds light on the experiences of each group and the aspects of healthcare in public hospitals of Rizal that may be improved, such as budget allocation, creating more tertiary hospitals, improving work conditions of healthcare professionals, more efficient healthcare systems, and more. This aligns with the theoretical framework, showing the relationship between systemic availability and individual capability which negatively affect a patient’s and a healthcare worker’s journey.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue is a disease that plagues the Philippines, and the experiences of dengue patients and healthcare workers in public hospitals in Rizal in response to the rising dengue cases remain to be further investigated.
