巴西登革热流行病学、这种被忽视的热带疾病的总体特征以及疫苗接种作为预防手段

Epidemiologia da dengue no Brasil, características gerais dessa doença tropical negligenciada e a vacinação como forma de prevenção
作者： Milena Bitencourt Santos, Maria Luiza de Oliveira Nogueira, Érika Maria Henriques Monteiro, Thiago César Nascimento, Igor Rosa Meurer
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热是一种被世界卫生组织列为被忽视的热带疾病的虫媒病毒病,对公共卫生有重要影响。
设计与对象
本研究旨在描述登革热的一般情况、疫苗接种作为预防手段的使用,并介绍其在巴西的流行病学状况。
核心方法
本研究为一项文献综述,通过检索2014年至2025年间在PubMed、LILACS和SciELO数据库中发表的文章进行。
主要结果
登革病毒(DENV)属于黄病毒科,具有四个不同的血清型。
解释与创新
该疾病表现出广泛的临床谱系,从无症状形式到严重和致命形式。
科研与公卫意义
通常,应强调支持性治疗、媒介控制与预防措施、及时诊断以及疫苗接种作为补充策略的重要性。
局限与证据强度
Em relação a epidemiologia no Brasil, ressalta-se que em 2024 foram registrados 6,4 milhões de casos prováveis e mais de 5.000 óbitos, em cenário de circulação dos quatro sorotipos, com elevada carga e repercussões clínicas, sociais e econômicas.

Epidemiologia da dengue no Brasil, características gerais dessa doença tropical negligenciada e a vacinação como forma de prevenção

Authors: Milena Bitencourt Santos, Maria Luiza de Oliveira Nogueira, Érika Maria Henriques Monteiro, Thiago César Nascimento, Igor Rosa Meurer
Original abstract (English)
A dengue é uma arbovirose incluída na lista de doenças tropicais negligenciadas da Organização Mundial da Saúde, com impacto relevante para a saúde pública. O presente estudo teve como objetivo descrever os aspectos gerais da dengue, o uso da vacinação como forma de prevenção e apresentar a sua situação epidemiológica no Brasil. Trata-se de uma revisão narrativa da literatura, realizada a partir da busca de artigos publicados entre 2014 e 2025 nas bases de dados PubMed, LILACS e SciELO. O vírus da dengue (DENV) pertence ao gênero Flavivirus e possui quatro sorotipos distintos. A doença apresenta amplo espectro clínico, variando de formas assintomáticas a quadros graves e fatais. Em geral, é importante destacar o papel relevante do tratamento de suporte, das ações de controle vetorial e prevenção, do diagnóstico oportuno e da vacinação como estratégia complementar. Em relação a epidemiologia no Brasil, ressalta-se que em 2024 foram registrados 6,4 milhões de casos prováveis e mais de 5.000 óbitos, em cenário de circulação dos quatro sorotipos, com elevada carga e repercussões clínicas, sociais e econômicas. Experiências com a abordagem One Health (Saúde Única) reforçam a importância da integração entre dimensões humanas, ambientais e vetoriais no enfrentamento da doença. Por fim, evidencia-se que a dengue é uma condição complexa, exigindo manejo clínico adequado, vigilância sensível, controle vetorial, vacinação guiada por critérios epidemiológicos e adoção da abordagem One Health como eixos complementares para reduzir sua carga e orientar políticas sustentáveis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
A dengue é uma arbovirose incluída na lista de doenças tropicais negligenciadas da Organização Mundial da Saúde, com impacto relevante para a saúde pública.
Design and population
O presente estudo teve como objetivo descrever os aspectos gerais da dengue, o uso da vacinação como forma de prevenção e apresentar a sua situação epidemiológica no Brasil.
Core methods
Trata-se de uma revisão narrativa da literatura, realizada a partir da busca de artigos publicados entre 2014 e 2025 nas bases de dados PubMed, LILACS e SciELO.
Main results
O vírus da dengue (DENV) pertence ao gênero Flavivirus e possui quatro sorotipos distintos.
Interpretation and novelty
A doença apresenta amplo espectro clínico, variando de formas assintomáticas a quadros graves e fatais.
Scientific and public-health significance
Em geral, é importante destacar o papel relevante do tratamento de suporte, das ações de controle vetorial e prevenção, do diagnóstico oportuno e da vacinação como estratégia complementar.

B 常规重要学术文献 · 研究B routine priorityAcademic literature · Research

巴西全国城市登革热预测中共享学习的运用:历史、气候与模型权衡的证据

Shared Learning for Nationwide Municipal Dengue Incidence Forecasting in Brazil: Evidence on History, Climate, and Model Trade-Offs
作者： Rhibull Tang
摘要中文翻译城市层面的登革热预测具有挑战性,因为每个时间序列都较短。此外,额外的预测变量并不总是提供有用的信息。我们评估了巴西所有5,570个城市的每月登革热预测,使用了60个滚动目标和12至48个月的历史窗口。我们首先使用一个50个城市的诊断样本测试了历史数据、共享学习与本地学习、额外信息以及神经网络模型的影响。随后进行了全国范围内的内部验证。在诊断样本中,共享门控循环单元(GRU)模型相比城市特定的GRU模型,将综合平均绝对误差(MAE)降低了23.0%–24.5%。气候因素提高了本地LightGBM模型的预测精度(MAE为3.9%–5.1%;RMSE为10.3%–11.5%)。然而,气候因素增加了共享GRU模型的MAE。因此,气候因素的预测价值取决于所使用的模型。在全国范围内,仅使用历史数据的共享GRU模型在每个回溯周期中均具有最低的均方根误差(RMSE)。一个自适应集成模型略微提高了MAE,但增加了RMSE。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决城市层面登革热预测的挑战,该挑战由于每个时间序列的持续时间较短而变得困难。研究旨在评估使用共享学习模型预测巴西全国登革热的有效性。
设计与对象
本研究评估了巴西所有5,570个城市每月的登革热预测。它使用了一个50个城市的诊断样本,测试了包括历史数据、共享学习与本地学习、额外信息以及神经网络模型在内的各种因素,随后进行了全国范围内的内部验证。
核心方法
评估使用了60个滚动目标和12至48个月的历史窗口。研究首先在50个城市的诊断样本上测试了模型,然后进行了全国范围内的内部验证以评估其性能。
主要结果
在诊断样本中,共享门控循环单元(GRU)模型相比城市特定的GRU模型,将综合平均绝对误差(MAE)降低了23.0%–24.5%。然而,气候因素增加了共享GRU模型的MAE。因此,气候因素的预测价值取决于所使用的模型。在全国范围内,仅使用历史数据的共享GRU模型在每个回溯周期中均具有最低的均方根误差(RMSE)。一个自适应集成模型略微提高了MAE,但增加了RMSE。
解释与创新
作者认为,共享GRU模型在与本地模型相比时显示出显著的准确性提升,但气候数据的纳入对不同模型类型的影响各不相同。这一发现强调了在登革热预测中结合气候数据时选择合适模型的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发更准确的登革热预测模型提供信息,这些模型利用城市间的共享学习,可能有助于提高巴西的公共卫生准备能力和资源分配效率。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于对摘要的分析,证据基于内部验证,而没有外部比较。由于缺乏详细的统计分析和诊断样本中可能存在的偏倚,研究结果的可信度中等。

Shared Learning for Nationwide Municipal Dengue Incidence Forecasting in Brazil: Evidence on History, Climate, and Model Trade-Offs

Authors: Rhibull Tang
Original abstract (English)
Municipality-level dengue forecasting is challenging because each time series is short. Additional predictors also do not always provide useful information. We evaluated one-month-ahead monthly incidence across all 5,570 Brazilian municipalities. The evaluation used 60 rolling targets and history windows of 12–48 months. We first used a 50-municipality diagnostic sample to test history, shared versus local learning, additional information, and neural models. We then conducted nationwide internal validation. In the diagnostic sample, the shared gated recurrent unit (GRU) reduced pooled MAE by 23.0%–24.5% compared with municipality-specific GRUs. Climate improved local LightGBM (MAE, 3.9%–5.1%; RMSE, 10.3%–11.5%). However, climate increased the shared GRU’s MAE. The predictive value of climate therefore depended on the model. Nationwide, the history-only shared GRU had the lowest RMSE at every lookback. An adaptive ensemble slightly improved MAE but increased RMSE.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of municipality-level dengue forecasting, which is difficult due to the short duration of each time series. The research sought to evaluate the effectiveness of using shared learning models for predicting dengue incidence across Brazil.
Design and population
The study evaluated one-month-ahead monthly incidence across all 5,570 Brazilian municipalities. It used a 50-municipality diagnostic sample to test various factors, including history, shared versus local learning, additional information, and neural models, followed by nationwide internal validation.
Core methods
The evaluation used 60 rolling targets and history windows of 12–48 months. The study first tested models on a 50-municipality diagnostic sample and then conducted nationwide internal validation to assess their performance.
Main results
In the diagnostic sample, the shared gated recurrent unit (GRU) reduced pooled MAE by 23.0%–24.5% compared with municipality-specific GRUs. However, climate increased the shared GRU’s MAE. The predictive value of climate therefore depended on the model. Nationwide, the history-only shared GRU had the lowest RMSE at every lookback. An adaptive ensemble slightly improved MAE but increased RMSE.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the shared GRU model showed significant improvement in accuracy when compared to local models, but the inclusion of climate data had varying effects depending on the model type. This finding highlights the importance of model selection in incorporating climate data for dengue forecasting.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more accurate dengue forecasting models that leverage shared learning across municipalities, potentially improving public health preparedness and resource allocation in Brazil.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only analysis, and the evidence is based on internal validation without external comparisons. The confidence in the findings is moderate due to the lack of detailed statistical analysis and potential biases in the diagnostic sample.

登革热流行病学和社会人口学视角:米纳斯吉拉斯州的现状分析

A dengue sob uma perspectiva epidemiológica e sociodemográfica: uma análise sobre o estado de Minas Gerais
作者： Maria Cristina Vasconcelos Teixeira
摘要中文翻译巴西登革热流行病学背景是当地和流行病期间的变化,代表了公众的持续发展。我们对米纳斯吉拉斯州登革热流行病学和社会人口学的总体分析进行了客观分析,并与乌贝兰迪亚的市政部门、最近的科学文献证据的一部分进行了研究。本书是有关研究的文献、描述和探索,是在国家和国际基础上实现的。我们的结果是,在过去的一段时间内,我们与社会环境、地区因素以及对萨乌德系统产生了重大影响。结论是米纳斯吉拉斯州长期存在的登革热问题,是流行病学、控制媒介和相互间交流的综合策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了巴西登革热面临的持续挑战,其特点是流行期和流行病 暴发 之间的振荡,旨在分析米纳斯吉拉斯州登革热的流行病学和社会人口学概况,重点是乌贝兰迪亚。
设计与对象
这是一项描述性和探索性的书目研究,使用国家和国际数据库进行,以分析米纳斯吉拉斯州登革热的流行病学和社会人口学概况,重点关注乌贝兰迪亚。
核心方法
该研究采用描述性和探索性的文献研究方法,利用国家和国际数据库收集和分析有关登革热的最新科学证据。
主要结果
结果表明,在一年中的某些时段,登革热的 发病率 较高,与社会环境因素、区域不平等密切相关,并对统一卫生系统产生重大影响。
解释与创新
作者解释说,登革热仍然是米纳斯吉拉斯州的一个重大公共卫生问题,需要采取流行病学 监测、媒介 控制和跨部门行动的综合策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明需要采取综合的 监测 和控制策略,强调解决社会环境因素和区域不平等以减轻登革热对卫生系统影响的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对书目研究的依赖而受到限制,书目研究可能无法捕获所有当前或本地数据;然而,最近科学证据的使用为研究结果提供了适度的可信度。

A dengue sob uma perspectiva epidemiológica e sociodemográfica: uma análise sobre o estado de Minas Gerais

Authors: Maria Cristina Vasconcelos Teixeira
Original abstract (English)
O contexto epidemiológico da dengue no Brasil caracteriza-se por oscilações entre períodos endêmicos e surtos epidêmicos, representando um desafio contínuo à saúde pública. Este estudo tem como objetivo geral analisar o perfil epidemiológico e sociodemográfico da dengue no estado de Minas Gerais, com ênfase no município de Uberlândia, a partir de evidências da literatura científica recente. Trata-se de uma pesquisa bibliográfica, de caráter descritivo e exploratório, realizada em bases de dados nacionais e internacionais. Os resultados indicam elevada incidência da doença em determinados períodos do ano, forte associação com fatores socioambientais, desigualdades regionais e impacto significativo sobre o Sistema Único de Saúde. Conclui-se que a dengue permanece como um importante problema de saúde pública em Minas Gerais, exigindo estratégias integradas de vigilância epidemiológica, controle vetorial e ações intersetoriais.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the continuous challenge of dengue in Brazil, characterized by oscillations between endemic periods and epidemic outbreaks, aiming to analyze the epidemiological and sociodemographic profile of dengue in Minas Gerais, with emphasis on Uberlândia.
Design and population
This is a descriptive and exploratory bibliographic research conducted using national and international databases to analyze the epidemiological and sociodemographic profile of dengue in Minas Gerais, focusing on Uberlândia.
Core methods
The study employed a descriptive and exploratory bibliographic research approach, utilizing national and international databases to gather and analyze recent scientific evidence on dengue.
Main results
The results indicate a high incidence of dengue during certain periods of the year, a strong association with socio-environmental factors, regional inequalities, and a significant impact on the Unified Health System.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that dengue remains a significant public health problem in Minas Gerais, requiring integrated strategies for epidemiological surveillance, vector control, and intersectoral actions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for integrated surveillance and control strategies, highlighting the importance of addressing socio-environmental factors and regional inequalities to mitigate dengue's impact on the health system.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on bibliographic research, which may not capture all current or local data; however, the use of recent scientific evidence provides moderate confidence in the findings.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

发现具有广谱 抗病毒药物 活性的 4'-叠氮基 Fleximer 核苷类似物,可对抗 DENV、EBOV 和其他 RNA 病毒。

Discovery of a 4'-Azido Fleximer Nucleoside Analog With Broad-Spectrum Antiviral Activity Against DENV, EBOV, and Other RNA Viruses.
作者： Kyle A Davis, Ryan L Slack, Thomas R Foulkes, Tereza Frčková, Lenka Tomková, Jessica L Smith, Meareg Amare, Grayson D Pipher, Peter Halfmann, Alec J Hirsch
摘要中文翻译RNA病毒在多个病毒家族中的持续出现和再出现凸显了开发广谱抗病毒药物治疗药物的迫切需求。为应对这一问题,一系列4'-叠氮柔性核苷类似物被合成并评估其对流行/大流行病原体的广谱抗病毒药物活性。其中一种化合物KAD-039,其特征为包含2',3'-二异丁酸-5'-ProTide前药,对黄病毒、丝状病毒和冠状病毒表现出强效的抗病毒药物活性,其EC50值范围为1至10 μM,且细胞毒性极低(CC50 > 50 μM)。对KAD-039的药代动力学评估显示其在狗、猴和人类血浆中具有高稳定性,半衰期范围为19.9至28小时,但在人类肝微粒体中稳定性较差(t1/2 = 6.1分钟)。为帮助阐明KAD-039对这些病毒家族的作用机制,使用几种病毒甲基转移酶的帽/GTP结合口袋中预期的三磷酸活性形式进行了计算分子对接研究,结果显示KAD-039TP在所有病毒MTases中的预测结合亲和力与天然底物相似或更高。此外,KAD-039TP对人类N7甲基转移酶的预测结合亲和力较低,表明其具有较低的宿主毒性。这些结果突显了4'-叠氮柔性核苷类似物作为具有前景的广谱抗病毒药物治疗药物的潜力,可能通过抑制病毒甲基转移酶发挥作用,并支持进一步的研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
RNA病毒在多个病毒家族中的持续出现和再出现凸显了开发广谱抗病毒药物治疗药物的迫切需求。本研究旨在通过评估一系列4'-叠氮柔性核苷类似物对流行/大流行病原体的抗病毒药物活性,应对这一问题。
设计与对象
一系列4'-叠氮柔性核苷类似物被合成并评估其对流行/大流行病原体的广谱抗病毒药物活性。研究包括针对黄病毒、丝状病毒和冠状病毒的化合物测试,重点关注登革病毒及其相关病毒。
核心方法
一种化合物KAD-039,其特征为包含2',3'-二异丁酸-5'-ProTide前药,被评估其对黄病毒、丝状病毒和冠状病毒的抗病毒药物活性。使用几种病毒甲基转移酶的帽/GTP结合口袋中预期的三磷酸活性形式进行了计算分子对接研究。
主要结果
对KAD-039的药代动力学评估显示其在狗、猴和人类血浆中具有高稳定性,半衰期范围为19.9至28小时。然而,其在人类肝微粒体中稳定性较差(t1/2 = 6.1分钟)。计算分子对接研究显示,KAD-039TP在所有病毒MTases中的预测结合亲和力与天然底物相似或更高。
解释与创新
结果表明,KAD-039可能通过抑制病毒甲基转移酶发挥作用,为其广谱抗病毒药物活性提供了一种潜在机制。这些发现支持进一步研究其治疗潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于开发针对登革病毒及其相关病原体的广谱抗病毒药物治疗药物,从而改善监测、诊断和治疗策略,以应对病毒感染。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于体外和计算机模拟评估,可能无法完全反映体内疗效。证据强度中等,因为结果基于计算模型和药代动力学数据,而没有临床试验。

Discovery of a 4'-Azido Fleximer Nucleoside Analog With Broad-Spectrum Antiviral Activity Against DENV, EBOV, and Other RNA Viruses.

Authors: Kyle A Davis, Ryan L Slack, Thomas R Foulkes, Tereza Frčková, Lenka Tomková, Jessica L Smith, Meareg Amare, Grayson D Pipher, Peter Halfmann, Alec J Hirsch
Original abstract (English)
The ongoing emergence and reemergence of RNA viruses from multiple viral families highlights the urgent need for broad-spectrum antiviral therapeutics. To address this issue, a series of 4'-azido fleximer nucleoside analogs were synthesized and evaluated for broad-spectrum antiviral activity against pathogens of epidemic/pandemic concern. One compound, KAD-039, featuring a 2', 3'-diisobutyrate-5'-ProTide prodrug, exhibited potent antiviral activity across flaviviruses, filoviruses, and coronaviruses, with EC50 values ranging from 1 to 10 μM and minimal cytotoxicity (CC50 > 50 μM). Pharmacokinetic evaluation of KAD-039 demonstrated high plasma stability in dog, monkey, and human plasma, with half-lives ranging from 19.9 to 28 h but exhibited poor stability in human liver microsomes (t1/2 = 6.1 min). To help elucidate the mechanism of action of KAD-039 against these viral families, computational molecular docking studies were performed using the expected triphosphate active form within the cap/GTP-binding pockets of several viral methyltransferases and showed that KAD-039TP docked into all of the viral MTases with similar or higher predicted affinity compared to the natural substrate. In addition, KAD-039TP displayed reduced predicted affinity for the human N7 methyltransferase, suggesting low host toxicity. These results highlight 4'-azido fleximer analogs as promising broad-spectrum antivirals, potentially functioning through viral methyltransferase inhibition, and support further investigation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The ongoing emergence and reemergence of RNA viruses from multiple viral families highlights the urgent need for broad-spectrum antiviral therapeutics. The study aimed to address this issue by evaluating a series of 4'-azido fleximer nucleoside analogs for antiviral activity against pathogens of epidemic/pandemic concern.
Design and population
A series of 4'-azido fleximer nucleoside analogs were synthesized and evaluated for broad-spectrum antiviral activity against pathogens of epidemic/pandemic concern. The study included compounds tested against flaviviruses, filoviruses, and coronaviruses, with a focus on dengue virus and related viruses.
Core methods
One compound, KAD-039, featuring a 2', 3'-diisobutyrate-5'-ProTide prodrug, was evaluated for antiviral activity across flaviviruses, filoviruses, and coronaviruses. Computational molecular docking studies were performed using the expected triphosphate active form within the cap/GTP-binding pockets of several viral methyltransferases.
Main results
Pharmacokinetic evaluation of KAD-039 demonstrated high plasma stability in dog, monkey, and human plasma, with half-lives ranging from 19.9 to 28 h. However, it exhibited poor stability in human liver microsomes (t1/2 = 6.1 min). Computational molecular docking studies showed that KAD-039TP docked into all of the viral MTases with similar or higher predicted affinity compared to the natural substrate.
Interpretation and novelty
The results suggest that KAD-039 may function through viral methyltransferase inhibition, offering a potential mechanism for its broad-spectrum antiviral activity. These findings support further investigation into its therapeutic potential.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of broad-spectrum antivirals for dengue virus and related pathogens, potentially improving surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment strategies for viral infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro and in silico evaluations, which may not fully reflect in vivo efficacy. The evidence is moderate, as the results are based on computational models and pharmacokinetic data without clinical trials.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

作为 抗病毒药物 药物开发靶标的 登革病毒 NS2B-NS3 蛋白酶的结构生物学。

Structural biology of the dengue virus NS2B-NS3 protease as a target for antiviral drug development.
作者： Rahit Paul, Deeti Jyothi, Maitreyee Mukherjee, Srijita Chatterjee, Priya Manna, Purna Chandra Pal, Maytree Mukerjee, Srijita Chaterjee
摘要中文翻译登革热是近年来最常见的全球性问题,特别是在热带和亚热带地区,但目前缺乏治疗或预防的抗病毒药物。数百万人受到这种 登革病毒 的影响,但目前还没有适当的药物来治愈这种疾病。DENV 基因组 编码的一种多蛋白被转化为病毒 发病机制 和复制所必需的结构和非结构蛋白。其中,非结构蛋白复合物 NS2B-NS3 对于病毒多蛋白加工、复制和 宿主 固有的 免疫应答 控制至关重要。它充当胰蛋白酶样丝氨酸蛋白酶。NS2B/NS3 蛋白酶是参与病毒复制的关键酶,是针对 登革病毒 药物开发的主要靶标。NS3 蛋白酶具有保守的催化三联体 (His-Asp-Ser),而 NS2B 是稳定酶活性构象并有助于底物识别的重要辅助因子。通过破坏干扰素信号通路,NS2B-NS3 蛋白酶不仅有助于病毒复制,还使免疫逃避变得更容易。抑制这种酶作用的化合物可能是 抗病毒药物 药物发现过程的先驱。本文全面概述了病毒蛋白酶 (NS2B/NS3) 酶的详细结构信息以及该酶的作用机制,并重点介绍了与 NS2B/NS3 蛋白酶相关的各种抑制剂。更彻底地理解这种蛋白酶可以促进有效的 抗病毒药物 药物的逻辑开发,以预防登革热感染。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 登革病毒 NS2B-NS3 蛋白酶的结构生物学,特别是其在病毒复制中的作用以及作为 抗病毒药物 药物开发靶标的作用。核心问题是了解蛋白酶的结构和功能如何有助于创建针对登革热感染的有效 抗病毒药物 疗法。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对 登革病毒 中 NS2B-NS3 蛋白酶的结构和功能方面的全面分析。它不报告特定数据库、检索日期或研究资格标准。该评论主要是叙述性的,总结现有的知识,没有详细说明荟萃分析或共识过程。
共识与结论
NS2B-NS3 蛋白酶是 登革病毒 复制的关键酶,对于加工病毒多蛋白至关重要。它作为胰蛋白酶样丝氨酸蛋白酶发挥作用,并在调节 宿主 固有 免疫应答 中发挥关键作用。该蛋白酶的保守催化三联体 (His-Asp-Ser) 以及 NS2B 作为辅助因子在稳定其活性构象方面的作用已得到充分证实。针对这种蛋白酶的抑制剂被认为有望用于 抗病毒药物 药物发现。
争议与缺口
该综述强调,虽然 NS2B-NS3 蛋白酶是药物开发的关键靶点,但有关免疫逃避的具体机制以及蛋白酶在不同 登革病毒 血清型中的精确作用的信息有限。此外,该综述并未涉及跨血清型抑制的潜力或体内抑制剂的有效性。
科研与实践启示
需要进一步的研究来探索 NS2B-NS3 蛋白酶在 登革病毒 血清型之间的结构变异,并开发对所有血清型有效的抑制剂。还需要进行体内研究来评估针对该蛋白酶的潜在 抗病毒药物 化合物的功效和安全性。此外,了解蛋白酶在免疫逃避中的作用可能会导致更有针对性的治疗策略。

Structural biology of the dengue virus NS2B-NS3 protease as a target for antiviral drug development.

Authors: Rahit Paul, Deeti Jyothi, Maitreyee Mukherjee, Srijita Chatterjee, Priya Manna, Purna Chandra Pal, Maytree Mukerjee, Srijita Chaterjee
Original abstract (English)
Dengue is the most common global problem in recent times, particularly in tropical and subtropical areas, yet antivirals for therapy or prophylaxis are lacking. Millions of people are affected by this dengue virus, but no proper medication is available yet to cure this disease. One polyprotein that is encoded by the DENV genome is converted into structural and non-structural proteins that are necessary for viral pathogenesis and replication. Among these, the non-structural protein complex NS2B-NS3 is essential for viral polyprotein processing, replication, and host innate immune response control. It acts as a trypsin-like serine protease. The NS2B/NS3 protease is a key enzyme involved in viral replication and serves as a major target for drug development against the dengue virus. The NS3 protease has a conserved catalytic triad (His-Asp-Ser), whereas NS2B serves as an essential cofactor that stabilizes the active conformation of the enzyme and aids in substrate recognition. By disrupting interferon signalling pathways, the NS2B-NS3 protease not only aids in viral replication but also makes immune evasion easier. The compound that inhibits the action of this enzyme could be pioneering in the antiviral drug discovery process. This article provides a comprehensive overview of the detailed structural information of the viral protease (NS2B/NS3) enzyme with the mechanistic role of this enzyme, and highlights various inhibitors related to the NS2B/NS3 protease. A more thorough comprehension of this protease could facilitate the logical development of potent antiviral medications to prevent dengue infection.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the structural biology of the dengue virus NS2B-NS3 protease, specifically its role in viral replication and as a target for antiviral drug development. The central question is how understanding the protease's structure and function can aid in creating effective antiviral therapies for dengue infection.
Evidence base and method
The review is a comprehensive analysis of the structural and functional aspects of the NS2B-NS3 protease in the dengue virus. It does not report on specific databases, search dates, or eligibility criteria for studies. The review is primarily narrative, summarizing existing knowledge without detailing a meta-analysis or consensus process.
Consensus and conclusions
The NS2B-NS3 protease is a critical enzyme for dengue virus replication and is essential for processing the viral polyprotein. It functions as a trypsin-like serine protease and plays a key role in modulating the host innate immune response. The protease's conserved catalytic triad (His-Asp-Ser) and the role of NS2B as a cofactor in stabilizing its active conformation are well-established. Inhibitors targeting this protease are considered promising for antiviral drug discovery.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights that while the NS2B-NS3 protease is a key target for drug development, there is limited information on the specific mechanisms of immune evasion and the precise role of the protease in different dengue virus serotypes. Additionally, the review does not address the potential for cross-serotype inhibition or the effectiveness of inhibitors in vivo.
Research and practice implications
Further research is needed to explore the structural variations of the NS2B-NS3 protease across dengue virus serotypes and to develop inhibitors that are effective against all serotypes. There is also a need for in vivo studies to evaluate the efficacy and safety of potential antiviral compounds targeting this protease. Additionally, understanding the protease's role in immune evasion could lead to more targeted therapeutic strategies.

小儿登革热中枢神经系统感染的不同神经放射学表现:来自巴基斯坦的图片回顾与文献相关性。

The varying neuroradiological manifestations of pediatric dengue central nervous system infection: a pictorial review from Pakistan with literature correlation.
作者： Kumail Khandwala, Syed Mohammad Hasan, Sahra Khan Mateen, Naila Nadeem, Kiran Hilal
摘要中文翻译登革热感染是儿童神经系统并发症的一个日益被认识的原因,其累及中枢和周围神经系统的范围广泛且不断演变。这篇图片综述使用基于病理生理学和成像模式的方法系统地说明了儿科登革热的神经放射学表现,并将特征性磁共振成像 (MRI) 结果与当前文献相关联。神经影像学表现大致分为直接病毒神经侵袭性、免疫介导性和可逆性水肿模式。病毒直接累及包括脑膜脑炎、深部灰质累及和菱形脑炎,严重病例表现出坏死性脑病样脑炎的特征性“双甜甜圈”征象和脑桥受累的“南瓜灯”征象。免疫介导的白质损伤包括扩散受限的急性白质脑病和急性播散性脑脊髓炎,它们的扩散特征和与感染的时间关系不同。可逆性水肿综合征包括可逆性压部病变综合征和较少报道的可逆性后部脑病综合征,两者都与良好的临床和放射学结果相关。脊髓和周围神经系统表现包括脊髓炎和吉兰-巴利综合征,MRI 在区分这些实体与其他感染性和炎症性脊髓神经根病方面发挥着关键作用。重要的是,磁敏感加权或梯度回忆回波成像的病灶内出血性变化,特别是在深灰核和脑干内,代表了一种有价值的成像标记,有利于登革热而不是几种病毒和炎症模拟物。识别这些基于特征模式的神经放射学表现及其潜在的病理生理学相关性可以提高诊断信心,促进与竞争性诊断的区分,并指导流行地区儿科登革热神经系统疾病的及时管理。

登革热感染是儿童神经系统并发症的一个日益被认识的原因,其累及中枢和周围神经系统的范围广泛且不断演变。

共识与结论
可逆性水肿综合征包括可逆性压部病变综合征和较少报道的可逆性后部脑病综合征,两者都与良好的临床和放射学结果相关。
争议与缺口
重要的是,磁敏感加权或梯度回忆回波成像的病灶内出血性变化,特别是在深灰核和脑干内,代表了一种有价值的成像标记,有利于登革热而不是几种病毒和炎症模拟物。
科研与实践启示

The varying neuroradiological manifestations of pediatric dengue central nervous system infection: a pictorial review from Pakistan with literature correlation.

Authors: Kumail Khandwala, Syed Mohammad Hasan, Sahra Khan Mateen, Naila Nadeem, Kiran Hilal
Original abstract (English)
Dengue infection is an increasingly recognized cause of neurological complications in children, with a wide and evolving spectrum of central and peripheral nervous system involvement. This pictorial review systematically illustrates the neuroradiological manifestations of pediatric dengue using a pathophysiology- and imaging pattern-based approach, correlating characteristic magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) findings with the current literature. Neuroimaging manifestations are broadly categorized into direct viral neuroinvasive, immune-mediated, and reversible edema patterns. Direct viral involvement includes meningoencephalitis, deep gray matter involvement, and rhombencephalitis, with severe cases demonstrating the characteristic "double doughnut" sign of necrotizing encephalopathy-like encephalitis and the "Jack-o'-lantern" sign of pontine involvement. Immune-mediated white matter injury includes acute leukoencephalopathy with restricted diffusion and acute disseminated encephalomyelitis, which differ in their diffusion characteristics and temporal relationship to infection. Reversible edema syndromes include reversible splenial lesion syndrome and the less commonly reported posterior reversible encephalopathy syndrome, both associated with favorable clinical and radiological outcomes. Spinal and peripheral nervous system manifestations include myelitis and Guillain-Barré syndrome, with MRI playing a key role in differentiating these entities from other infectious and inflammatory myeloradiculopathies. Importantly, intralesional hemorrhagic changes on susceptibility-weighted or gradient recalled echo imaging, particularly within the deep gray nuclei and brainstem, represent a valuable imaging marker favoring dengue over several viral and inflammatory mimickers. Recognition of these characteristic pattern-based neuroradiological manifestations and their underlying pathophysiological correlates improves diagnostic confidence, facilitates differentiation from competing diagnoses, and guides timely management of pediatric dengue neurological disease in endemic regions.
View review five-element analysis

Evidence base and method

Consensus and conclusions
Reversible edema syndromes include reversible splenial lesion syndrome and the less commonly reported posterior reversible encephalopathy syndrome, both associated with favorable clinical and radiological outcomes.
Controversies and gaps
Importantly, intralesional hemorrhagic changes on susceptibility-weighted or gradient recalled echo imaging, particularly within the deep gray nuclei and brainstem, represent a valuable imaging marker favoring dengue over several viral and inflammatory mimickers.
Research and practice implications

评估登革热病毒血症作为登革热 抗病毒药物 III 期试验替代疗效结果的适用性:系统评价

Evaluating the Suitability of Dengue Viraemia as a Surrogate Efficacy Outcome for Phase III Trials of Dengue Antivirals: A Systematic Review
作者： Jeslyn Gonsalves, Yan Naung Win, Elinor Harriss, Richard J. Maude, Xin Hui S Chan, Elinor Harris
摘要中文翻译摘要背景:随着地域扩张到欧洲和非洲,登革热病例在 2024 年达到创纪录水平。重症登革热 的低进展率对登革热 III 期 抗病毒药物 试验的支持提出了挑战。病毒血症在 II 期试验中被用作病毒学结果,但 治疗 诱导的减少是否可靠地预测临床益处仍不确定。这项系统评价评估了登革热病毒血症与疾病严重程度之间的关联及其作为 III 期 抗病毒药物 试验的替代疗效结果的适用性。方法:我们于 2025 年 6 月 20 日检索了 Ovid MEDLINE、Ovid Embase、Scopus 和 Web of Science,查找自 2017 年以来编入索引的研究,扩展了之前的系统评价。我们纳入了实验室确诊的登革热临床研究,在入组时和至少一个后续时间点通过 RT-PCR 测量定量血清病毒载量,并报告 重症登革热 作为临床结果。该审查已在 PROSPERO 注册 (CRD420251077148),并遵循 PRISMA 2020 报告指南。结果:筛选3303条记录,审阅全文60篇;11 项研究符合纳入标准,主要包括来自亚太地区的住院儿童和年轻成人 队列研究。七项研究报告称,病情较严重的患者早期病毒血症(通常在患病第 1-5 天期间)较高,而三项研究发现没有显着关联,一项研究提供的证据不足。两项评估病毒血症动力学的研究发现,病毒清除速度越快,重症登革热 风险越低。采样时间、严重程度分类以及 宿主 和病毒因素调整的异质性限制了解释。证据的质量非常低。结论:登革热病毒血症与疾病严重程度之间相关性的证据不一致,并且受到方法学异质性的限制。大多数研究在入院时评估严重程度,无法确定病毒血症是否可以预测随后从非严重疾病进展为严重疾病。没有一个旨在确定 治疗 诱导的病毒血症减少是否转化为临床益处。因此,登革热病毒血症应被视为早期 抗病毒药物 开发的药效生物标志物和支持性病毒学结果,而不是作为 III 期试验的经过验证的替代疗效结果,因为 III 期试验仍需要具有临床意义的以患者为中心的结果。

登革病毒 1-4 和登革热病;其他黄病毒属于近邻排除项。生物相关的动物、分类学、模型、差异-诊断 和比较实体仅是补充,除非还存在材料目标特定证据;只有显式的词汇或不相关的硬排除才是最终的。

我们于 2025 年 6 月 20 日在 Ovid MEDLINE、Ovid Embase、Scopus 和 Web of Science 中检索了自 2017 年以来编入索引的研究,扩展了之前的系统综述。该审查已在 PROSPERO 注册 (CRD420251077148),并遵循 PRISMA 2020 报告指南。
共识与结论
登革热病毒血症与疾病严重程度之间关联的证据不一致,并且受到方法学异质性的限制。证据的质量非常低。
争议与缺口
采样时间、严重程度分类以及 宿主 和病毒因素调整的异质性限制了解释。大多数研究在入院时评估严重程度,无法确定病毒血症是否可以预测随后从非严重疾病进展为严重疾病。
科研与实践启示
没有一个旨在确定 治疗 诱导的病毒血症减少是否转化为临床益处。因此,登革热病毒血症应被视为早期 抗病毒药物 开发的药效生物标志物和支持性病毒学结果,而不是作为 III 期试验的经过验证的替代疗效结果,因为 III 期试验仍需要具有临床意义的以患者为中心的结果。

Evaluating the Suitability of Dengue Viraemia as a Surrogate Efficacy Outcome for Phase III Trials of Dengue Antivirals: A Systematic Review

Authors: Jeslyn Gonsalves, Yan Naung Win, Elinor Harriss, Richard J. Maude, Xin Hui S Chan, Elinor Harris
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background: Dengue cases reached record levels in 2024 alongside geographic expansion into Europe and Africa. The low rate of progression to severe dengue presents a challenge for powering phase III dengue antiviral trials. Viraemia is used as a virological outcome in phase II trials, but whether treatment-induced reductions reliably predict clinical benefit remains uncertain. This systematic review assessed the association between dengue viraemia and disease severity and its suitability as a surrogate efficacy outcome for phase III antiviral trials. Methods: We searched Ovid MEDLINE, Ovid Embase, Scopus, and Web of Science on 20 June 2025 for studies indexed since 2017, extending a previous systematic review. We included clinical studies of laboratory-confirmed dengue measuring quantitative serum viral load by RT-PCR at enrolment and at least one subsequent timepoint and reporting severe dengue as a clinical outcome. The review was registered with PROSPERO (CRD420251077148) and followed PRISMA 2020 reporting guidelines. Results: We screened 3303 records and reviewed 60 full texts; 11 studies met the inclusion criteria, comprising predominantly hospitalised paediatric and younger adult cohorts from the Asia-Pacific. Seven studies reported higher early viraemia (typically during illness days 1– 5) in patients with more severe disease, whereas three found no significant association and one provided insufficient evidence. Two studies assessing viraemia kinetics found that faster viral clearance was associated with lower risk of severe dengue. Heterogeneity in sampling timing, severity classification, and adjustment for host and viral factors limited interpretation. Certainty of evidence was very low. Conclusions: Evidence for an association between dengue viraemia and disease severity was inconsistent and limited by methodological heterogeneity. Most studies assessed severity around the time of hospital admission and could not establish if viraemia predicted subsequent progression from non-severe to severe disease. None were designed to determine whether treatment-induced reductions in viraemia translate into clinical benefit. Dengue viraemia should therefore be regarded as a pharmacodynamic biomarker and supportive virological outcome for early-phase antiviral development, rather than as a validated surrogate efficacy outcome for phase III trials, for which clinically meaningful patient-centred outcomes remain necessary.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Dengue viruses 1–4 and dengue disease; other flaviviruses are near-neighbour exclusions. Biologically related animal, taxonomic, model, differential-diagnosis and comparative entities are supplementary-only unless material target-specific evidence is also present; only explicit lexical or unrelated hard exclusions are terminal.
Evidence base and method
We searched Ovid MEDLINE, Ovid Embase, Scopus, and Web of Science on 20 June 2025 for studies indexed since 2017, extending a previous systematic review. The review was registered with PROSPERO (CRD420251077148) and followed PRISMA 2020 reporting guidelines.
Consensus and conclusions
Evidence for an association between dengue viraemia and disease severity was inconsistent and limited by methodological heterogeneity. Certainty of evidence was very low.
Controversies and gaps
Heterogeneity in sampling timing, severity classification, and adjustment for host and viral factors limited interpretation. Most studies assessed severity around the time of hospital admission and could not establish if viraemia predicted subsequent progression from non-severe to severe disease.
Research and practice implications
None were designed to determine whether treatment-induced reductions in viraemia translate into clinical benefit. Dengue viraemia should therefore be regarded as a pharmacodynamic biomarker and supportive virological outcome for early-phase antiviral development, rather than as a validated surrogate efficacy outcome for phase III trials, for which clinically meaningful patient-centred outcomes remain necessary.

登革病毒患病率、暴发病毒复制、诊断、治疗、媒介控制及预防策略:全面综述

Insights into Dengue Prevalence, Outbreaks, Virus Replication, Diagnosis, Treatment, Vector Control, and Preventive Strategies: A Comprehensive Review
作者： Nizam Uddin, Md. Shahadat Bin Shahid, Anika Tasnim
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
Dengue virus (DENV), a mosquito-borne RNA virus of the Flaviviridae family, poses a major global health challenge, with nearly half of the world’s population at risk and annual cases ranging from 100 to 400 million.
证据基础与方法
本综述对登革病毒的流行病学、病毒传播动力学、复制机制、临床表现和诊断方法进行了全面分析。它主要通过伊蚊传播,特别是埃及伊蚊(Aedes aegypti)和白纹伊蚊(Aedes albopictus),这些蚊子在热带和亚热带地区繁衍生息。该病毒包含四个血清型(DENV-1至DENV-4),每个血清型可提供对同型再感染的终身免疫力,但缺乏交叉免疫力。
共识与结论
登革病毒的传播增加是由气候变化、城市化以及防控措施不足所驱动的,南亚、美洲和东南亚等地区每年报告数百万例病例。
争议与缺口
尽管在疫苗研究方面做出了努力,但媒介控制仍然是登革热预防的主要策略,重点在于环境管理、生物控制和有针对性的化学干预。研究结果强调了在全球范围内加强监测系统、整合媒介管理以及开展公共卫生举措的迫切需求。
科研与实践启示
增强诊断能力、扩大疫苗接种计划并投资于创新的蚊媒控制策略,对于减轻登革热日益增长的负担至关重要。

Insights into Dengue Prevalence, Outbreaks, Virus Replication, Diagnosis, Treatment, Vector Control, and Preventive Strategies: A Comprehensive Review

Authors: Nizam Uddin, Md. Shahadat Bin Shahid, Anika Tasnim
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus (DENV), a mosquito-borne RNA virus of the Flaviviridae family, poses a major global health challenge, with nearly half of the world’s population at risk and annual cases ranging from 100 to 400 million. It is transmitted primarily by Aedes mosquitoes, specifically Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus, which thrive in tropical and subtropical regions. The virus consists of four serotypes (DENV-1 to DENV-4), each conferring lifelong immunity against reinfection by the same serotype but lacking cross-immunity. The increasing incidence of dengue outbreaks is driven by climate change, urbanization, and inadequate vector control measures, with regions such as South Asia, the Americas, and Southeast Asia reporting millions of cases annually. This review provides a comprehensive analysis of dengue epidemiology, virus transmission dynamics, replication mechanisms, clinical manifestations, and diagnostic approaches. It also explores recent advancements in vaccine development, particularly the comparison between Qdenga and Dengvaxia, highlighting their differences in efficacy, safety, and target populations. Despite efforts in vaccine research, vector control remains the primary strategy for dengue prevention, focusing on environmental management, biological control, and targeted chemical interventions. The study further examines Bangladesh as a case study, documenting its largest-ever dengue outbreak in 2023 and its transition into an endemic phase in early 2025. The findings underscore the urgent need for enhanced surveillance systems, integrated vector management, and public health initiatives to combat dengue globally. Strengthening diagnostic capabilities, expanding vaccination programs, and investing in innovative mosquito control strategies will be crucial in mitigating the growing burden of dengue worldwide. Bangladesh Journal of Infectious Diseases, June 2026;13(1):397-410

Consensus and conclusions

Controversies and gaps

The findings underscore the urgent need for enhanced surveillance systems, integrated vector management, and public health initiatives to combat dengue globally.

在登革热高风险工业区建立无登革热工厂评估框架的主动举措:叙述性审查。

Proactive Initiative to Establish a Dengue-Free Factory Assessment Framework in Dengue High-Risk Industrial Areas: A Narrative Review.
作者： Wanapa Ritthison, Adisak Bhumiratana, Chanthana Padungtod, Narongsak Tongtummachart
摘要中文翻译在工业扩张与伊蚊 媒介 繁殖栖息地交叉的流行国家,登革热造成的威胁日益严重。工厂和制造场所经常产生大量不受管理的储水容器,包括储水箱、机械部件、景观美化设施和可回收废物,这些容器有利于埃及伊蚊 媒介 的常年生长。尽管公共卫生负担巨大,但工作场所的职业登革热预防仍然很大程度上是被动的、分散的,并且与安全、健康和环境 (SHE) 管理体系不够一致。这篇叙述性综述综合了工业环境中登革热 流行病学、工厂环境中的幼虫源生态、工人的登革热风险认知以及登革热流行国家职业健康监管环境的证据。利用这些证据领域,我们提出了旨在预防和减少AE的主动“无登革热工厂评估框架”(D-FAF)的概念基础。控制工业区工作场所埃及伊蚊孳生和登革热高风险,保障员工健康,最大限度减少工作损失,实现登革热持续减少发病率。该框架将 ISO 45001 和 ISO 14001 的“计划-实施-检查-行动”(PDCA) 逻辑集成为可扩展、符合标准的工具,适用于登革热高风险地区的不同工业部门。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注 登革病毒 及其与工业领域的关联,特别研究工厂在创造埃及伊蚊 媒介 繁殖栖息地方面的作用。核心问题是如何建立一个积极主动的框架来预防高风险工业环境中的登革热。
证据基础与方法
该综述对工业环境中的登革热 流行病学、工厂环境中的幼虫源生态、工人的风险认知以及登革热流行国家的职业健康监管环境进行了叙述性综合。它不报告荟萃分析或汇总估计。
共识与结论
该审查强调,登革热在 媒介 繁殖栖息地与人类活动重叠的工业地区构成越来越大的威胁。结论是,拟议的无登革热工厂评估框架 (D-FAF) 是一个旨在预防和减少AE的概念工具。工作场所埃及伊蚊繁殖和登革热风险。
争议与缺口
审查指出,职业性登革热预防仍然是被动且分散的,与 SHE 管理系统的一致性不够。工业环境中缺乏主动控制登革热的综合框架。
科研与实践启示
审查表明,需要像 D-FAF 这样可扩展、符合标准的框架来预防工业领域的登革热。它强调了将 媒介 控制与工作场所安全和健康管理系统相结合的重要性。

Proactive Initiative to Establish a Dengue-Free Factory Assessment Framework in Dengue High-Risk Industrial Areas: A Narrative Review.

Authors: Wanapa Ritthison, Adisak Bhumiratana, Chanthana Padungtod, Narongsak Tongtummachart
Original abstract (English)
Dengue poses a growing threat in endemic countries where industrial expansion intersects with Aedes vector-breeding habitats. Factories and manufacturing premises often generate a multitude of unmanaged water-holding containers-including water storage tanks, machinery components, landscaping features, and recyclable waste-that favor perennial reservoirs for Aedes aegypti vectors. Despite substantial public health burdens, occupational dengue prevention in the workplace remains largely reactive, fragmented, and insufficiently aligned with safety, health, and environment (SHE) management systems. This narrative review synthesizes evidence on dengue epidemiology in industrial contexts, larval source ecology in factory environments, workers' dengue risk perception, and the regulatory landscape governing occupational health in dengue-endemic countries. Drawing on these evidence domains, we propose the conceptual foundations of a proactive "Dengue-Free Factory Assessment Framework" (D-FAF) designed to prevent and reduce Ae. aegypti breeding and high dengue risk in workplaces in industrial areas, safeguard employee health, minimize work loss, and achieve a sustained reduction in dengue incidence. The framework integrates the "Plan-Do-Check-Act" (PDCA) logic of ISO 45001 and ISO 14001 into a scalable, standards-aligned tool applicable across diverse industrial sectors in dengue high-risk areas.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on dengue virus and its association with industrial areas, particularly examining the role of factories in creating breeding habitats for Aedes aegypti vectors. The central question is how to establish a proactive framework to prevent dengue in high-risk industrial settings.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis of evidence on dengue epidemiology in industrial contexts, larval source ecology in factory environments, workers' risk perception, and the regulatory landscape for occupational health in dengue-endemic countries. It does not report on meta-analysis or pooled estimates.
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights that dengue poses a growing threat in industrial areas where vector breeding habitats overlap with human activity. It concludes that the proposed Dengue-Free Factory Assessment Framework (D-FAF) is a conceptual tool designed to prevent and reduce Ae. aegypti breeding and dengue risk in workplaces.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that occupational dengue prevention remains reactive and fragmented, with insufficient alignment with SHE management systems. There is a lack of comprehensive frameworks for proactive dengue control in industrial settings.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests the need for a scalable, standards-aligned framework like D-FAF to prevent dengue in industrial areas. It emphasizes the importance of integrating vector control with workplace safety and health management systems.

儿童 重症登革热:重症登革热、早期识别和循证管理

Severe Dengue in Children: Pathogenesis, Early Recognition, and Evidence-Informed Management
作者： Hsien-Yi Wang, Shih-Bin Su, Chung‐Yi Li, Kow‐Tong Chen
摘要中文翻译重症登革热 仍然是热带和亚热带地区儿科住院、发病和 死亡率 的重要原因,特别是在快速分诊和儿科重症监护能力有限的地区。这篇结构化叙述性综述综合了儿童 流行病学、发病机制、早期识别、诊断、管理和预防的证据。严重疾病反映了病毒因素、预先存在的免疫力、宿主 反应失调和微血管内皮损伤之间的相互作用。抗体依赖性增强、炎症介质、登革热非结构蛋白 1 和内皮糖萼破坏会导致血管通透性过高、血浆渗漏、休克、严重出血和器官损伤。由于病情恶化通常在退热前后突然发生,因此系列临床评估、血细胞比容趋势、尿量监测和及时识别警告信号是风险分层的核心。分子检测和 NS1 抗原检测在发热早期最为有用,尽管诊断效果因发病时间和免疫状态而异。仔细滴定的等渗晶体疗法仍然是 治疗 的基石;延迟复苏和过量输液都可能使结果恶化。减少 死亡率 需要综合临床和公共卫生策略,结合标准化儿科管理、可及的诊断、有效的转诊系统、疫苗接种(酌情)、监测 和 媒介 控制。

Severe Dengue in Children: Pathogenesis, Early Recognition, and Evidence-Informed Management

Authors: Hsien-Yi Wang, Shih-Bin Su, Chung‐Yi Li, Kow‐Tong Chen
Original abstract (English)
Severe dengue remains an important cause of pediatric hospitalization, morbidity, and mortality in tropical and subtropical regions, particularly where rapid triage and pediatric critical-care capacity are limited. This structured narrative review synthesizes evidence on the epidemiology, pathogenesis, early recognition, diagnosis, management, and prevention of severe dengue in children. Severe disease reflects interactions among viral factors, pre-existing immunity, dysregulated host responses, and microvascular endothelial injury. Antibody-dependent enhancement, inflammatory mediators, dengue nonstructural protein 1, and endothelial glycocalyx disruption contribute to vascular hyperpermeability, plasma leakage, shock, severe bleeding, and organ impairment. Because deterioration often occurs abruptly around defervescence, serial clinical assessment, hematocrit trends, urine-output monitoring, and timely recognition of warning signs are central to risk stratification. Molecular assays and NS1 antigen testing are most useful during the early febrile phase, although diagnostic performance varies with illness timing and immune status. Carefully titrated isotonic crystalloid therapy remains the cornerstone of treatment; both delayed resuscitation and excessive fluid administration may worsen outcomes. Reducing mortality requires integrated clinical and public-health strategies combining standardized pediatric management, accessible diagnostics, effective referral systems, vaccination where appropriate, surveillance, and vector control.

Severe dengue remains an important cause of pediatric hospitalization, morbidity, and mortality in tropical and subtropical regions, particularly where rapid triage and pediatric critical-care capacity are limited.
Evidence base and method
This structured narrative review synthesizes evidence on the epidemiology, pathogenesis, early recognition, diagnosis, management, and prevention of severe dengue in children.
Consensus and conclusions

Antibody-dependent enhancement, inflammatory mediators, dengue nonstructural protein 1, and endothelial glycocalyx disruption contribute to vascular hyperpermeability, plasma leakage, shock, severe bleeding, and organ impairment.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not report controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
Because deterioration often occurs abruptly around defervescence, serial clinical assessment, hematocrit trends, urine-output monitoring, and timely recognition of warning signs are central to risk stratification. Molecular assays and NS1 antigen testing are most useful during the early febrile phase, although diagnostic performance varies with illness timing and immune status. Carefully titrated isotonic crystalloid therapy remains the cornerstone of treatment; both delayed resuscitation and excessive fluid administration may worsen outcomes.

登革热后的暴发性急性播散性脑脊髓炎的去骨瓣减压术:病例报告及文献综述

Decompressive hemicraniectomy for fulminant acute disseminated encephalomyelitis following dengue fever: Case report and literature review
作者： José Luis Quelho Filho, Paulo Eduardo Albuquerque Zito Raffa, Henrique Francisco Santana, Ayanne Alves de Oliveira, Gabriel Nunes Melo Assunção, Leonardo Vieira Nunes, Leonardo Peixoto Garcia, Lucas Crociati Meguins
摘要中文翻译背景:急性播散性脑脊髓炎(ADEM)是一种罕见的免疫介导的中枢神经系统脱髓鞘疾病,通常发生在病毒或细菌感染后。尽管大多数患者对免疫调节治疗反应良好,但一小部分患者会出现暴发性 ADEM,其特征是恶性脑水肿、难治性颅内高压和 死亡率 的高风险。去骨瓣减压术很少被报道为此类病例的挽救生命的干预措施。病例描述:一名 25 岁女性在 登革热 后出现暴发性 ADEM,最初表现为反复发作,进展为癫痫持续状态。脑磁共振成像显示广泛的双侧脱髓鞘病变,涉及额顶叶、枕叶、颞叶和小脑白质,并延伸至胼胝体。尽管采取了积极的医疗治疗,包括大剂量静脉注射甲泼尼龙、渗透疗法、镇静和血浆置换,但患者仍出现与弥漫性脑水肿、瞳孔不等和即将发生脑疝的放射学证据相关的进行性神经功能恶化。由于最大限度的药物治疗失败,进行了左侧半颅骨减压术。患者随后接受了额外的免疫调节 治疗,度过了疾病的急性期,并表现出显着的神经功能恢复。文献综述发现了 9 例先前报道的暴发性 ADEM 病例,均采用去骨瓣减压术治疗。结论:暴发性 ADEM 是一种罕见但可能致命的神经急症。对于并发恶性脑水肿和难治性颅内高压的病例,去骨瓣减压术可能是一种挽救生命的治疗选择。早期识别和及时的手术干预似乎与良好的神经学结果相关。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述的重点是 登革热 后使用减压性半颅骨切除术治疗暴发性急性播散性脑脊髓炎 (ADEM)。核心问题是,对于 登革病毒 引起的暴发性 ADEM 病例,去骨瓣减压术是否是一种可行的挽救生命的干预措施。
证据基础与方法
该综述是病例报告与文献综述相结合的。它包括一名 25 岁女性在 登革热 后患有暴发性 ADEM 的单个病例研究,并参考了之前报道的 9 例接受去骨瓣减压术治疗的暴发性 ADEM 病例。该审查没有明确报告荟萃分析或共识过程。
共识与结论
暴发性 ADEM 是一种罕见但可能致命的神经急症。这表明,对于并发恶性脑水肿和难治性颅内高压的病例,去骨瓣减压术可能是一种挽救生命的治疗选择。早期识别和及时的手术干预与良好的神经学结果相关。
争议与缺口
证据仅限于 病例报告,其强度不足以建立明确的共识。没有提及支持在暴发性 ADEM 中使用去骨瓣减压术的随机对照试验或大规模研究。该综述也没有涉及暴发性 ADEM 发展的潜在机制或其与 登革病毒 的关系。
科研与实践启示
需要进一步的研究来评估去骨瓣减压术治疗 登革病毒 引起的暴发性 ADEM 的有效性和安全性。这包括开展随机对照试验并探索暴发性 ADEM 的病理生理机制。在临床实践中,早期识别暴发性 ADEM 并及时进行手术干预可能对于改善患者预后至关重要。

Decompressive hemicraniectomy for fulminant acute disseminated encephalomyelitis following dengue fever: Case report and literature review

Authors: José Luis Quelho Filho, Paulo Eduardo Albuquerque Zito Raffa, Henrique Francisco Santana, Ayanne Alves de Oliveira, Gabriel Nunes Melo Assunção, Leonardo Vieira Nunes, Leonardo Peixoto Garcia, Lucas Crociati Meguins
Original abstract (English)
Background: Acute disseminated encephalomyelitis (ADEM) is a rare immune-mediated demyelinating disorder of the central nervous system that typically occurs after viral or bacterial infections. Although most patients respond favorably to immunomodulatory therapy, a small subset develops fulminant ADEM characterized by malignant cerebral edema, refractory intracranial hypertension, and a high risk of mortality. Decompressive craniectomy has rarely been reported as a life-saving intervention in such cases. Case Description: A 25-year-old woman developed fulminant ADEM following dengue fever and initially presented with recurrent seizures progressing to status epilepticus. Brain magnetic resonance imaging demonstrated extensive bilateral demyelinating lesions involving the frontoparietal, occipital, temporal, and cerebellar white matter, with extension into the corpus callosum. Despite aggressive medical management, including high-dose intravenous methylprednisolone, osmotherapy, sedation, and plasma exchange, the patient experienced progressive neurological deterioration associated with diffuse cerebral edema, anisocoria, and radiological evidence of impending cerebral herniation. Owing to the failure of maximal medical therapy, a left decompressive hemicraniectomy was performed. The patient subsequently underwent additional immunomodulatory treatment, survived the acute phase of the disease, and demonstrated significant neurological recovery. A review of the literature identified nine previously reported cases of fulminant ADEM treated with decompressive craniectomy. Conclusion: Fulminant ADEM is an uncommon but potentially fatal neurological emergency. In cases complicated by malignant cerebral edema and refractory intracranial hypertension, decompressive craniectomy may represent a life-saving therapeutic option. Early recognition and timely surgical intervention appear to be associated with favorable neurological outcomes.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the use of decompressive hemicraniectomy for fulminant acute disseminated encephalomyelitis (ADEM) following dengue fever. The central question is whether decompressive craniectomy is a viable life-saving intervention in cases of fulminant ADEM caused by dengue virus.
Evidence base and method
The review is a case report combined with a literature review. It includes a single case study of a 25-year-old woman with fulminant ADEM following dengue fever and references nine previously reported cases of fulminant ADEM treated with decompressive craniectomy. The review does not explicitly report on meta-analysis or consensus processes.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that fulminant ADEM is an uncommon but potentially fatal neurological emergency. It suggests that decompressive craniectomy may represent a life-saving therapeutic option in cases complicated by malignant cerebral edema and refractory intracranial hypertension. Early recognition and timely surgical intervention are associated with favorable neurological outcomes.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence is limited to case reports, which are not robust enough to establish a definitive consensus. There is no mention of randomized controlled trials or large-scale studies to support the use of decompressive craniectomy in fulminant ADEM. The review also does not address the mechanisms underlying the development of fulminant ADEM or its relationship with dengue virus.
Research and practice implications
Further research is needed to evaluate the efficacy and safety of decompressive craniectomy in fulminant ADEM caused by dengue virus. This includes conducting randomized controlled trials and exploring the pathophysiological mechanisms of fulminant ADEM. In clinical practice, early recognition of fulminant ADEM and prompt surgical intervention may be critical for improving patient outcomes.

利用环保纳米技术靶向登革热病毒蛋白:抗病毒药物 研究的一条有希望的道路

Targeting Dengue Viral Proteins with Eco-Friendly Nanotechnology: A Promising Path for Antiviral Research
作者： D Madan Kumar, R. Raj Bharath, P. Venkataraman, Kumar Sivasubramanian, Leelavathi Prakasam
摘要中文翻译登革病毒 (DENV) 是一种属于黄病毒科的蚊媒病原体,仍然是全球主要的公共卫生问题,每年在热带和亚热带地区造成数百万例感染。该病毒编码结构蛋白和非结构蛋白,这些蛋白在其复制、组装和免疫逃避中发挥着关键作用。尽管研究取得了实质性进展,但仍然没有具体的 抗病毒药物 药物可用,目前的 抗病毒药物 仍然主要是支持性的。本综述强调了 DENV 结构蛋白(C、prM/M 和 E)和非结构蛋白(NS1-NS5)作为 抗病毒药物 干预措施的潜在分子靶点的重要性。绿色合成的银、金和铁氧化物纳米颗粒通过靶向关键病毒蛋白(如包膜 (E)、NS3 蛋白酶/解旋酶和 NS5 RNA 依赖性 RNA 聚合酶),表现出对 DENV 的强大抑制活性。这些纳米颗粒干扰病毒进入、复制和蛋白质合成,同时表现出高生物相容性和最小的细胞毒性。总的来说,本次综述中讨论的结果强调了环保纳米技术作为开发下一代 抗病毒药物 治疗 抗病毒药物 的可持续和有效平台的前景。

范围与问题
该综述重点关注 登革病毒 (DENV),这是一种蚊媒黄病毒,在全球范围内导致数百万例感染和严重疾病,并探讨如何利用其结构蛋白 (C、prM/M、E) 和非结构蛋白 (NS1-NS5) 作为使用环保纳米技术进行 抗病毒药物 干预措施的分子靶点。

Targeting Dengue Viral Proteins with Eco-Friendly Nanotechnology: A Promising Path for Antiviral Research

Authors: D Madan Kumar, R. Raj Bharath, P. Venkataraman, Kumar Sivasubramanian, Leelavathi Prakasam
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus (DENV), a mosquito-borne pathogen belonging to the Flaviviridae family, continues to be a major global public health concern, causing millions of infections annually across tropical and subtropical regions. The virus encodes both structural and non-structural proteins that play critical roles in its replication, assembly, and immune evasion. Despite substantial research progress, there is still no specific antiviral drug available, and current treatment remains primarily supportive. This review highlights the significance of DENV structural proteins (C, prM/M, and E) and non-structural proteins (NS1–NS5) as potential molecular targets for antiviral interventions. Green-synthesized silver, gold, and iron oxide nanoparticles have demonstrated strong inhibitory activity against DENV by targeting key viral proteins such as the envelope (E), NS3 protease/helicase, and NS5 RNA-dependent RNA polymerase. These nanoparticles interfere with viral entry, replication, and protein synthesis while exhibiting high biocompatibility and minimal cytotoxicity. Collectively, the findings discussed in this review underscore the promise of eco-friendly nanotechnology as a sustainable and effective platform for developing next-generation antiviral therapies against dengue virus.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on dengue virus (DENV), a mosquito‐borne flavivirus that causes millions of infections and severe disease worldwide, and asks how its structural (C, prM/M, E) and non‐structural (NS1‐NS5) proteins can be exploited as molecular targets for antiviral interventions using eco‐friendly nanotechnology.
Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence does not report the review type, databases searched, dates, eligibility criteria, or study selection process.
Consensus and conclusions
Green‐synthesized silver, gold, and iron‐oxide nanoparticles exhibit strong inhibitory activity against DENV by targeting the envelope protein, NS3 protease/helicase, and NS5 polymerase; these nanomaterials interfere with viral entry, replication, and protein synthesis while showing high biocompatibility and low cytotoxicity, underscoring their promise as a sustainable platform for next‐generation dengue antivirals.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not discuss any controversies, conflicting findings, or specific evidence gaps related to nanotechnology‐based dengue antivirals.
Research and practice implications
Further research should prioritize in‐depth evaluation of eco‐friendly nanoparticle formulations against DENV proteins, optimization of delivery and safety profiles, and translation of these findings into scalable antiviral strategies for dengue prevention and treatment.

越南住院儿童登革热伴警告征象的临床和流行病学特征:一项横断面研究。

Clinical and epidemiological characteristics of dengue with warning signs among hospitalized children in Vietnam: A cross-sectional study.
作者： Yen Nguyen Thi Ngoc, Phong Nguyen Doan, Trung Nguyen Quang, Lien Tran Thi, Linh Dang Viet, Nhung Pham Thi, Linh Dang Phuong, Chuc Dang Van

范围与问题
目的登革热是一种主要的媒介媒介传播的病毒性感染,在流行地区是儿童住院的常见原因。
证据基础与方法
本研究描述了越南北部住院儿童中实验室确诊登革热的临床和实验室特征,并探讨了与登革热伴警告征象(DWS)相关的因素。方法本项基于医院的横断面研究包括回顾性和前瞻性部分。
共识与结论
In the multivariable analysis, age >10 years (adjusted odds ratio [aOR] 2.17, 95% confidence interval [CI] 1.28-3.66; p = 0.004), urban residence (aOR 2.88, 95% CI 1.27-6.51; p = 0.011), platelet count ≤100 × 109/L (aOR 3.34, 95% CI 1.86-5.97; p < 0.001), and AST ≥100 U/L (aOR 2.83, 95% CI 1.58-5.05; p < 0.001) were independently associated with DWS.

Clinical and epidemiological characteristics of dengue with warning signs among hospitalized children in Vietnam: A cross-sectional study.

Authors: Yen Nguyen Thi Ngoc, Phong Nguyen Doan, Trung Nguyen Quang, Lien Tran Thi, Linh Dang Viet, Nhung Pham Thi, Linh Dang Phuong, Chuc Dang Van
Original abstract (English)
ObjectiveDengue is a major vector-borne viral infection and a frequent cause of pediatric hospitalization in endemic settings. This study described the clinical and laboratory characteristics of hospitalized children with laboratory-confirmed dengue in northern Vietnam and examined factors associated with dengue with warning signs (DWS).MethodsThis hospital-based cross-sectional study included retrospective and prospective components. Medical records were retrospectively reviewed for eligible children aged 0-16 years admitted from 1 September to 29 December 2022, whereas eligible children in the same age group were prospectively enrolled from 30 December 2022 to 31 August 2023. DWS was classified according to the World Health Organization 2009 criteria and the Vietnamese Ministry of Health guideline. Multivariable logistic regression was used to identify factors associated with DWS.ResultsAmong 319 children, 126 (39.5%) met the criteria for DWS. In the multivariable analysis, age >10 years (adjusted odds ratio [aOR] 2.17, 95% confidence interval [CI] 1.28-3.66; p = 0.004), urban residence (aOR 2.88, 95% CI 1.27-6.51; p = 0.011), platelet count ≤100 × 109/L (aOR 3.34, 95% CI 1.86-5.97; p < 0.001), and AST ≥100 U/L (aOR 2.83, 95% CI 1.58-5.05; p < 0.001) were independently associated with DWS. Among children with DWS, the most frequently observed warning signs were abdominal pain or tenderness (64.3%), persistent vomiting (31.7%), and mucosal bleeding (25.4%).ConclusionsDWS was common among hospitalized children with dengue at a tertiary children's hospital in northern Vietnam. Older age, urban residence, thrombocytopenia, and elevated aspartate aminotransferase may help identify children who require closer monitoring during hospitalization.

Evidence base and method

Controversies and gaps

Research and practice implications

抗病毒药物药物研发中的蝎子肽:深入综述。

Scorpion Peptides in Antiviral Drug Discovery: An In-depth Review.
作者： Mojtaba Memariani, Hamed Memariani
摘要中文翻译尽管在疫苗学和抗病毒药物疗法领域取得了显著进展,病毒感染仍然是全球范围内导致发病率和死亡率的主要原因之一。因此,识别新的、有效的抗病毒药物药物候选物至关重要。蝎子毒液被视为结构多样且具有生物活性的肽的丰富来源,这些肽可能在诊断、预防和治疗方面提供机会。本综述旨在提供关于蝎子来源肽的当代且详细的分析,特别关注其结构多样性、细胞毒性、抗病毒药物特性以及作用机制。此外,本文还强调了未来研究的几个潜在有成效的领域。目前的证据表明,蝎子来源的肽对多种重要的医学病毒病原体具有有效性,例如寨卡病毒、登革病毒、肠病毒71型、乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)、单纯疱疹病毒1型、人类免疫缺陷病毒1型、甲型流感病毒(IAV)、严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒-2(SARS-CoV-2)以及登革热病毒。然而,大多数这些发现来自于体外研究,而动物模型中的证据仍然有限,目前尚无人类临床评估数据。已有研究提示这些肽可能通过多种作用机制起效,包括直接病毒灭活作用、抑制病毒进入、调节免疫应答、减少病毒诱导的细胞病变效应以及抑制病毒基因表达。新兴证据还表明,某些蝎子来源的肽在HBV、IAV和新城疫病毒感染的动物模型中显示出有效性。总体而言,这些肽似乎为病毒感染的治疗开辟了新的途径。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦于登革病毒和登革病,特别强调蝎子来源肽的结构多样性、细胞毒性、抗病毒药物特性以及作用机制。核心问题是这些肽是否可以作为有效的抗病毒药物药物用于登革病毒及其相关黄病毒的治疗。

本综述是对蝎子来源肽的叙述性分析,没有明确提及数据库、检索日期、纳入标准或荟萃分析。证据来源于体外研究和有限的动物模型,未报告临床数据。

综述得出结论,蝎子来源的肽显示出作为抗病毒药物药物用于登革病毒治疗的潜力,有证据表明其具有直接病毒灭活作用、抑制病毒进入、调节免疫应答以及抑制病毒基因表达等特性。然而,这些发现主要基于体外研究,动物模型中的证据仍然有限。

综述指出,大多数发现来自体外研究,动物模型中的证据仍然有限。目前尚无人类临床评估数据,这造成了显著的证据缺口。此外,作用机制尚未完全阐明,缺乏评估抗病毒药物效果的标准方法。

Scorpion Peptides in Antiviral Drug Discovery: An In-depth Review.

Authors: Mojtaba Memariani, Hamed Memariani
Original abstract (English)
Despite remarkable progress in the fields of vaccinology and antiviral therapy, viral infections persist as a leading cause of morbidity and mortality worldwide. Therefore, it is crucial to identify new and effective antiviral drug candidates. Scorpion venoms are viewed as a rich source of structurally diverse and biologically active peptides, which may present opportunities for diagnostic, preventive, and therapeutic applications. This review intends to provide a contemporary and detailed analysis of scorpion-derived peptides, with a particular focus on their structural diversity, cytotoxic effects, antiviral properties, and mechanisms of action. Additionally, this paper highlights several potential fruitful areas for future studies. Current evidence indicates that scorpion-derived peptides are effective against a wide array of medically important viral pathogens such as chikungunya virus, dengue virus, enterovirus 71, hepatitis B virus (HBV), herpes simplex virus 1, human immunodeficiency virus 1, influenza A virus (IAV), severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus-2, and Zika virus. Nevertheless, the majority of these findings have been obtained from in vitro studies, whereas evidence from animal models remains limited and clinical evaluation in humans is currently unavailable. Available studies suggest that these peptides may act through diverse mechanisms of action, including direct virucidal activity, inhibition of viral entry, immune system modulation, reduction of virus-induced cytopathic effects, and suppression of viral gene expression. Emerging evidence also demonstrates the efficacy of certain scorpion-derived peptides in animal models of HBV, IAV, and Newcastle disease virus infection. Overall, such peptides appear to inaugurate new avenues for the treatment of viral infections.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on dengue virus and dengue disease, with an emphasis on the structural diversity, cytotoxic effects, antiviral properties, and mechanisms of action of scorpion-derived peptides. The central question is whether these peptides can serve as effective antiviral agents against dengue virus and related flaviviruses.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative analysis of scorpion-derived peptides, with no explicit mention of databases, search dates, eligibility criteria, or meta-analysis. The evidence is derived from in vitro studies and limited animal models, with no clinical data reported.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that scorpion-derived peptides show promise as antiviral agents against dengue virus, with evidence of direct virucidal activity, inhibition of viral entry, immune system modulation, and suppression of viral gene expression. However, these findings are primarily based on in vitro studies, and there is limited evidence from animal models.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights that most findings are from in vitro studies, and evidence from animal models remains limited. Clinical evaluation in humans is currently unavailable, creating a significant evidence gap. Additionally, the mechanisms of action are not fully understood, and there is a lack of standardized methodologies for assessing antiviral efficacy.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that future studies should focus on validating the antiviral properties of scorpion-derived peptides in animal models and eventually in clinical trials. It also emphasizes the need for further research into the mechanisms of action and the development of standardized protocols for evaluating their efficacy.

PTBP1在宿主病毒界面的作用:病毒RNA翻译、复制和免疫调节的机制角色

PTBP1 at the host-virus interface: mechanistic roles in viral RNA translation, replication, and immune modulation.
作者： Kajal, Achyut Pandey, Rajesh Yadav, Rishikesh Shukla, Shruti Mishra
摘要中文翻译病毒需要宿主 RNA结合蛋白来完成其生命周期中的重要步骤。多嘧啶区结合蛋白1(PTBP1)是一种广泛存在于细胞中的RNA结合蛋白,通过其四个RRMs结构域以及在细胞核和细胞质之间的穿梭作用,执行重要的调控功能,包括可变剪接、RNA稳定性、RNA定位和翻译。越来越多的证据表明,许多病毒利用PTBP1的这些调控功能来促进其基因表达和复制。本综述描述了现有关于PTBP1在病毒感染过程中功能的机制性知识,特别关注其在病毒RNA翻译、病毒RNA 基因组复制以及调控宿主 抗病毒药物反应中的作用。特别强调了PTBP1在肠道病毒和丙型肝炎病毒IRES依赖性翻译中的调控作用,以及其在如登革病毒和日本脑炎病毒等黄病毒中对RNA稳定性、远距离RNA相互作用和基因组环化的作用。此外,还讨论了最近关于PTBP1在冠状病毒RNA代谢中的功能数据。进一步地,本综述从先天免疫信号通路、应激颗粒生物学以及病毒-宿主相互作用的角度,回顾了PTBP1在同时具有促病毒和抗病毒作用中的角色。最后,我们将探讨尽管PTBP1作为关键的细胞RNA结合蛋白具有多功能性,但其是否可以被用作宿主导向的抗病毒药物药物靶点的可能性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦于PTBP1在登革病毒感染中的作用,具体包括其在病毒RNA翻译、基因组复制以及调控宿主 抗病毒药物反应中的参与。核心问题是PTBP1如何在登革病毒感染过程中促进这些过程。
证据基础与方法
本综述是对现有关于PTBP1在病毒感染过程中功能的机制性知识的叙述性综合。它并未报告特定数据库、搜索日期或元分析方法。证据来源于对PTBP1在病毒RNA代谢和宿主-病毒相互作用中作用的一般性描述。
共识与结论
有共识认为PTBP1在调控病毒RNA翻译、基因组复制以及宿主 抗病毒药物反应中起关键作用。具体而言,PTBP1在如登革病毒和日本脑炎病毒等黄病毒中促进RNA稳定性、远距离RNA相互作用和基因组环化。它还影响肠道病毒和丙型肝炎病毒的IRES依赖性翻译。
争议与缺口
综述指出PTBP1具有促病毒和抗病毒的双重作用,这引发了一些争议。此外,综述还提到PTBP1的多功能性使其在作为宿主导向的抗病毒药物药物靶点时面临挑战。目前缺乏关于PTBP1如何在登革病毒感染背景下调控宿主先天免疫信号通路和应激颗粒生物学的详细机制研究。
科研与实践启示
需要进一步研究以明确PTBP1如何调控宿主 抗病毒药物反应,并探索其作为宿主导向的抗病毒药物药物靶点的潜力。这包括对其在先天免疫信号通路和应激颗粒生物学中的作用的详细研究,以及对其在登革病毒感染过程中与其他病毒和宿主因子相互作用的调查。

PTBP1 at the host-virus interface: mechanistic roles in viral RNA translation, replication, and immune modulation.

Authors: Kajal, Achyut Pandey, Rajesh Yadav, Rishikesh Shukla, Shruti Mishra
Original abstract (English)
Viruses require the involvement of host RNA binding proteins for completion of important steps of their life cycle. Polypyrimidine tract binding protein 1 (PTBP1) is an RNA-binding protein found ubiquitously which performs important regulatory functions like alternative splicing, RNA stability, RNA localization, and translation by virtue of its four RRMs and shuttling between nucleus and cytoplasm. There is increasing evidence showing that many viruses make use of such regulatory roles of PTBP1 to facilitate their gene expression and replication. This review describes the existing mechanistic knowledge about the PTBP1 functions during viral infection, paying attention to the role of PTBP1 in viral RNA translation, viral RNA genome replication, and regulation of host antiviral response. Special attention is paid to the regulation by PTBP1 of IRES-dependent translation of enteroviruses and hepatitis C virus, as well as to the PTBP1 contribution to RNA stabilization, long-distance RNA interactions, and genome cyclization of flaviviruses such as dengue virus and Japanese encephalitis virus. Recent data on the PTBP1 function in coronavirus RNA metabolism are discussed as well. Furthermore, the role of PTBP1 in being both proviral and antiviral is reviewed in terms of innate immunity signalling pathways, stress granule biology, and virus-host interaction. Finally, we will explore the possibility of PTBP1 being used as a host-directed antiviral drug target despite the hurdles in doing so considering its multifunctionality as an essential cellular RNA-binding protein.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on the role of PTBP1 in dengue virus infection, specifically its involvement in viral RNA translation, genome replication, and modulation of host antiviral responses. The central question is how PTBP1 contributes to these processes during dengue virus infection.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis of existing mechanistic knowledge about PTBP1 functions during viral infection. It does not report on specific databases, search dates, or meta-analysis methods. The evidence is derived from general descriptions of PTBP1's roles in viral RNA metabolism and host-virus interactions.
Consensus and conclusions
There is consensus that PTBP1 plays a critical role in the regulation of viral RNA translation, genome replication, and host antiviral responses. Specifically, PTBP1 contributes to RNA stabilization, long-distance RNA interactions, and genome cyclization in flaviviruses such as dengue virus and Japanese encephalitis virus. It also influences IRES-dependent translation in enteroviruses and hepatitis C virus.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights the dual role of PTBP1 as both proviral and antiviral, which introduces some controversy. Additionally, the review notes that the multifunctionality of PTBP1 presents challenges in targeting it as a host-directed antiviral drug. There is a lack of detailed mechanistic studies on how PTBP1 modulates host innate immunity signaling pathways and stress granule biology in the context of dengue virus.
Research and practice implications
Further research is needed to clarify the precise mechanisms by which PTBP1 modulates host antiviral responses and to explore its potential as a host-directed antiviral drug target. This includes detailed studies on its role in innate immunity signaling pathways and stress granule biology, as well as investigations into its interactions with other viral and host factors during dengue virus infection.

A 高优先级公共卫生新闻

世界卫生组织(WHO)"Shastho Chorcha"对话将政府与社会整体行动置于登革热应对的核心 - 世界卫生组织(WHO)

WHO “Shastho Chorcha” dialogue puts whole-of-government, whole-of-society action at the centre of dengue response - World Health Organization (WHO)
新闻简报世界卫生组织(WHO)于2026年9月9日宣布举办名为"Shastho Chorcha"的对话,强调在应对登革热时需要采取政府与社会整体行动。该举措旨在加强全球对抗登革热的共同努力,登革热是重要的公共卫生问题。此次对话是更广泛活动的一部分,包括对不丹和东帝汶登革热消除的验证。尽管没有报告具体病例或死亡人数,但该事件凸显了世卫组织持续致力于通过协作策略应对登革热的承诺。对话定于2026年9月16日举行,重点在于提升准备和实施能力。此官方声明强调了整合方法在抗击登革热中的重要性,符合世卫组织更广泛的卫生倡议。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该事件于2026年9月9日被报道,相关活动于2026年9月16日举行。
地点与对象
该事件与全球卫生倡议相关,特别提到了不丹和东帝汶作为参与卫生相关验证的国家。
事件
世卫组织举办名为"Shastho Chorcha"的对话,以强调在登革热应对中采取政府与社会整体行动。这是世界卫生组织的官方声明。
规模、影响与风险
此次对话聚焦于提升全球登革热应对策略,但证据中未报告具体病例数、死亡数或暴露人数。
应对、状态与不确定性
此次对话是世卫组织的官方举措,表明其持续努力应对登革热。未提供具体调查或检测细节。
A high priorityPublic-health news

WHO “Shastho Chorcha” dialogue puts whole-of-government, whole-of-society action at the centre of dengue response - World Health Organization (WHO)

News Brief
The World Health Organization (WHO) announced a dialogue titled 'Shastho Chorcha' on 9 September 2026, emphasizing the need for whole-of-government and whole-of-society action in responding to dengue. This initiative aims to strengthen global efforts against dengue, which is a significant public health concern. The dialogue was part of a broader set of activities, including validations of disease elimination in Bhutan and Timor-Leste. While no specific case or death numbers were reported, the event highlights WHO's ongoing commitment to addressing dengue through collaborative strategies. The dialogue was scheduled for 16 September 2026, with a focus on enhancing preparedness and implementation. This official statement underscores the importance of integrated approaches to combat dengue, aligning with WHO's broader health initiatives.
